Abstract: Existing evaluations of concept unlearning in text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models primarily rely on attack success rates obtained through automated adversarial prompt search. However, these metrics provide only empirical evidence over a finite set of queries and leave residual leakage over the broader prompt space largely unquantified. This limitation can lead to overestimating unlearning effectiveness and underestimating safety risks. To address this gap, we introduce a novel certification framework for T2I concept unlearning that provides high-confidence guarantees with bounded error on residual concept leakage. Our approach combines statistical certification with worst-case analysis along concept-relevant embedding directions to derive explicit upper bounds on leakage probability under user-specified confidence levels. We evaluate our framework across three major concept categories namely NSFW content, artistic styles, and celebrity identities, and six state-of-the-art unlearning methods. Certified leakage bounds consistently exceed standard attack success rates by 16.2%, uncovering substantial residual risks missed by existing evaluation protocols. Crucially, our results demonstrate that empirical attack-based evaluations can significantly underestimate residual leakage and establish certification as a necessary complement for reliable auditing of concept unlearning in T2I diffusion models.

</details>


### [71] [Estimating Pedestrian Volumes from GIS-Derived Built-Environment Features: A Machine Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12173)
*Bahareh Golchin,Banafsheh Rekabdar,Sirisha Kothuri,Joseph Broach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一套机器学习流程，利用开放GIS数据的建成环境、土地利用和街道网络特征，预测波特兰101个城市交叉口的2小时晚高峰行人流量，并通过多种交叉验证策略筛选配置，最优模型较负二项GLM基线显著降低预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通机构需要覆盖全网的行人流量估计以优化安全投资优先级，但人工计数成本高昂且仅覆盖少量交叉口，因此需借助可扩展的预测模型填补数据空白。

Method: 以实践中常用的负二项GLM为基线，引入特征选择、计数量感知的梯度提升与重复交叉验证；基于RMSE、MAPE、SMAPE的联合排名在四种交叉验证策略下选择单一配置；最优模型为采用Poisson损失与L1 Lasso特征选择的直方图梯度提升。

Result: 最优模型将交叉验证RMSE较GLM基线降低12%（89.8降至78.7），留出集RMSE降低19%（108.0降至87.9）；代码已在GitHub发布。

Conclusion: 计数量感知的梯度提升结合特征选择与重复交叉验证，可在开放GIS数据支持下有效提升城市交叉口行人流量预测精度，为全网行人流量估计提供可行替代方案。

Abstract: Transportation agencies need pedestrian volume estimates across entire road networks to prioritize safety investments, yet manual counts are expensive and cover only a small share of intersections. We present a machine learning pipeline that predicts 2-hour PM peak pedestrian volume at 101 urban intersections in Portland, Oregon, from built-environment, land-use, and street-network features drawn from open GIS data. Starting from the Negative Binomial GLM used in practice, we add feature selection, count-aware gradient boosting, and repeated cross-validation, selecting one configuration by a combined rank over RMSE, MAPE, and SMAPE across four cross-validation strategies. The winner, a histogram-based gradient boosting model with Poisson loss and L1 Lasso feature selection, reduces cross-validated RMSE by 12% over the GLM baseline (89.8 to 78.7) and holdout RMSE by 19% (108.0 to 87.9). Code is released on GitHub.

</details>


### [72] [Explanations-Driven Active Feature Acquisition for Algorithmic Recourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12179)
*Vinura Galwaduge,Jagath Samarabandu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将算法追索与主动特征获取联合建模，基于马尔可夫毯理论统一了反事实、半事实与替代事实解释，提出解释驱动的特征获取方法EDFA，在保持精度的同时显著减少特征获取数量并提供更可行动的追索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法追索方法假设模型可获得个体的全部特征，但现实中特征获取成本高、决策常基于部分信息；而主动特征获取（AFA）方法虽处理成本受限的预测问题，却与解释无关——只在获取特征之后才给出解释，未利用解释来指导特征获取。

Method: 以马尔可夫毯理论为统一框架，刻画反事实、半事实、替代事实三类解释，并描述可用追索空间随特征获取的增长方式；据此提出解释驱动的特征获取方法EDFA，按“单位成本解释价值”选择特征；进一步为基于部分信息给出的追索引入无分布有效性保证，并给出认证所需校准数据量的下界。

Result: 在7个公开数据集与基于神经网络的预测模型上，EDFA比当前最先进的AFA基线获取的特征数量显著更少，同时保持可比的预测精度，并产生更具决策相关性与可行动性的追索。

Conclusion: 将算法追索与特征获取统一处理是可行且有效的：解释可作为特征获取的驱动力，在降低成本的同时提升追索质量，并提供可信的部分信息追索保证；实现已开源。

Abstract: Algorithmic recourse methods typically assume that a predictive model has access to all features of an individual. In practice, decisions are often made with partial information, because features are costly to acquire. Active feature acquisition addresses cost-constrained prediction, but existing methods are explanation-agnostic: prior work provides explanations only after acquiring additional features, rather than using explanations to drive acquisition. This work flips that and treats algorithmic recourse and feature acquisition jointly. We use Markov Blanket theory to unify counterfactual, semifactual, and alterfactual explanations and to characterize how available recourse grows as features are acquired. Building on this framework, we propose an Explanation-Driven Feature Acquisition (EDFA) method that selects features by explanatory value per unit cost. The framework is further extended with distribution-free validity guarantees for recourse issued from partial information, which signal trustworthy, lower-cost recourse, along with a lower bound on the calibration data required to certify them. Experiments on 7 publicly available datasets with neural network-based predictive models show that EDFA acquires substantially fewer features than state-of-the-art AFA baselines while maintaining comparable accuracy and yielding more decision-relevant, actionable recourse. The implementation is available on GitHub.

</details>


### [73] [Predicting Collision Cross Sections with GRACE: Geometric Residual Adduct Conditioning via Early-fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12223)
*Parthasarathy Suryanarayanan,Susanta Das,Shreyans Sethi,Kenneth M. Merz,,Joseph A. Morrone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRACE：一种基于三维分子几何编码器与早期融合加合物条件化的碰撞截面积（CCS）预测模型，在多种数据划分与外部测试集上均取得最优精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CCS是离子迁移质谱用于分子注释的常用描述符，但预测困难，因为它同时反映气相分子离子的尺寸、形状与电离状态。现有预测器要么忽略显式三维结构，要么把加合物身份当作后期类别特征，难以捕捉加合物依赖的几何效应。

Method: 提出GRACE，通过早期融合引入几何残差加合物条件化，对预训练分子几何编码器进行适配。融合两种归纳偏置：（1）相对于加合物感知物理描述符基线的残差目标；（2）编码器内部的加合物条件化，通过学习到的加合物token与低秩注意力适配器实现。在9,000余条实验分子-加合物CCS记录上，采用随机、骨架与加合物敏感三种划分，分别考察插值、骨架泛化与加合物驱动泛化。

Result: 三种划分下GRACE均为所评估学习模型中平均百分比差异最低：随机划分1.67%，骨架划分2.11%，加合物敏感划分2.36%。诊断分析表明，残差学习通过去除主导的"质量-CCS"趋势稳定训练，早期融合相比后期融合提升加合物敏感预测。在四个独立外部测试集上误差持续低于其他模型；在与四种已报道物理工作流的对比中，于留出集上取得最低平均百分比差异。

Conclusion: 残差学习与编码器级加合物条件化是实用且有效的归纳偏置，可实现快速、准确的CCS预测。

Abstract: Collision cross section (CCS), derived from ion mobility mass spectrometry, is a common descriptor for molecular annotation. Prediction is challenging for machine learning models because it reflects the size, shape, and ionization state of a gas-phase molecular ion. Most predictors either ignore explicit 3D structure or treat adduct identity as a late categorical feature, which limits their ability to capture adduct-dependent geometric effects. We present GRACE (Geometric Residual Adduct Conditioning via Early-fusion), a 3D CCS predictor that adapts a pretrained molecular geometry encoder using geometric residual adduct conditioning via early fusion. GRACE combines two inductive biases: a residual objective relative to an adduct-aware physical descriptor baseline and adduct conditioning within the encoder via a learned adduct token and low-rank attention adapters. We evaluate the model on a curated set of over 9,000 experimental molecule-adduct CCS records with random, scaffold, and adduct-sensitive splits designed to separate interpolation, scaffold generalization, and adduct-driven generalization. GRACE achieves the best mean percentage difference among the evaluated learned models on all three splits: 1.67% on the random split, 2.11% on the scaffold split, and 2.36% on the adduct-sensitive split. Diagnostic analyses suggest that residual learning stabilizes training by removing the dominant mass-CCS trend, while early fusion improves adduct-sensitive prediction relative to late fusion. Across four independent external test sets, GRACE shows consistently lower error than the other evaluated models. On a held-out set, GRACE also attains the lowest mean percent difference when compared with four previously reported physics-based workflows. These results support residual learning and encoder-level adduct conditioning as practical inductive biases for fast, accurate CCS prediction.

</details>


### [74] [Patient-Reported Survey Data Improve Prediction of Opioid Use Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12224)
*Xiyue Jiang,Zihan Ding,Grace Han,Yinan Liu,Richard N. Rosenthal,Fusheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在267,747名有阿片类药物暴露记录的All of Us参与者中，将调查问卷数据与电子健康记录（EHR）结合，能稳定提升首诊阿片使用障碍（OUD）的预测性能，并揭示调查数据可用性的重要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录（EHRs）可能无法完整捕获与阿片使用障碍（OUD）相关的患者自报告因素，因此需要评估调查数据是否能补充EHR并改善OUD首诊预测。

Method: 基于All of Us队列中267,747名有阿片暴露记录者（含15,287例OUD），在6、12、24个月回看窗口下，比较仅EHR模型与EHR+调查模型；使用逻辑回归、随机森林、XGBoost、LightGBM、多层感知机、LSTM、GRU和Transformer，并以PR-AUC评估，辅以排列重要性分析。

Result: 调查数据增强在全部24个“模型-窗口”组合中均提升PR-AUC，幅度为0.0087–0.0505；最佳24个月LightGBM模型由0.6219提升至0.6603。调查覆盖率随窗口延长而提高，且因OUD状态而异（24个月：OUD阳性21.7% vs. 阴性60.7%）。排列分析显示，24个月时调查特征在两个评估模型中均为第二重要的信息域。

Conclusion: 患者自报告数据在结构化EHR之外提供了互补的预测信号，同时凸显调查数据可用性对OUD预测的重要影响。

Abstract: Electronic health records (EHRs) may incompletely capture patient-reported factors associated with opioid use disorder (OUD). We evaluated whether survey data improve prediction of a first recorded OUD diagnosis among 267,747 All of Us participants with documented opioid exposure, including 15,287 OUD cases. We compared EHR-only and EHR+survey models across 6-, 12-, and 24-month look-back windows using logistic regression, random forest, XGBoost, LightGBM, multilayer perceptron, LSTM, GRU, and Transformer. Survey augmentation improved PR-AUC across all 24 model-window combinations by 0.0087-0.0505; the best 24-month LightGBM model improved from 0.6219 to 0.6603. Survey coverage increased with longer windows and differed by OUD status (24 months: 21.7% OUD-positive vs. 60.7% OUD-negative). Permutation analysis ranked survey features as the second most important information domain at 24 months in both evaluated models. Patient-reported data provide complementary predictive signals beyond structured EHRs while highlighting the importance of survey availability.

</details>


### [75] [PLSP (Pre-hoc Liminal Space Profiling): OOD Prediction over Detection -- An Anticipatory Approach for Machine Learning Model Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12225)
*Vipul Bansal,Himanshu Buckchash,Balasubramanian Raman,Deepak Dhungana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出从OOD检测转向OOD预测，构建pre-hoc预期框架PLSP，并引入CREDS、可信度曲线与可信度热图来刻画模型在分布偏移下的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OOD数据会导致模型部署失败，但现有方法均为post-hoc，依赖准确率、AUC-ROC等推理期指标间接评估模型对OOD的响应，缺乏事前的预测能力。

Method: 提出pre-hoc预期框架PLSP，用于OOD预测；提出与数据集无关的CREDibility Score（CREDS）指标；引入可信度曲线研究模型可达到的最大可信度；引入可信度热图及曲面下体积刻画模型跨数据集的事前行为。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，所提指标是提升机器学习模型面向OOD预测鲁棒性的有价值的度量。

Conclusion: 该工作将OOD问题从检测转向预测，为分布偏移下的信号处理提供了新视角。

Abstract: Out-of-Distribution (OOD) data poses a significant threat to machine learning models, often leading to model failure during deployment. All existing OOD detection methods are post-hoc, relying on evaluation metrics such as accuracy and AUC-ROC during inference to indirectly assess the model's response to OOD data by measuring deviations. In contrast to existing approaches, the proposed work shifts the paradigm from OOD detection to OOD prediction by proposing a pre-hoc anticipatory framework called PLSP for OOD prediction. We make several key contributions: (a) a dataset-independent metric called the CREDibility Score (CREDS) is proposed for OOD prediction; (b) credibility curves are introduced to study the maximum credibility a model can attain; and (c) credibility heat maps (and volume under surface) are introduced to characterize pre-hoc model behavior across different datasets. This work provides a novel perspective on signal processing under distributional shifts. Experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate that the proposed metric serves as a valuable measure for improving the robustness of machine learning models toward OOD prediction.

</details>


### [76] [CRFCAN: A Complex-Valued Cross-Domain Residual Network for Joint Channel and Phase Noise Estimation in Sub-THz OFDM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12244)
*Ruilin Wang,Xiaodai Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CRFCAN——一种复值残差FFT卷积注意力网络，用于次太赫兹通信中信道与相位噪声的端到端联合估计，以单次固定复杂度推理实现并在NMSE和BER上超越传统算法与先进深度学习模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 次太赫兹通信中超宽带宽与严重相位噪声耦合，使传统联合信道与相位噪声估计复杂度极高、计算代价难以承受；现有深度学习方案多依赖级联网络或神经网络与传统迭代估计器结合的混合框架，并非真正的端到端方案。

Method: 提出CRFCAN复值残差FFT卷积注意力网络：将FFT与逆FFT模块嵌入残差组，实现时域与频域间的迭代特征交互；引入两个专用残差块，分别用于复特征提取与乘性相位失真建模；采用带软归一化的物理感知相位噪声输出尾部，以提升估计稳定性并保持有效相位噪声过程的物理特性。

Result: 仿真结果表明，CRFCAN在归一化均方误差（NMSE）和误码率（BER）方面显著优于传统算法及当前最优深度学习模型；其以单次、固定复杂度推理取得更优性能。

Conclusion: CRFCAN对未见过的相位噪声模型具有良好的泛化能力，无需微调即可适用，体现了其在次太赫兹接收机中的鲁棒性与实用性。

Abstract: In sub-terahertz (sub-THz) communications, the coupling of ultra-wide bandwidth and severe phase noise (PN) impairments renders conventional joint channel and PN estimation highly complex and computationally prohibitive. To address this, we propose CRFCAN, a complex-valued residual FFT convolutional attention network designed for joint channel and PN estimation. Unlike existing deep learning schemes that rely on cascaded networks or hybrid frameworks combining neural networks with conventional iterative estimators, CRFCAN performs joint recovery in a truly end-to-end fashion through a physics-inspired cross-domain structure. Specifically, Fast Fourier Transform (FFT) and inverse FFT modules are embedded within residual groups to enable iterative feature interaction across the time and frequency domains, thereby capturing both frequency-selective fading and time-varying phase distortions. In addition, two dedicated residual blocks are introduced for complex feature extraction and multiplicative phase-distortion modeling, respectively. A physics-aware PN output tail with soft normalization is further employed to improve estimation stability while preserving the physical characteristics of the effective PN process. Simulation results demonstrate that CRFCAN significantly outperforms conventional algorithms and state-of-the-art deep learning models in terms of normalized mean square error (NMSE) and bit error rate (BER). Notably, CRFCAN achieves superior performance with single-shot, fixed-complexity inference and generalizes well to unseen PN models without fine-tuning, highlighting its robustness and practicality for sub-THz receivers.

</details>


### [77] [The Rank the Task Demands: A Causal Rank Law for Matrix Memories Trained on Group Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12259)
*Samuel Larson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在单状态硬瓶颈（固定解码器）下训练群复合状态追踪任务，梯度下降招募的秩恰好等于任务代数所需的最小忠实实表示维数 $d_{\min}$（Spearman $\rho=0.9747$）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵值记忆的秩决定其可绑定的独立方向数，因此秩是学习表示的天然预算。本文旨在从标量容量界推进到表示论层面的"秩定律"，并给出因果证据说明梯度下降是否精确招募任务代数所需的秩。

Method: 继承 Larson(2026a) 的 K-pair 联想绑定测试床工具，扩展到群复合状态追踪。在单状态瓶颈+固定解码器（无法"洗白"秩）条件下，向嵌入大矩阵的选定最小忠实参考表示训练，覆盖五个有限群（含可解/不可解分界）。设计预注册的 force-rank 检验与锚点相对阈值（0.9）检验。

Result: 招募秩等于 $d_{\min}$（$\rho=0.9747$，达到设计的并列封顶最大值）。维数匹配的 $S_4$/$A_5$ 对在预注册检验下统计等价。force-rank 检验：比 $d_{\min}$ 低一秩时余弦相似度被目标并列单位谱封顶于 $\sqrt{(d_{\min}-1)/d_{\min}}\le0.894$（各组均低于 0.9），实测为封顶的 86–95%（均值 91%）；在 $d_{\min}$ 处（先验无保证）五组各四个种子均超过预注册锚点相对标准。

Conclusion: 在该测试床内，测得的有效秩紧密追踪表示维数，且维数匹配的 $S_4$/$A_5$ 比较在预注册容差内成立，为"秩即表示维数"的表示论秩定律提供了因果证据。

Abstract: Matrix-valued memories make rank the natural budget of a learned representation: the number of independent directions a state spans bounds what it can bind, compose, and track. We report causal evidence, on a group-composition testbed trained under a hard single-state bottleneck with a fixed decoder that cannot launder rank, that gradient descent recruits precisely the rank the task's algebra demands. A companion paper [Larson, 2026a] establishes the analogous recruitment and causal necessity pattern on a $K$-pair associative-binding testbed, where exact recovery provably requires state rank at least $K$; this paper inherits that instrument and extends the rank law from a scalar capacity bound to a representation-theoretic one. We train toward chosen minimal faithful reference representations embedded in larger matrices. On group-composition state tracking over five finite groups spanning the solvable/non-solvable divide, the recruited rank equals the group's minimal faithful real representation dimension $d_{\min}$ (Spearman $ρ= 0.9747$, the design's tie-capped maximum), the dimension-matched solvable/non-solvable pair $S_4$/$A_5$ is statistically equivalent under a pre-registered test, and a pre-registered force-rank test separates a guaranteed similarity ceiling from empirical recovery at the target dimension: one rank below $d_{\min}$, cosine similarity is capped by the target's tied unit spectrum at $\sqrt{(d_{\min}{-}1)/d_{\min}} \le 0.894$, below the $0.9$ threshold in every group by construction, with observed cells at 86-95% (mean 91%) of that ceiling; at $d_{\min}$, not guaranteed a priori, recovery clears the pre-registered anchor-relative bar at four seeds per group in all five groups. Within this testbed, measured effective rank tracks representation dimension; the matched-dimension $S_4$/$A_5$ comparison establishes equivalence within the pre-registered tolerance.

</details>


### [78] [Adaptive Chemotherapy Control under Tumor Heterogeneity via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12264)
*Bereket Sitotaw Kidane,Md Samiul Haque Motayed,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了基于高维异质性肿瘤模型训练的闭环深度强化学习（DRL）给药策略（连续动作TD3与离散动作DQN）与PMP开环最优控制基准，并在100例虚拟患者队列上评估泛化性，揭示疗效与一致性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肿瘤异质性与耐药性阻碍了患者特异性模型最优控制在多样化人群中的应用，亟需能适应状态变化、泛化性强的闭环给药策略。

Method: 构建高维异质性肿瘤模型，训练连续动作空间（TD3）与离散动作空间（DQN）的闭环DRL给药策略，并与基于庞特里亚金极大值原理（PMP）的开环最优控制基准进行对比；在含生长与药物敏感性参数±10%均匀扰动的100例虚拟患者队列上评估参数异质性下的泛化性能。

Result: 在整个虚拟队列中，TD3实现更高的平均肿瘤削减率，DQN则表现出更紧的患者间给药一致性，揭示了疗效与一致性之间的明显权衡。仿真假设所有肿瘤亚群完全可观测。

Conclusion: 仿真训练的DRL能够学习状态依赖的反馈给药策略，可作为开环最优控制基准的补充；但向稀疏、带噪临床测量的转化需要部分可观测建模和/或状态估计。

Abstract: Designing effective chemotherapy regimens is hindered by tumor heterogeneity and drug resistance, which complicate the deployment of patient-specific model-based optimal control across diverse populations. We develop and compare closed-loop deep reinforcement learning (DRL) dosing policies with continuous (TD3) and discrete (DQN) action spaces trained on a high-dimensional heterogeneous tumor model. The DRL policies are benchmarked against a Pontryagin's Maximum Principle (PMP)-derived open-loop benchmark. We assess generalization under parametric heterogeneity using a 100-patient virtual cohort with plus or minus 10 percent uniform perturbations in growth and drug-sensitivity parameters. Across this cohort, TD3 achieves higher average tumor reduction, while DQN yields tighter inter-patient dosing consistency, revealing a clear efficacy-consistency trade-off in this study. Our simulations assume full observation of all tumor subpopulations; translation to sparse and noisy clinical measurements will require partial-observability formulations and/or state estimation. Overall, the results show that simulation-trained DRL can learn state-dependent feedback dosing policies that complement open-loop optimal control benchmarks.

</details>


### [79] [Reinforcement Learning over Patient Trajectories for Clinical Reasoning in EHR Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12277)
*Yuxin Xiao,Sheng Zhang,Chandan Singh,Tristan Naumann,Hoifung Poon,Jianfeng Gao,Xiaodong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出将EHR基础模型视作患者轨迹上的生成式策略，用强化学习微调框架提升其临床推理能力，并设计了时间感知、轨迹敏感的奖励机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EHR基础模型虽在临床预测任务上表现优异，但其推理能力受限于有限的、不完整的EHR数据上的下一词预测范式，难以进行真正的临床推理。

Method: 将EHR基础模型建模为患者轨迹上的生成式策略，把常见临床预测问题（如再入院）形式化为事件条件化、时间窗口化的推理任务，并设计时间感知、对rollout敏感的奖励函数，以应对有限rollout长度和时间上不确定的结果。

Result: RL微调持续优于预训练骨干网络和强基线；在数据受限场景下使小模型超越更大的预训练模型，并产生跨任务的正迁移；微调后模型生成的轨迹在结构和语义上与真实轨迹更对齐，下游效用更高。

Conclusion: 将EHR基础模型作为生成式策略进行RL微调，可有效增强其临床推理与轨迹生成质量，是提升EHR基础模型实用性的可行方向。

Abstract: Electronic health record (EHR) foundation models trained on longitudinal patient trajectories have demonstrated strong performance across diverse clinical prediction tasks. However, their clinical reasoning capabilities remain constrained by next-token prediction on limited and incomplete EHR data. To address this, we propose a reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tuning framework that treats EHR foundation models as generative policies over patient trajectories. We formulate common clinical prediction problems (e.g., hospital readmission) as event-conditioned, time-windowed reasoning tasks. We then design time-aware, rollout-sensitive rewards to account for finite rollout lengths and temporally inconclusive outcomes. We find that RL fine-tuning consistently improves over pre-trained backbones and strong baselines. Notably, it enables smaller models to surpass larger pre-trained models in data-limited regimes and induces positive transfer across tasks. Further analysis shows that RL fine-tuned models generate trajectories with stronger structural and semantic alignment to ground truth and greater downstream utility.

</details>


### [80] [Amortized Low-Rank Adaptation for Model-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12278)
*Fernando Palafox,David Fridovich-Keil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CLAW，一种用超网络在测试时生成低秩（LoRA）适配器的方法，兼顾计算效率与表达力，实现对未知测试环境世界模型的快速适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型可通过预测动作后果帮助智能体规划，但环境变化会使其失准。现有测试时适应方法存在计算成本与表达力的权衡：上下文学习计算廉价但表达力受限，基于梯度的适应表达力强但计算昂贵。作者希望打破这一权衡。

Method: CLAW 使用超网络在测试时生成低秩（LoRA）适配器。预训练阶段，模拟对多种环境的适应，联合训练超网络与基础世界模型；测试阶段，冻结基础模型，仅用一小批测试时转移数据经超网络一次前向传播生成适配器。

Result: 在动力学、形态和奖励各异的运动与操作环境族中评估。仅用数秒测试时数据，CLAW 在在线适应中优于基于梯度的适应和上下文学习；在数据稀缺情况下避免过拟合；其优势来自表达力强的适配器而非上下文条件化；且预训练时联合训练超网络与基础模型优于事后单独训练超网络。

Conclusion: CLAW 通过超网络生成低秩适配器，在测试时以极低计算成本获得高表达力的世界模型适应能力，验证了联合预训练与 LoRA 适配器设计的有效性。

Abstract: World models let agents plan by predicting the consequences of their actions, but changes in the environment can make them inaccurate. We study the problem of adapting a world model to an unknown test-time environment, drawn from a known environment family, using only a few episodes of interaction. Existing approaches trade off computational cost against expressivity, i.e., the range of models a method can produce. For example, in-context learning is computationally cheap but limited in expressivity, and gradient-based adaptation is expressive but computationally expensive. We present CLAW (Context-conditioned Low-rank Adaptation of World models), which addresses this tradeoff by using a hypernetwork to generate low-rank (LoRA) adapters at test time. During pretraining, we simulate adaptation to a variety of environments and jointly train the hypernetwork and base world model. At test time, we freeze the base model and use a forward pass of the hypernetwork to generate adapters from a small batch of test-time transitions. We evaluate CLAW in locomotion and manipulation environment families that vary in dynamics, embodiment, and reward. We show that, using only seconds of test-time data, CLAW outperforms gradient-based adaptation and in-context learning during online adaptation. We also show that CLAW avoids overfitting in data-scarce regimes, that its advantage comes from the expressive adapters rather than context conditioning, and that pretraining the hypernetwork jointly with the base model outperforms training it post hoc.

</details>


### [81] [FRIST: FMRI Representation Informed Shared-space Training Improves EEG-only Individual-Finger BCI Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12298)
*Jintao Zhang,Yidan Ding,Joshua Kosnoff,Maxim Karrenbach,Hanwen Wang,Bin He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FRIST两阶段框架，利用fMRI空间表征信息提升EEG手指级运动解码性能，推理时仅需EEG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手指级运动解码对自然脑机接口控制很重要，但基于头皮脑电（EEG）的单指解码困难：手指在感觉运动皮层表征空间邻近，且受容积传导模糊。fMRI具有高空间分辨率，可提供互补的空间约束。

Method: 提出FRIST（fMRI Representation-Informed Shared-Space Training）两阶段EEG解码框架：第一阶段从同步EEG-fMRI记录中学习fMRI-informed的频谱投影；第二阶段利用fMRI导出的类别几何结构指导EEG预测的残差精修。跨记录通过共享手指标签传递信息，无需配对试次，推理时仅用EEG。在12名健康被试上评估运动执行（ME）与运动想象（MI），采用二类和三类的时序session留出解码模拟在线场景；以EEGNet为特征提取器，并验证多主干泛化性。

Result: 以EEGNet为特征提取器，FRIST较仅EEG基线显著提升：二类ME由66.93%升至74.53%，三类ME由44.83%升至56.58%，二类MI由80.78%升至85.63%，三类MI由60.93%升至69.90%。当目标被试自身fMRI数据不可用时，FRIST仍能提升仅EEG解码。以EEG Conformer为特征提取器时，二类MI达87.40%，三类MI达72.54%，显示跨主干泛化性。

Conclusion: fMRI可为EEG表征学习提供有效空间约束。FRIST提升了非侵入式EEG手指级BCI解码性能，为融合fMRI空间特异性与EEG实时适用性提供了多模态策略。

Abstract: Finger-level motor decoding is important for naturalistic brain-computer interface (BCI) control, yet individual-finger decoding from scalp electroencephalography (EEG) remains challenging because finger representations are spatially close in the sensorimotor cortex and blurred by volume conduction. Leveraging the high spatial resolution of functional MRI (fMRI), we introduce fMRI Representation-Informed Shared-Space Training (FRIST), a two-stage EEG decoding framework that first learns fMRI-informed spectral projections from simultaneous EEG-fMRI recordings and then uses fMRI-derived class geometry to guide residual refinement of EEG predictions. FRIST transfers information across recordings through shared finger labels without requiring paired trials and uses only EEG at inference. We evaluated 12 able-bodied participants during movement execution (ME) and motor imagery (MI) under two-class and three-class chronological session-held-out decoding simulating the online scenario. Using EEGNet as the EEG feature extractor, FRIST increased group average accuracy from 66.93% to 74.53% for two-class ME, from 44.83% to 56.58% for three-class ME, from 80.78% to 85.63% for two-class MI, and from 60.93% to 69.90% for three-class MI compared with the EEG-only EEGNet baseline. FRIST is also shown to improve EEG-only decoding when the target participant's own fMRI data were unavailable. FRIST also generalized across multiple EEG decoding backbones, reaching 87.40% in two-class MI and 72.54% in three-class MI with EEG Conformer as the EEG feature extractor. These findings indicate that fMRI provide useful spatial constraints for EEG representation learning. FRIST improves noninvasive EEG-based finger-level BCI decoding, offering a multimodal strategy for integrating the spatial specificity of fMRI with real-time applicability of EEG.

</details>


### [82] [Sampling via Decision-Flow: Training-Free Extraction of Improved Latent Reasoning Paths in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12317)
*Zhendong Mi,Shaoyi Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出免训练、免数据的推理时框架 Decision-Flow Sampling (DF-Sample)，通过构建层次化推理树、评估终端节点质量并回传效用引导分支决策，挖掘基座模型中已有的潜在高奖励推理路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是RL是否赋予LLM真正的新能力，还是仅重塑已有知识的表达。基于"分布锐化"假说（RL将概率质量重新分配到基座模型中已潜在存在的高奖励轨迹），本文追问：能否不经过昂贵的RL微调即解锁这些潜在路径。

Method: DF-Sample 是一个免训练、免数据的推理时框架：构建层次化推理树，对终端节点进行质量打分，并将效用回传（back-propagate）以指导每个中间分支决策。与传统仅做局部逐步选择的采样策略不同，它在确定路径前进行显式的全局轨迹评估，从而恢复标准解码所忽略的高质量但低概率的推理链。

Result: 在 GPQA 上达到 45.6% 准确率，超过 power sampling (38.9%) 与 GRPO (39.9%)，表明免训练方法可超越经过训练的方法；在三个模型与四个基准上持续优于基线。

Conclusion: 结果指示预训练基座模型中存在大量潜在推理能力，免训练推理时方法即可有效释放。

Abstract: A central question in LLM reasoning is whether reinforcement learning (RL) instills genuinely new capabilities or merely reshapes how existing knowledge is expressed during inference. Building on the distribution-sharpening hypothesis, which holds that RL reallocates probability mass toward high-reward trajectories already latent in base models, we ask: can we unlock those latent paths without costly RL fine-tuning? We present Decision-Flow Sampling (DF-Sample), a training-free, data-free inference-time framework that constructs a hierarchical reasoning tree, scores terminal nodes for quality, and back-propagates utilities to inform each intermediate branching decision. Unlike conventional sampling strategies that make purely local step-wise choices, DF-Sample performs explicit global trajectory evaluation before committing to a path, recovering high-quality but low-probability reasoning chains that standard decoding overlooks. On GPQA, DF-Sample achieves 45.6% accuracy, surpassing power sampling (38.9%) and GRPO (39.9%), showing that a training-free method can outperform a trained one. Across three models and four benchmarks, DF-Sample consistently outperforms baselines, indicating substantial latent reasoning potential in pretrained base models.

</details>


### [83] [Simulating Disengaged Students to Evaluate LLM-based Tutors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12331)
*Xianghui Meng,Jionghao Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DAS2——一个可复现的部署前协议，用于建模五种学习投入状态并评估AI导师在不同状态下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算模型生成的模拟学生是评估人机导师教学策略的实用手段，但需考虑走捷径（gaming the system）、原地打转（wheel-spinning）和离题（off-task）等脱离行为，因为导师对不同学习状态需采取不同应对。

Method: DAS2建模 engaged、gaming、wheel-spinning、off-task 和 mixed 五种学习投入状态。使用ASSISTments09数据，两名编码者独立依据匿名交互日志摘要标注100个辅导会话；并将模拟条件化于目标学习状态，比较模拟与真实会话差异。采用微调Qwen2.5-7B与仅提示的GPT-4o生成模拟学生，并评估Claude、Llama、Gemini、Qwen、GPT五个家族的AI导师。

Result: 编码者一致性84%（kappa=0.78）；一致案例中人类共识标签与DAS2规则标签吻合81%（kappa=0.75）。条件化模拟将模拟与真实会话的正确率差距：gaming由0.54降至0.20，wheel-spinning由0.51降至0.18。微调Qwen2.5-7B更贴合真实响应时间分布，仅提示GPT-4o生成的 learner states 更易区分。五类AI导师相对排名跨状态与交互长度保持稳定，但绝对表现有差异，揭示状态特异的支持差异；人工验证显示自动评估与人类判断并不完全一致。

Conclusion: DAS2为部署前评估AI导师如何应对多样化学习投入状态提供了框架。

Abstract: Simulated students generated by computational models provide a practical way to evaluate tutoring strategies and pedagogical approaches used by human and AI tutors. However, such simulations should account for disengaged behaviors, including gaming the system, wheel-spinning, and off-task behavior, because tutors may need different responses for different learner states. We present Disengagement-Aware Student Simulators (DAS2), a reproducible pre-deployment protocol that models five learner-engagement states: engaged, gaming, wheel-spinning, off-task, and mixed, and evaluates AI tutor performance across these states. Using ASSISTments09, two coders independently labeled 100 sampled tutoring sessions based on anonymized interaction-log summaries. They achieved 84% agreement (Cohen's kappa = 0.78), and among agreed cases, human consensus labels matched DAS2 rule-based labels in 81% of cases (kappa = 0.75). Conditioning simulations on intended learner states reduced the correctness-rate gap between simulated and authentic sessions from 0.54 to 0.20 for gaming and from 0.51 to 0.18 for wheel-spinning. Fine-tuned Qwen2.5-7B better matched authentic response-time distributions, while prompt-only GPT-4o generated more distinguishable learner states. Evaluation of five AI tutors from the Claude, Llama, Gemini, Qwen, and GPT families showed that relative rankings remained stable across learner states and interaction lengths, while absolute performance varied, revealing state-specific differences in tutor support. Human validation further showed that automated tutor evaluation does not fully align with human judgment. DAS2 provides a pre-deployment framework for evaluating how AI tutors respond to diverse learner-engagement states before deployment.

</details>


### [84] [Theoretical Guarantees for One-Shot Magnitude Pruning and Compute-Adaptive Early Exit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12337)
*Erdem Koyuncu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 从"部分计算 vs 完整计算"的统一视角研究神经网络的计算量压缩，涵盖静态的一次性幅度剪枝与自适应的早退机制，并在单神经元与深层网络上给出理论刻画及数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络计算量压缩中存在静态剪枝与动态早退两类不同范式，缺乏统一的理论视角来刻画"部分计算替代完整计算"所带来的精度损失与计算量节省之间的关系。

Method: 在渐近单神经元模型中，证明一次性幅度剪枝的集中定理并给出显式收敛速率；提出用于早退的"条件感知机"模型；进一步将分析推广到深层网络，刻画剪枝导致的失真随深度的累积，并在神经网络高斯过程模型下推导冻结主干早退的计算-精度权衡。

Result: 一次性幅度剪枝在单神经元模型中具有显式速率的集中现象；条件感知机的超额泛化误差随计算量差距呈幂律衰减，且当部分计算与完整计算的对齐程度趋近于1时，该幂指数趋于无穷；深层网络中剪枝失真随深度累积，冻结主干早退呈现相应的计算-精度权衡；数值模拟验证了所预测的标度律。

Conclusion: 通过统一的部分-完整计算视角，可对剪枝与早退两类计算压缩方法建立一致的理论框架，所推出的标度律与权衡关系得到数值模拟支持。

Abstract: We study compute reduction in neural networks through a unified partial versus full computation view, captured by one-shot magnitude pruning in the static regime and early exit in the adaptive regime. In an asymptotic single-neuron model, we prove a concentration theorem for one-shot magnitude pruning with explicit rates. We also introduce the conditional perceptron for early exit and show that its excess generalization error decays as a power of the compute gap, with an exponent that grows to infinity as the alignment between partial and full computations tends to one. We then extend the analysis to deep networks, characterizing how pruning-induced distortions accumulate with depth and deriving a corresponding compute-accuracy tradeoff for frozen-backbone early exit under a neural network Gaussian process model. Numerical simulations corroborate the predicted scaling laws.

</details>


### [85] [ParaRecover: A Process-Level Benchmark for Error Localization and Recovery in Parallel Tool-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12345)
*Bowen Guan,Zhentao Yin,Yanming Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ParaRecover过程级基准，评估多轮并行工具调用智能体的中间错误定位与恢复能力，含14类错误分类、10626个实例及SDE评价量规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准主要评估最终任务成功或工具调用正确性，难以揭示智能体能否可靠地诊断并从中间执行失败中恢复；在多轮并行工具使用场景中，错误会跨依赖分支传播并引发级联失败，该问题尤为关键。

Method: 构建基于14种错误类型（覆盖规划依赖、工具选择、参数匹配）细粒度分类的ParaRecover基准，包含两个难度级别的10626个实例；并提出SDE量规，从结构完整性、诊断推理、演化策略三个维度进行面向过程的细粒度评估。

Result: 在十余个主流大语言模型上的实验表明，即使最先进模型在多轮错误传播、隐式工具使用失败和精确重规划方面仍表现不佳；SDE量规还能为提升智能体反思性恢复能力提供有效的监督信号。

Conclusion: ParaRecover将智能体评估从结果导向推进到过程导向，为多轮并行工具使用中的错误定位、恢复诊断与反思能力提升提供了细粒度基准与监督手段，数据和代码已开源。

Abstract: Existing agent benchmarks mainly evaluate final task success or tool-call correctness, providing limited insight into whether agents can reliably diagnose and recover from intermediate execution failures. This limitation becomes particularly critical in multi-turn parallel tool-use scenarios, where errors may propagate across dependent branches and trigger cascading failures. We introduce ParaRecover, a process-level benchmark for evaluating error localization and recovery in multi-turn parallel tool-use agents. Built upon a fine-grained taxonomy of 14 error types covering planning dependencies, tool selection, and argument matching, the benchmark comprises 10,626 instances spanning two difficulty levels. To enable finegrained, process-oriented evaluation, we further propose the SDE rubric, which measures structural integrity, diagnostic reasoning, and evolutionary strategy during agent execution.Experiments across more than ten mainstream LLMs reveal that even state-of-the-art models still struggle with multi-turn error propagation,implicit tool-use failures, and precise replanning. Moreover, we demonstrate that the SDE rubric provides effective supervision signals for improving agents' reflective recovery capabilities. Our data and code are available at https://github.com/gbw206/ParaRecover.

</details>


### [86] [When Connected Does Not Mean Similar: Charting the Homophily Boundary of SNAP-KG for Streaming Entity Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12356)
*Jui-Chien Lin,Oshani Seneviratne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文评估SNAP-KG在多视图知识图谱语义社区分配中的表现，发现其依赖同质性假设：当缺乏同质视图时，聚类质量急剧下降，该假设是整个方法族共有的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNAP-KG作为仅依赖原始特征、无需图访问和推理时重训练的知识图谱社区分配框架，其原始评估始终包含至少一个同质图视图。作者希望探究在该同质性设定之外（即异质图）方法是否仍然有效，以界定其适用边界。

Method: 将评估扩展到三个异质图（Texas、Wisconsin、Chameleon），测量每个视图的边同质性，并与原评估中的直推式基线进行对比，分析同质性来源（关系而非关系数量）以及多视图融合的作用条件。

Result: 当没有任何同质视图时，SNAP-KG与直推式基线聚类质量均急剧下降；决定性能的是关系的同质性而非关系数量；多视图融合仅在至少有一个同质关系提供可靠基础时才有帮助。

Conclusion: 同质性假设为整个方法族所共有，而非SNAP-KG特有；异质多视图聚类应被视为独立研究问题，超出本工作范围，并展望将异质性感知教师蒸馏到SNAP-KG投影器中以同时服务同质与异质知识图谱。

Abstract: SNAP-KG is a framework for assigning newly arriving entities to semantic communities in a growing knowledge graph (KG) using only their raw features, with no graph access and no retraining at inference time. It was evaluated on five multi-view benchmarks and a 2.4M-node OGB-WikiKG2 KG. In each of these datasets, at least one graph view is homophilous, meaning that connected nodes usually belong to the same class, and SNAP-KG performs well on all of them. This paper asks what happens outside that setting. We extend the evaluation to three heterophilous graphs (Texas, Wisconsin, Chameleon) and measure the edge homophily of every view. When no homophilous view is available, clustering quality drops sharply for both SNAP-KG and the transductive baselines used in its original evaluation. What decides this is the homophily of the relation, not the number of relations. Multi-view fusion still helps, but only when at least one homophilous relation provides a reliable foundation. The homophily assumption is therefore shared by the whole method family, not specific to SNAP-KG. We argue that heterophilous multi-view clustering is a separate research problem, outside the scope of this work. As future work, we outline how a heterophily-aware teacher could be distilled into SNAP-KG's projector to serve both homophilous and heterophilous KGs.

</details>


### [87] [LatentVerse: A Framework for Understanding Shared and Modality-Specific Information in Multimodal Latent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12364)
*Majd Alafrange,Samuel Friedman,John Kitonyo,Sana Tonekaboni,Mahnaz Maddah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出LatentVerse，一个用于表征分析的资源工具，结合Web可视化分析平台与命令行接口，统一各类表征质量评估指标并支持多模态分析，通过仿真、真实生物医学嵌入分析及用户研究验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在嵌入已成为现代机器学习的核心数据抽象，尤其在生物医学领域，基础模型广泛用于编码临床文本、医学图像、组学与生理信号等多模态数据。然而，这些表征的价值取决于对其质量、结构和所编码信息的理解。现有表征评估工作流碎片化，分散于自定义脚本和孤立指标之间，且缺乏多模态分析能力，限制了可访问性与可复现性。

Method: 提出LatentVerse，一个表征分析资源。其包含两大部分：面向可访问、报告驱动探索的Web可视化分析平台，以及面向可扩展技术工作流的命令行接口。该工具统一了多种表征质量指标的诊断方法，并通过将嵌入分解为共享成分与模态特有成分来扩展到多模态场景。

Result: 通过受控的单模态与多模态仿真实验、针对真实生物医学嵌入的探索性分析，以及覆盖多种用例的用户研究，对LatentVerse进行了评估。

Conclusion: LatentVerse支持对潜在空间进行全面且可解释的评估，使基础模型表征在生物医学与数据科学应用中更易被理解。

Abstract: Latent embeddings have become a central data abstraction in modern machine learning, especially in biomedicine, where foundation models are increasingly used to encode multimodal data like clinical text, medical images, omics, and physiological signals. However, the utility and value of these representations depends on understanding their quality, structure, and the information they encode. Existing analysis workflows for evaluating representations remain fragmented across custom scripts, isolated metrics, and most importantly lack multimodal analysis, limiting accessibility and reproducibility. We present LatentVerse, a representation analysis resource that combines a web-based visual analytics platform for accessible, report-driven exploration with a command-line interface for scalable technical workflows. LatentVerse unifies diagnostics for various representation quality metrics and extends to multimodal settings by decomposing embeddings into shared and modality-specific components. We evaluate LatentVerse through controlled unimodal and multimodal simulations, discovery-oriented analyses on real biomedical embeddings, and a user study across diverse use cases. By supporting thorough and interpretable evaluation of latent spaces, LatentVerse makes foundation model representations more understandable in biomedical and data science applications.

</details>


### [88] [Certified AI Triage of ICU Alarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12365)
*Mohammed Sameer Syed,Rozhin Yasaei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将室性心动过速（VT）警报缩减重构为"保留/抑制/延迟"三路分诊，并用置信上界约束使被抑制警报中的真阳性比例在给定预算内（95%置信度）。在VT aC基准上，5%预算可实现抑制74.8%假警报、仅静音1.5%真警报（AUROC 0.953，Challenge Score 83.33），同时揭示多重性校正的代价：更细的策略网格反而能认证更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VT aC基准中71%的VT警报为假，但静音真警报会延误危险心律失常的识别。现有警报缩减方法关注分类准确率，却未对"被抑制警报中混入真警报"这一真正有害的决策提供统计保证，因此需要一种带风险约束的分诊框架。

Method: 将警报缩减建模为三路分诊（保留、抑制、延迟）；以95%置信度约束被抑制警报中真警报比例低于用户设定预算，主分析假设事件i.i.d.抽样，考虑同一波形记录内警报的相关性，故以聚类分析作为敏感性检验；在官方划分上跨三个随机种子验证；进一步分析多重性校正对候选网格认证的影响，并比较自主声明网格与在独立选择分区上选网格两种策略；估计各预算所需的校准量；在策略网格中加入学习型可靠性维度。

Result: 官方划分上，5%预算在三个种子下均获认证，抑制74.8%假警报、静音1.5%真警报，AUROC 0.953，Challenge Score 83.33，与11个已发表系统中最强者数值相当。多重性代价显著：自主声明的885格网格在15次折运行中仅认证1次，而在独立选择分区上选网格可认证8次。加入学习型可靠性维度并未锐化认证前沿。

Conclusion: 带置信保证的三路分诊可在严格控制有害静音风险的同时大幅抑制假警报。核心洞见是多重性校正按每个候选收费，更细网格未必带来更强的认证结果；因此所需校准量应作为设计参数，使"不可认证的预算"成为可控设计决策，而学习型可靠性维度无助于改进认证前沿。

Abstract: In the VTaC benchmark 71% of ventricular-tachycardia alarms are false, but silencing a real one can delay recognition of a dangerous arrhythmia. We reframe alarm reduction as three-way triage (retain, suppress, or defer) and bound the decision this analysis treats as harmful: among suppressed alarms, the fraction that were genuine stays below a user-set budget with 95% confidence, under i.i.d. event sampling. Alarms sharing a waveform record are dependent, so the clustered analysis is a sensitivity check. On the official split a 5% budget certifies in all three seeds, suppressing 74.8% of false alarms while silencing 1.5% of genuine ones, at AUROC 0.953 and Challenge Score 83.33, numerically comparable to the strongest of the eleven published systems. Our central finding measures what multiplicity costs: the correction charges for every candidate, so a finer grid can certify strictly less. Under held-out calibration the 885-cell grid we declared certifies 1 of 15 fold-runs, while choosing the grid on a separate selection partition certifies 8. We project the calibration volume each budget needs, making an uncertifiable budget a design parameter. Finally, adding a learned reliability dimension to the policy grid did not sharpen the certified frontier.

</details>


### [89] [Split Conformal Prediction with Label-Shift-Adjusted Bayesian Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12386)
*Hyeonsu Lee,Juyeon Kim,Erkhembayar Jadamba,Seungjin Choi,Hyunjin Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出标签偏移调整贝叶斯分数（LSA score），通过对后验预测进行重要性加权倾斜校正，在标签偏移下生成更短且覆盖可比的区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测依赖可交换性假设，而标签偏移（标签边缘分布变化、条件分布不变）会破坏该假设，使标准共形过程失去目标覆盖保证。现有重要性加权方法多配合基于残差的非共形分数，忽略预测不确定性，导致区间宽度均匀；贝叶斯共形方法虽产生自适应区间，但在源域预测分布下评估一致性，与标签偏移下的目标域不匹配。

Method: 从后验预测倾斜恒等式出发推导LSA score。该恒等式表明目标预测分布是源预测分布的重要性加权变换，据此对贝叶斯分数做直接校正。以贝叶斯岭回归为实例，校正项具有闭式解；方法适用于任意具有可处理对数密度的源预测分布。

Result: 在受控标签偏移的分子性质预测任务上，LSA score 持续产生比基于残差和基于源域的贝叶斯分数更短的区间，同时目标域覆盖率与之相当。偏移更强时，由于基于伪标签的密度比估计，所有方法均出现一定覆盖损失。

Conclusion: LSA score 通过倾斜恒等式实现了对贝叶斯非共形分数的目标域直接校正，兼顾区间自适应性与目标覆盖，且定义通用、实例化简洁，是标签偏移下共形预测的有效方案；其局限在于依赖伪标签密度比估计，强偏移下覆盖会下降。

Abstract: Conformal prediction provides distribution-free uncertainty quantification under exchangeability. However, this assumption is violated by label shift, where the marginal distribution of labels changes while the conditional distribution of inputs given labels remains stable. Under such shifts, standard conformal procedures no longer maintain their intended coverage behavior. Existing approaches address this via importance weighting. They pair the reweighting with residual-based nonconformity scores that ignore predictive uncertainty. The resulting intervals have uniform width. Bayesian conformal methods produce adaptive intervals by leveraging predictive distributions. They evaluate conformity under the source predictive, which is misaligned with the target domain under label shift. We propose the \emph{Label-Shift-Adjusted Bayesian Score} (LSA score), a nonconformity score derived from a posterior predictive tilting identity. This identity shows that the target predictive is an importance-weighted transformation of the source predictive. We use it to derive a direct correction to the Bayesian score. We evaluate the method on molecular property prediction under controlled label shift. The LSA score consistently yields shorter intervals than residual-based and source-based Bayesian scores. Coverage in the target domain remains comparable. Under stronger shift, all methods incur some coverage loss due to pseudo-label-based density-ratio estimation. The LSA score is defined for any source predictive with a tractable log-density. We instantiate it with Bayesian Ridge Regression, where the correction admits a closed form.

</details>


### [90] [RiPPLE: Cross-Space Performance Prediction from Early Training for Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12418)
*Yifan Yang,Zhaoyan Wang,Zheng Gao,Xiaoyu Li,Jiaojiao Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RiPPLE 将部分训练视为锚点标签来源，通过前缀训练、学习曲线外推与标签传播实现免完全训练的全空间架构排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络架构搜索需评估大量候选网络，但完全训练整个空间代价高昂；零成本代理的排序质量在不同搜索空间波动大，而学习型预测器通常依赖完全训练标签或逐候选的部分训练特征。因此需要一种低成本、跨空间稳定且无需逐候选训练的排序方法。

Method: 提出 RiPPLE（Ranking via Prefix-Propagated Label Extrapolation）：选取小规模覆盖集作为锚点，仅训练到早期前缀，将其学习曲线外推为代理标签，再基于无标签架构特征把标签传播到整个空间。早期训练信号作为锚点标签而非逐候选特征；特征、读出与编码规则不依赖留存精度即可选定，并可跨搜索空间复用。

Result: 在四个搜索空间家族的十二个基准单元及更大的 DARTS 空间上评估，考察了排序质量、标签效率、架构选择以及读出、覆盖与传播的作用。结果显示，RiPPLE 能以分数级锚点训练预算提供全空间排序；相关比较在其各自的评估与成本协议下进行解释。

Conclusion: RiPPLE 通过前缀传播标签外推，避免了完全训练与逐候选特征提取的高成本，实现了低成本、可跨搜索空间复用的全空间架构排序，为 NAS 评估提供了一种高效替代方案。

Abstract: Neural architecture search (NAS) evaluates candidate networks, but fully training enough architectures to rank an entire space is expensive. Zero-cost proxies score architectures at initialization, yet their ranking quality varies across search spaces. Learned predictors reduce evaluation cost but typically require fully trained labels or partial-training features for individual candidates. We introduce $\textbf{RiPPLE}$, $\underline{\textbf{R}}$anking v$\underline{\textbf{i}}$a $\underline{\textbf{P}}$refix-$\underline{\textbf{P}}$ropagated $\underline{\textbf{L}}$abel $\underline{\textbf{E}}$xtrapolation, which treats partial training as a source of labels for a small coverage set of anchors. RiPPLE trains these anchors to an early prefix, extrapolates their learning curves to surrogate labels, and propagates the labels over label-free architecture features. The early-training signal remains a label on the anchors rather than a per-candidate feature. Feature, readout, and encoding rules are selected without held-out accuracy and reused across search spaces. We evaluate the method on twelve benchmark cells from four search-space families and on the larger DARTS space. The results examine ranking quality, label efficiency, architecture selection, and the roles of readout, coverage, and propagation. RiPPLE provides a whole-space ranking from a fractional anchor-training budget, with comparisons interpreted under their respective evaluation and cost protocols.

</details>


### [91] [MInTRL: Off-policy Intervention can boost On-policy RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12419)
*Mingyu Chen,Yefan Tao,Gerald Friedland,Xuezhou Zhang,Chris Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MInTRL，通过稀疏局部干预扩展on-policy探索前沿，配合序列级优势回归目标，在数学与代码任务上超越on-policy与off-policy基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可验证奖励的强化学习通常采用on-policy，数据贴近当前策略但受限于策略自身可发现的轨迹；off-policy方法（如SFT）能利用外部知识却存在较大分布偏移。核心挑战是在不牺牲可学习性的前提下扩展探索。

Method: MInTRL：生成阶段由裁判-干预策略周期性审查当前策略输出，将错误后缀替换为简短修正后立即交还控制权；训练阶段采用序列级优势回归目标，避免重要性采样。

Result: 在数学与代码基准上持续优于标准on-policy和off-policy基线；消融显示自干预与不同裁判策略下均有效，且性能在中等干预强度达到峰值。

Conclusion: 稀疏局部干预可在保持轨迹on-policy性质的同时显著提升覆盖，最小干预是增强on-policy强化学习的有效范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards is typically performed on-policy, keeping training data close to the current policy but limiting learning to trajectories that the policy can discover itself. Off-policy methods such as supervised fine-tuning, on the other hand, can leverage external knowledge beyond the base model's capabilities, but may suffer from large distribution shift. The key challenge is thus to expand exploration without sacrificing learnability. In this work, we introduce Minimal Intervention Reinforcement Learning (MInTRL), which expands the exploration frontier through sparse, local interventions in otherwise on-policy rollouts. During generation, a judge-intervention policy periodically reviews the current policy's output, replaces erroneous suffixes with short corrections, and immediately returns control to the policy. During training, MInTRL adopts a sequence-level advantage-regression objective that eliminates the need for importance sampling. We show that sparse, local interventions can substantially improve coverage beyond finite-budget on-policy sampling while preserving the overall on-policy nature of the resulting trajectories. Across math and code benchmarks, MInTRL consistently outperforms standard on-policy and off-policy baselines. Ablations show that MInTRL remains effective with self-intervention and across different judge policies, while performance peaks at moderate intervention intensity, highlighting the importance of intervening minimally. These results establish minimal intervention as an effective paradigm for enhancing on-policy RL.

</details>


### [92] [Granularity-Adaptive Credit Assignment for Long-Horizon LLM Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12424)
*Taoran Liang,Yang Liu,Shang Luo,Yingguang Yang,Rongrong Zhang,Yingzong Min,Yulin Huang,Jianshen Zhang,Yongzhi Qi,Kefu Xu,Congjing Ran,Bin Chong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出GACA，一种无评论家（critic-free）的强化学习优势估计器，其信号粒度随状态不确定性自适应变化，在ALFWorld与WebShop上超越GRPO和GiGPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域任务中奖励稀疏，GRPO等组相对方法只把轨迹级标量广播到每步，无法指出哪一步决策决定结果；GiGPO虽通过锚定状态分组恢复步级信号，却以固定权重融合步级与回合级估计，对关键分支决策与近乎确定性的常规转移投入相同分辨率。作者主张正确的分辨率应是状态依赖的。

Method: 提出GACA：以每步自身rollout已记录的负对数似然（NLL）作为不确定性关键性代理进行打分，并用随该分数增长的逐步权重融合步级与回合级优势——NLL高于均值时加大细粒度信号权重，低于均值时偏向回合级信号。理论上给出所实现混合的精确风险分解，证明在正向方向对齐下足够小的调制优于固定混合；并用条件性结果以期望NLL界定局部动作价值变化，用误差投影分析刻画混合何时比标量不确定性重加权更有价值。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop上，GACA在1.5B与7B两种规模下均提升了任务成功率，优于GRPO与GiGPO。

Conclusion: GACA表明优势估计的信号粒度应依据状态不确定性自适应调整，通过基于NLL的逐步权重调制兼顾关键步骤的细粒度信号与常规步骤的回合级信号，并具备理论保障与实证收益。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is now the standard way to train large language model agents on long-horizon tasks, where dozens of interdependent actions precede a single sparse reward. Critic-free, group-relative methods such as GRPO suit this regime, but they broadcast one trajectory-level scalar to every step and cannot say which decision drove the outcome. GiGPO recovers a step-level signal by grouping time steps that share an anchor state, yet it merges the step- and episode-level estimates under one fixed weight, spending the same resolution on a pivotal branching decision as on a routine, near-deterministic transition. We argue that the right resolution is state-dependent, and propose GACA, a critic-free estimator whose granularity follows an uncertainty-based criticality proxy. GACA scores every step by the negative log-likelihood its own rollout already records, then blends the two advantages with a per-step weight that grows with that score, so the gradient places more weight on the fine-grained signal at above-average NLL and on the episode-level signal below it. We derive an exact risk decomposition for the implemented mixture and show that sufficiently small modulation improves on fixed mixing under positive directional alignment. A separate conditional result bounds local action-value variation using expected NLL, while an error-projection analysis characterizes when mixing adds value beyond scalar uncertainty reweighting. On ALFWorld and WebShop, GACA improves task success over GRPO and GiGPO at both 1.5B and 7B scales.

</details>


### [93] [Observation-Anchored Selective Assimilation for Longitudinal Tumor-State Proxy Forecasting in Post-Treatment Glioma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12435)
*Yeonjae Jung,Minwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将胶质瘤治疗后MRI的纵向随访建模为"观测锚定的数字孪生更新"问题，提出OASA方法，用SegMamba单步预测器生成更新提议，并以中间观测作为状态锚点选择性融合；在120个三时间点样本上测试，OASA在保持Dice(τ=0.2)与持续性基线相当的同时提高了τ=0.5 Dice，校准可进一步提升但主要由近阈值体素支持度调整驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶质瘤患者治疗后MRI随访可提供序列观测以更新患者特异性的肿瘤状态代理估计，但肿瘤外观与演化轨迹变异大，导致预测困难。作者希望将预测问题重构为"观测感知的数字孪生状态更新"，即利用已有的中间观测锚定患者特异性状态，以缓解纯外推式预测的不稳定性。

Method: 数据：203例患者、594个随访时间点；采用预定义的"无新治疗"标准，从236个候选三元组中保留120个，按患者层面划分为81/24/15（训练/验证/测试）。每个时间点用MRI病灶标签导出的连续体素级肿瘤状态代理图（取值[0,1]）表示。模型：基于SegMamba的单步预测器，从多模态源状态张量预测更新提议。融合：提出"观测锚定选择性同化"（OASA），保留观测到的中间代理作为状态锚点，通过验证集选出的分层病例级规则与体素级软门控选择性施加更新。对比方法：初始扫描预测、无同化的rollout、最近观测持续性、直接预测、OASA、OASA+校准、形态学膨胀。所有检查点、OASA规则与校准阈值仅用验证集选择。

Result: 在15个留出测试三元组、3个随机种子上：OASA在τ=0.2处Dice与持续性基线相当（0.6071±0.0025 vs. 0.6070），在τ=0.5处Dice数值上更高（0.4269±0.0079 vs. 0.3981），RMSE略有增加。校准将τ=0.2的Dice提升至0.6178±0.0025，同时假阳性支持度由11,836升至18,663，假阴性支持度由22,107降至17,536。

Conclusion: OASA能够在以中间观测为锚点的数字孪生更新框架下稳定预测肿瘤状态代理，在高阈值下优于持续性基线且不牺牲低阈值性能。但校准带来的提升主要反映近阈值体素支持度的重新分配，而非生物学预测能力的真正改善，需谨慎解读。代码已公开。

Abstract: Post-treatment MRI in patients with glioma provides serial observations for updating patient-specific tumor-state proxy estimates, but variable appearances and trajectories complicate forecasting. We formulate forecasting as an observation-aware digital-twin update in which an intermediate observation anchors the patient-specific state. Among 203 patients and 594 follow-up time points, a predefined no-new-treatment criterion retained 120 of 236 candidate triplets, split into 81/24/15 training/validation/test triplets at the patient level. Each time point was represented by a continuous voxel-wise tumor-state proxy map in [0,1] derived from MRI lesion labels. A SegMamba-based single-step forecaster predicted update proposals from multimodal source-state tensors. Observation-Anchored Selective Assimilation (OASA) retained the observed intermediate proxy as the state anchor and selectively applied updates through a validation-selected tiered case-level rule and voxel-wise soft gate. We compared initial-scan forecasting, rollout without assimilation, latest-observation persistence, direct prediction, OASA, OASA + calibration, and morphological dilation. Checkpoints, OASA rules, and calibration thresholds were selected using validation data only. Across three seeds on 15 held-out test triplets, OASA maintained Dice at $τ$ = 0.2 comparable to persistence (0.6071 $\pm$ 0.0025 vs. 0.6070) while yielding numerically higher Dice at $τ$ = 0.5 (0.4269 $\pm$ 0.0079 vs. 0.3981), with a small RMSE increase. Calibration increased Dice at $τ$ = 0.2 to 0.6178 $\pm$ 0.0025, increased false-positive (FP) support (11,836$\rightarrow$18,663), and reduced false-negative (FN) support (22,107$\rightarrow$17,536). This reflects near-threshold support calibration rather than improved biological predictive capability. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/jsudg436/longitudinal-proxy-forecasting.

</details>


### [94] [Beyond the Query: Do Retrieval Signals Improve Adaptive Multimodal RAG Routing?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12437)
*Qiaomu Li,Qiuyuan Zhang,Nong Ming*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在文档、音频、视频三类 RAG 中，采用严格匹配的 query-only 与 query+retrieval 路由器对比，发现检索信号在最终评测中并未对路由决策带来可靠增益，提示其路由价值需通过与 query-only 对照来证明而非默认可得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应 RAG 普遍利用检索时信号来决定是否触发额外检索、重排序或多模态步骤，但这类信号是否在已知查询本身的前提下仍具有增量路由价值尚不明确，本文旨在检验这一默认假设。

Method: 跨文档、音频、视频 RAG 设定，在固定可选动作、路由器族、训练流程与评测协议的前提下，构建匹配的 query-only 与 query+retrieval 路由器进行对照实验，并以留出的最终评测衡量路由性能。

Result: 在最终评测中，加入所测试的检索信号相较 query-only 基线未产生可靠的路由提升；部分检索信号虽与后续步骤是否有帮助相关，但这种可预测性并未稳定转化为更优的 RUN/SKIP 决策。

Conclusion: 结论具有方法论意义：除非检索状态特征能在匹配的 query-only 对照下取得改进，否则不应赋予其路由价值；结果并非否定路由或检索状态本身的作用，而是强调其增量价值必须被实证，而非被假定。

Abstract: Adaptive RAG often uses retrieval-time signals to decide whether another retrieval, reranking, or multimodal step should run. We ask whether these signals add routing value once the query itself is already known. Across document, audio, and video RAG, we compare matched query-only and query+retrieval routers while holding the optional actions, router family, training procedure, and evaluation fixed. On the held-out final evaluation, adding the tested retrieval signals does not produce a reliable routing improvement over the query-only baseline. Some retrieval signals are associated with whether a later step will help, but that predictability does not consistently lead to bet- ter RUN/SKIP decisions. The main lesson is therefore methodological: retrieval-state features should not be credited with routing value unless they improve over a matched query-only control. Our results do not show that routing or retrieval state is generally useless; they show that the incremental value of retrieval signals must be demonstrated rather than assumed.

</details>


### [95] [3D Digital Twin Visualization of Multiclass GRF-Based Gait Disorder Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12442)
*Nayoung Son,Minwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个集分类、可解释性与3D可视化于一体的步态分析框架，利用双侧GRF和COP信号区分健康与多种肌骨损伤步态，验证准确率99.00%、测试准确率90.07%，并通过类别特异ε-LRP和Blender 3D可视化提升模型透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化步态分析不仅需要准确分类，还需要可解释的输出。现有方法往往侧重分类精度而忽视模型透明度和结果的可视化检查，难以在临床或实际应用中建立信任并支持样本级审查。

Method: 使用双侧地面反作用力（GRF）和压力中心（COP）信号，在支撑相上进行归一化，并采用训练集统计量进行标准化。构建分类模型区分健康步态与多个肌骨损伤组，在会话级划分下评估。采用类别特异ε-LRP解释方法，识别双侧、多信号成分及不同支撑相阶段的正负贡献。将处理后的GRF信号与模型预测同步于基于Blender的3D可视化中，实现步态试验与分类结果的样本级检查。

Result: 模型在会话级划分下取得99.00%的验证准确率和90.07%的测试准确率。类别特异ε-LRP成功识别出双侧、多个信号成分以及不同支撑相阶段的正负贡献。处理后的GRF信号与模型预测在Blender 3D可视化中实现同步，支持样本级步态试验与分类结果检查。

Conclusion: 该框架将分类、可解释性与3D可视化整合，提升了模型透明度。源代码已在指定GitHub仓库公开。

Abstract: Automated gait analysis requires accurate classification and interpretable outputs. We propose an integrated framework for classifying healthy gait and multiple musculoskeletal impairment groups using bilateral ground reaction force (GRF) and center-of-pressure (COP) signals. The signals were normalized over the stance phase and standardized using training-set statistics. The model achieved a validation accuracy of 99.00\% and a test accuracy of 90.07\% under a session-level split. Class-specific $ε$-LRP identified positive and negative contributions across both sides, multiple signal components, and different stance phases. Separately, the processed GRF signals and model predictions were synchronized within a Blender-based 3D visualization, enabling sample-level inspection of gait trials and classification results. The proposed framework integrates classification, explainability, and 3D visualization to improve model transparency. The source code is available in the following repository: https://github.com/nyoico/grf-gait-3d-visualization.git

</details>


### [96] [SAGE-Loop: Reliable Closed-Loop LLM-Driven AutoML with Trial-and-Correction and Adaptive Ensembling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12455)
*Junquan Gu,Shibo Cui,Xiangfeng Luo,Hang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAGE-Loop：面向LLM驱动AutoML的可靠闭环自适应框架，通过多轮"试错-修复"生成与验证，并在监督/无监督任务中自适应选择集成策略，统一"如何生成模型"与"如何使用模型"，在20个公开数据集上提升分类、回归、聚类任务的性能与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的AutoML管道呈单向结构：中间失败通常被终止或绕过，缺乏执行期即时反馈与自适应优化；一旦运行进入次优或失败状态，缺少过程级纠错机制。同时固定范式偏重模型生成，集成决策保持静态，削弱了执行可靠性与结构多样性的可控利用。因此需要"试错-纠正-改进"的闭环能力，以及基于证据地利用模型多样性。

Method: 提出SAGE-Loop，一个可靠、闭环、自适应的LLM驱动AutoML框架：执行多轮生成与验证以实现试错与修复（trial-and-repair）；在监督与无监督任务中自适应选择集成策略，从而将模型生成与模型使用统一在同一闭环中。

Result: 在20个公开数据集上，SAGE-Loop在分类、回归与聚类任务中一致地提升了性能与稳定性；额外结果进一步表明其具备从执行失败中恢复的能力，并保持稳健的管道行为。

Conclusion: 闭环的试错-纠正-改进机制结合基于证据的多样性利用，可有效提升LLM驱动AutoML的执行可靠性与自适应优化能力，为数据驱动科学与工业实践中的自动化建模提供更稳健的范式。

Abstract: Automated machine learning (AutoML) is reshaping data-driven science and industrial practice, and as large language models are introduced into AutoML, pipeline reliability becomes as important as automation efficiency. However, existing AutoML still struggles to realize instant feedback and adaptive optimization during execution, so once a run drifts into a suboptimal or failed state, it lacks a process-level correction mechanism. The fundamental pathology lies in its one-way pipeline: intermediate failures are typically terminated or bypassed, while fixed paradigms often strengthen model generation but leave ensemble decisions static, weakening both execution reliability and the controlled use of structural diversity. This indicates that LLM-driven AutoML needs a closed-loop ability for trial-correction-improvement together with evidence-based use of model diversity. To this end, we propose SAGE-Loop, a reliable closed-loop, self-adaptive, LLM-driven AutoML framework that performs multi-round generation and validation for trial-and-repair, and adaptively selects ensemble strategies in both supervised and unsupervised tasks, thereby unifying how to generate with how to use models. Across 20 public datasets, SAGE-Loop consistently improves performance and stability on classification, regression, and clustering tasks. Additional results further show its ability to recover from execution failures and maintain robust pipeline behavior.

</details>


### [97] [A Differentially Private Federated Proximal Optimization Framework for Customer Churn Prediction in Heterogeneous Federated Telecom Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12470)
*Joydeb Kumar Sana,Subrata Chakraborty,M M Manjurul Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出差分隐私联邦近端优化框架（DP-FedProx），在电信客户流失预测中兼顾非独立同分布数据下的预测性能与隐私保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电信客户流失预测传统上采用集中式机器学习，需将客户数据集中于中央存储库，引发隐私担忧并可能违反数据保护法规；联邦学习虽可避免原始数据外传，但真实客户数据常呈异质（non-IID）分布，会削弱标准联邦学习性能，且训练模型易受隐私攻击。

Method: 提出基于差分隐私（DP）的联邦近端优化框架（DP-FedProx）。在两个公开电信流失数据集上，训练FedAvg、DP-FedAvg、FedProx及所提DP-FedProx，并引入若干集中式与本地模型作为基线，采用七种常用评价指标进行评估，并使用SHAP进行模型解释。

Result: 基于FedProx的模型一致优于基于FedAvg的模型；与最佳集中式模型相比，DP-FedProx在提供隐私保证的同时仅产生小幅精度下降，预测性能具有竞争力；SHAP分析表明DP-FedProx方法优先关注收入组（revenue group）特征。

Conclusion: 所提DP-FedProx框架在预测性能与数据隐私保护之间实现了实用的平衡，适用于电信客户流失预测场景。

Abstract: Customer churn is one of the major issues in the telecommunication industry. To predict customer churn, conventional centralized machine learning approaches have been widely used. This centralized approach requires customer data to be stored in a central repository, which raises privacy concerns and may violate data protection regulations. Federated learning addresses this problem by allowing multiple telecom operators to collaboratively train a global model without transferring their raw customer data. However, real-world customer data are often heterogeneous (non-IID), which may negatively affect the performance of standard federated learning. Trained models can also suffer from privacy attacks. To address those issues, we propose a Differentially Private (DP) based Federated Proximal optimization (FedProx) framework. All experiments were performed on two publicly available telecom churn datasets. We trained Federated Averaging (FedAvg), DP-FedAvg, FedProx, and the proposed DP-FedProx framework. For baseline comparison, we also used several centralized and local models. To evaluate the models, we employed seven widely used evaluation metrics. The experimental results show that the FedProx based models consistently outperform the FedAvg based models. Compared with the best centralized model, the proposed DP-FedProx framework achieves competitive prediction performance with only a small reduction in accuracy while providing privacy guarantees. To explain our model, we conducted SHAP analysis which shows that DP-FedProx method priorities revenue group features. These results indicate that the proposed DP-FedProx framework provides a practical balance between prediction performance and data privacy protection.

</details>


### [98] [Temporal Recurrence Favors Fewer Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12531)
*Ivan Anokhin,Johan Obando-Ceron,Irina Rish,Sebastian Risi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究流式任务中时间递归（temporal recurrence）对逐步内深度需求的影响：在固定每步计算量的条件下，通过调节层深、专家宽度与并行专家数，比较递归与非递归的最优计算分配，发现递归可将最优分配显著推向更少的层数，且性能相当或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归模型能在时间维度上跨步传递隐式计算，使每步更新建立在此前表示之上，由此引出一个基本问题：既然时间递归已提供跨步的序列计算，那么单步内部还需要多少深度？已有工作表明递归可让浅层模型具备竞争力，但缺乏从计算分配角度的系统分析。

Method: 将问题建模为计算分配问题，在多个计算预算下联合变动三个维度：步内深度（层数）、专家宽度、每层并行专家数；对每个预算，在每步计算量近似匹配的条件下，比较观测到的最优递归与非递归分配及其性能。实验平台为 Sokoban 与自回归 FineWeb 语言建模。

Result: 在 Sokoban 与 FineWeb 语言建模任务上，时间递归使最优观测计算分配显著向更少的层数转移，同时保持相当或更优的性能。

Conclusion: 时间递归与步内深度在计算分配上存在替代关系：递归提供的跨步计算可降低对步内深度的需求，因此在同等每步计算预算下应优先将计算分配给递归机制与专家宽度等其他维度，而非堆叠层数。

Abstract: In streaming tasks, recurrent models can carry latent computation across time, allowing each update to build on representations produced earlier. This raises a basic question: once temporal recurrence provides sequential computation across steps, how much depth is still needed within each step? Prior work has shown that recurrence can make shallow models competitive. We instead study this question as a compute-allocation problem, varying within-step depth, expert width, and the number of parallel experts per layer across several compute budgets. For each budget, we compare the best observed recurrent and non-recurrent allocations and the performance they achieve under approximately matched per-step computation. Across Sokoban and autoregressive FineWeb language modeling, we find that temporal recurrence shifts the best observed compute allocation toward substantially fewer layers, with comparable or better performance.

</details>


### [99] [$\text{GSF-}χ$: Global Stereochemical Fields for Chiral Graph Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12532)
*Jiaqing Xie,Yuxin Wang,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GSF-χ图Transformer，用立体单元调制所有成对交互，并借手性RoPE与C₂投影分别处理反射奇偶信息，在手性分子任务上超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对映体共享原子、键与成对距离，却能在手性环境中表现不同，因此分子编码器须对原子重标号与正常旋转保持不变，同时又不能对反射失明。现有方法难以兼顾等变性、手性敏感性与全局交互。

Method: GSF-χ是一种图Transformer：每个中心或轴向立体单元在所有原子上生成反射偶相位场，手性赝标量χ在潜在query-key块上设定相对旋转方向，形成反射会反转而非保持不变的Chiral-RoPE；再用C₂投影将镜像偶的ECD峰数与峰位同镜像奇的峰符号分离。论文证明该算子的偶-奇分解及在规范角色条件下的标注反转、置换与单位序恒等式，并用性质测试与坐标反射审计端到端验证。

Result: GSF-χ在所有中心ECD输出上领先；轴向任务中Rotation与Symbol分别较最强基线提升12.6%和7.9%。等预算对照表明Rotation优势来自全局带符号支持而非参数量或边数；在完整监督下C₂投影以小幅原始精度代价实现精确对映体对一致性，并在镜像监督稀缺时具备预测性。

Conclusion: 通过将手性信息编码为全局的反射奇偶相位与投影结构，GSF-χ在不牺牲原子重标号与旋转等变性的前提下获得强手性判别能力，并给出手性算子应满足的代数恒等式与验证方案。

Abstract: Enantiomers share atoms, bonds, and pairwise distances yet can behave differently in chiral environments, so molecular encoders must respect atom relabelings and proper rotations without becoming blind to reflection. We introduce GSF-$χ$, a graph transformer in which stereogenic units modulate all pairwise interactions rather than single out one atom as special. Each central or axial stereogenic unit creates a reflection-even phase field over all atoms, a handedness pseudoscalar $χ$ sets the direction of a relative rotation on latent query--key blocks, giving a \textbf{Chiral-RoPE} that reflection inverts rather than leaves fixed. A $C_2$ projection separates mirror-even ECD peak counts and positions from mirror-odd peak signs. We prove the operator's even--odd decomposition and its annotation-inversion, permutation, and unit-order identities under explicit canonical-role conditions; property tests and a coordinate-reflection audit verify the laws end to end. GSF-$χ$ leads every central-ECD output and improves axial Rotation and Symbol by $12.6\%$ and $7.9\%$ over the strongest baseline. Equal-budget controls attribute the Rotation advantage to global signed support rather than parameter or edge count; the $C_2$ projection yields exact enantiomer-pair consistency at a small raw-accuracy cost under complete supervision and becomes predictive when mirror supervision is scarce.

</details>


### [100] [Quality-Constrained Routing over a Fixed Pool of Quantized Mixture-of-Experts Instances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12550)
*Zhenghong Huang,Hongfan Wu,Jiheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究固定实例池下的量化MoE服务路由问题：提出FWP风险指标预测请求级量化损伤，并用窗口级线性规划实现路由，获得优于请求无关混合与静态W4的服务吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化MoE服务可预置同一基础模型的多个量化实例，但量化损伤随请求和位宽变化剧烈；实例物化与副本数受内存与重配置速度约束，故需在固定常驻池内做智能路由以平衡质量与吞吐。

Method: 将实例物化与副本数视为上游供给决策，聚焦固定池内路由：定义FWP（Fragility-Weighted Perplexity），基于参考实例prefill的prompt token计算并校准到候选实例的退化；其底层为精确双专家亲和度—脆弱性分解及条件多层top-k展开，显式保留偏置、交互、路由变更、可分性与高阶项；用校准风险构建窗口级线性规划，得到与LP最优KKT一致的带符号约减奖励分数，并做原问题可行的平局分配。

Result: 在88条扩展Qwen提示上，量化全部6,144个专家块的完整W2、W3、W4实例平均ΔNLL分别为0.9437、0.1832、0.0513；在相同样本与τ=0.1513下，FWP分配达到1.284×离线基于模型的倍率，优于请求无关混合的1.253×和静态W4的1.000×，相对提升2.5%。

Conclusion: 在固定常驻池约束下，基于FWP校准风险的窗口级LP路由可有效利用请求间量化脆弱性差异，在质量预算内显著提升MoE量化服务吞吐。

Abstract: Quantized Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) services can hold several pre-materialized instances of one base model, but quantization damage varies sharply across requests and bitwidths. Because instance materialization and replica counts consume memory and require slow reconfiguration, we treat them as upstream provisioning decisions and study routing within a fixed resident pool. Within this fixed-pool boundary, we route each request to maximize modeled throughput under a class-level expected quality-degradation budget and measured instance capacities. To predict this request-specific risk, we introduce FWP (Fragility-Weighted Perplexity), computed from prompt tokens on a reference-instance prefill and calibrated to candidate-instance degradation. Underlying FWP is an exact two-expert affinity--fragility decomposition and a conditional multi-layer top-$k$ expansion whose bias, interaction, route-change, separability, and higher-order terms remain explicit. Using these calibrated risks, a window-level linear program yields a signed reduced-reward score that is KKT-consistent with the LP optimum under optimal prices and primal-feasible tie allocation. On 88 extended Qwen prompts, complete W2, W3, and W4 instances quantizing all 6,144 expert blocks incur mean $Δ$NLL of $0.9437$, $0.1832$, and $0.0513$. Under the same population and $τ=0.1513$, FWP allocation reaches a $1.284\times$ offline model-based multiplier versus $1.253\times$ for request-agnostic mixing and $1.000\times$ for static W4, an incremental $2.5\%$ relative FWP gain.

</details>


### [101] [TokenMapper: A Step Toward Interoperable Speech Token Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12563)
*Tal Kozakov,Tal Rosenwein,Eliya Nachmani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TokenMapper：一种方向感知框架，实现异构语音分词器间的离散域直接token到token翻译，避免波形重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经音频编解码器将语音离散化为token序列，但不同模型的token空间在词表与码本结构上不一致，无法直接通信；跨模型信息传递通常需解码为波形再用第二分词器重编码，导致延迟增加与信息损失。

Method: 提出TokenMapper方向感知框架，在离散域内直接完成异构语音分词器间的token到token翻译；支持结构不匹配的token空间，包括单码本与多码本表示间的映射，并统一在共享有效token率下进行。

Result: 在GLM-4-Voice、MiMi和DualCodec上取得一致的跨模型性能：翻译WER接近原生重建，绝对WER差距2.5–6.8%；人类MOS为2.29–4.39，与UTMOS呈同方向趋势；端到端延迟相对波形桥接降低4.8–94.5%，单句最高可达972 ms。

Conclusion: 结果证明了无需中间波形重建即可实现跨模型语音token互操作，为跨模型语音token互操作提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Neural audio codecs discretize speech into token sequences, but the resulting token spaces differ in vocabulary and codebook structure, preventing direct communication across models. This limitation affects applications such as conversational voice agents and speech to speech translation systems where multiple speech models must interact. As a result, transferring information between speech systems typically requires decoding to waveform audio and re-encoding with a second tokenizer, increasing latency and introducing potential information loss. To address these limitations, we present TokenMapper, a direction aware framework for direct token to token translation between heterogeneous speech tokenizers in the discrete domain. TokenMapper supports structurally mismatched token spaces, including mappings between single codebook and multi codebook representations, under a shared effective token rate. Experiments on GLM-4-Voice, MiMi and DualCodec show consistent cross model performance. Specifically, translation WER approaches native reconstructions within 2.5-6.8% absolute WER, human MOS for TokenMapper outputs ranges from 2.29 to 4.39, following the same direction level trends as UTMOS and end to end latency is reduced by 4.8-94.5% relative to waveform bridging, reaching up to 972 ms per utterance. These results provide a practical step toward cross model speech token interoperability without intermediate waveform reconstruction.

</details>


### [102] [SCOPE-OPSD: Fisher-Conditioned Privileged Subspaces for On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12579)
*Yunmeng Chen,Kunyu Wang,Peihan Li,Yi Wang,Shuyin Xia,Yi Liu,Xinyong Cheng,Dehui Wang,Xiangyong Zhai,Yanxing Liu,Song Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 SCOPE-OPSD：将特权师生残差投影到由残差协方差与 LM-head Fisher 敏感性估计的冻结 rank-64 子空间，作为 OPSD 的第二监督通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD 仅通过下一 token 概率传递监督，未利用已对齐的末层差异这一潜在通道；同时需要一种设计，能在不混淆"几何方向效应"与"辅助强度效应"的前提下检验该通道。

Method: 将特权教师-学生残差投影到冻结的 rank-64 因子（由残差协方差与语言模型头 Fisher 敏感性估计）；复用 OPSD 已有前向，不增加 rollout 或推理模块；设置匹配 Random 对照，保持相同秩与非零谱，并用 per-arm gradient-RMS 校准，以隔离数据依赖方向的作用。

Result: 在 Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B 的 25/50/75/100 步完整轨迹中，Structured 从未低于 Pure OPSD，12 个模型-检查点组合中 11 个严格提升、4B 第 25 步持平；对匹配 Random 在 12 个组合中 10 个占优。Qwen3-1.7B 第 75 步两次独立重跑均领先匹配 Random 1.39 Macro Avg@12 分；交叉拟合诊断显示留出特权差距捕获能力为随机方向的 4.40 倍。

Conclusion: 结果支持在短预算 OPSD 中使用紧凑的、Fisher 条件化的特权子空间。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) scores student-generated prefixes with a solution-conditioned self-teacher, yet transfers supervision only through next-token probabilities. We ask whether the aligned final-layer discrepancy offers a useful second channel, and how to test that channel without confusing its geometry with auxiliary strength. SCOPE-OPSD projects the privileged teacher-student residual onto a frozen rank-64 factor estimated from residual covariance and language-model-head Fisher sensitivity. It reuses the forwards already required by OPSD and adds neither rollouts nor inference-time modules. A matched Random control preserves the structured factor's rank and nonzero spectrum and uses per-arm gradient-RMS calibration, isolating the effect of the data-dependent orientation. Across the complete 25/50/75/100-step trajectories for Qwen3-1.7B, 4B, and 8B, Structured is never below Pure OPSD, with strict gains in 11 of the 12 model-checkpoint combinations and an exact tie at 4B step 25. Structured also exceeds matched Random in 10 of the 12 combinations. At step 75 on Qwen3-1.7B, Structured exceeds matched Random by 1.39 Macro Avg@12 points in each of two independent training reruns. A cross-fitted diagnostic also shows 4.40 times greater held-out privileged-gap capture than the matched random orientation. The results support a compact, Fisher-conditioned privileged subspace for short-budget OPSD.

</details>


### [103] [Clustering-Based Balanced Sampling and Allocation with Data Parallelism for High-Performance Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12584)
*Hyunjin Kim,Youngeun Nam,Jaemin Han,Wonhyeok Choi,Jae-Gil Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CluSTER，一种面向DP指令微调的聚类感知平衡采样框架，通过梯度空间聚类与DP感知均衡分配构建精简数据集，在几乎不掉点的情况下将训练时间最多减少69.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM指令微调数据集体量大、冗余且分布不均衡，限制高效适配；朴素大批量微调在DP多GPU下反复包含过表示样本组、却对欠表示但信息量高的样本覆盖不足。

Method: CluSTER：基于梯度空间聚类做代表性精简数据集筛选，结合DP感知的均衡分配，实现聚类与worker的双层覆盖；并通过加权更新保持原始数据分布。

Result: 在多个指令微调数据集上，相比已有采样与数据精简方法，训练时间最多降低69.6%，且几乎无精度损失；同时减少冗余计算、提升训练稳定性。

Conclusion: CluSTER能在不损害模型质量的前提下显著提升DP指令微调的效率，是一种有效的聚类感知平衡采样式数据精简方案。

Abstract: Instruction-tuning datasets for large language models (LLMs) are often large, redundant, and imbalanced, limiting efficient adaptation. Naive large-batch fine-tuning repeatedly includes overrepresented sample groups while weakly covering underrepresented but informative ones, especially under data parallelism (DP) across multiple GPUs. We propose CluSTER, a Cluster-aware balanced Sampling framework for Training Efficient data Reduction in DP instruction tuning. CluSTER curates a representative reduced dataset through gradient-space clustering and DP-aware balanced allocation, ensuring dual-level coverage across clusters and workers, while preserving the original data distribution by weighted update. As a result, CluSTER reduces redundant computation and improves training stability without compromising model quality. Across multiple instruction-tuning datasets, CluSTER reduces training time by up to 69.6% with almost no accuracy loss compared to prior sampling and data reduction methods. Code is available at https://github.com/kaist-dmlab/CluSTER.

</details>


### [104] [MAxBench: A Multinomial Concept Recovery Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13072)
*Divya Appapogu,Freya Behrens,Yonatan Belinkov,Aaron Mueller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MAxBench，一个与几何结构无关的多项概念表示评估框架，基于从恢复的概念表示中采样；用其比较 10 种定位方法（5 类几何）在 6 个概念、4 个模型上的表现，发现仿射子空间在引导性和召回率上更优，其优势主要来自非零偏移，流形引导具有竞争力，但整体上无方法稳定超越提示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型行为的细粒度控制（如引导）是可解释性研究中最具可操作性的成果之一。对于拒绝等二元概念，激活空间中的单一方向通常足以实现引导；但许多概念（如动物、国家）并非二元，而是包含多个子类别、每类又有多个实例。对此类概念，可能的表示几何搜索空间远大于二元概念，因此尚不清楚何种几何最合适、何种方法最能有效恢复它们，故需构建评估框架来系统比较。

Method: 提出 MAxBench：一个与几何假设无关的多元（多项）概念表示评估框架，核心思路是基于从恢复出的概念表示中进行采样来评估。利用该框架比较 10 种定位方法（覆盖 5 种几何类型），并在 6 个概念和 4 个模型上进行实验。

Result: 实验发现：(i) 仿射子空间比秩一或线性子空间引导更可靠且召回率更高；(ii) 这一优势主要归因于更优的非零偏移，而非基的选择；(iii) 在适用时，流形引导与最佳方法具有竞争力；(iv) 没有任何方法能稳定优于提示（prompting），这与二元概念上的先前发现一致。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了将可解释性研究与元评估的范围扩展到结构更丰富多样的概念上的重要性。

Abstract: Fine-grained control of language model behaviors (e.g., steering) is among the more actionable outcomes of interpretability research. For binary concepts such as refusal, a single direction in activation space often suffices for steering. However, many concepts are not binary: Animals and Countries contain many subcategories, each with multiple instances. For these concepts, the search space over possible representation geometries is far larger than for binary concepts; it is thus not clear what geometries are most appropriate, nor what methods are most effective at recovering them. In this work, we introduce MAxBench, a geometry-agnostic evaluation framework for multinomial concept representations based on sampling from the recovered concept representation. We use MAxBench to compare 10 localization methods (covering 5 geometry types) across 6 concepts and 4 models. Using this framework, we find that (i) affine subspaces steer more reliably and have greater recall than rank-one or linear subspaces; (ii) much of this advantage is due to better non-zero offsets rather than the choice of bases; (iii) manifold steering is competitive with the best methods when applicable; and (iv) no method consistently outperforms prompting, in alignment with prior findings on binary concepts. These findings underscore the importance of expanding the scope of interpretability research and meta-evaluation to concepts with more varied structure.

</details>


### [105] [Where Decoder Cosine Similarity Fails for SAE Feature Flow Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12591)
*Hendrik Droste,Christian Medeiros Adriano,Kathrin Korte,Holger Giese*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建残差流特征与MLP更新特征的"转移图谱"三元组，发现状态特征与更新特征共同预测下游残差特征时多为非平凡组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型通过微调、模型编辑与对齐持续演化，需理解其内部计算机制；SAE虽提供可解释特征字典，但状态特征与更新特征如何交互产生下游残差特征尚不清楚。作者以MLP更新作为首个测试案例。

Method: 构建三元组 s_k + u_j → t_ℓ 的转移图谱，其中残差状态特征与MLP更新特征联合预测目标残差特征；通过消融解码后的更新特征来因果验证候选三元组。在Pythia-160M L7→L8上做2千万token的完整图谱实验；在Gemma-3-4B L21→L22上将2千万token实验作为跨模型初步检验，仅对排名前30,000的候选三元组做因果验证。

Result: Pythia中找到38,125个强消融效应转移，其中88.0%的状态-目标与更新-目标解码器余弦相似度均低于0.7。Gemma中53.6%的强效应三元组两个余弦相似度均低于0.7，方向与Pythia一致但更弱，因为更新-目标余弦能恢复许多最强Gemma效应，且该实验并非完整图谱因果验证。

Conclusion: 特征流图谱可作为表征更新机制的诊断工具，进而为引导模型更新的工具提供依据；未来工作将在更多层、模型与SAE族上验证更复杂模式。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly adapted through fine-tuning, model editing, and alignment procedures while retaining previously acquired capabilities. Understanding the internal computations that support these adaptations is therefore becoming increasingly important for continual model evolution. Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) provide interpretable feature dictionaries for residual-stream activations and sublayer outputs, but it remains unclear how state features and update features interact to produce downstream residual features. In this work, we focus on MLP updates as a first test case.
