Extensive experiments on real production cluster traces demonstrate that MCRL2 significantly outperforms existing baselines in load balancing, scheduling success rate and average completion time across diverse workload patterns.

</details>


### [141] [A Unified and Constrained View of Regularization-Based Robust Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13050)
*Amine Andam,Jamal Bentahar,Mustapha Hedabou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将多种基于正则化的深度强化学习对抗鲁棒训练方法统一到一个理论框架下，通过推导标称策略与最坏情况策略性能差距的新上界，提出以约束优化形式进行鲁棒训练并自适应调整正则权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于正则化的深度强化学习鲁棒训练方法缺乏统一的理论解释，且正则权重（拉格朗日乘子）通常固定，需要手动调参，难以保证鲁棒性与性能的平衡。

Method: 推导标称策略与最坏情况策略之间性能差距的新上界，证明该上界等于现有正则目标加上标称与最坏情况策略间的KL散度惩罚；据此将鲁棒训练建模为约束优化问题，并让拉格朗日乘子与策略联合更新以自动调节正则权重。

Result: 理论分析表明现有方法等价于固定拉格朗日乘子的特例，并解释了KL惩罚提升鲁棒性的原因；在多个连续控制任务上的大量对抗评估验证了理论分析的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为基于正则化的对抗鲁棒强化学习提供了统一的理论视角，并提出可自适应调整正则权重的约束优化训练框架，实验验证其有效性。

Abstract: Regularization-based methods have become a standard approach for training Deep Reinforcement Learning policies against adversarial input perturbations. In this paper, we unify these methods by deriving new upper bounds on the performance gap between the nominal and worst-case policies. Each upper bound is expressed as an existing regularization objective plus a KL-divergence penalty between the nominal and worst-case policies, which further explains why adding a KL penalty improves robustness in practice. Building on these bounds, we formulate robust training as a constrained optimization problem, showing that existing methods correspond to the special case of a fixed Lagrange multiplier. We instead update the multiplier jointly with the policy to automatically tune the regularization weight. Finally, we conduct extensive adversarial evaluations across several continuous control tasks to validate our theoretical analysis.

</details>


### [142] [Benign Loss Landscapes Can Coexist with Worst-Case Hardness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13057)
*Zach Furman,Stephan Wäldchen,Yangda Bei,Liam Hodgkinson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究树张量网络（TTN）的优化景观，证明其可嵌入任意read-once布尔公式（含梯度下降多项式时间不可学的worst-case目标），但对所有可实目标损失景观条件良性（最小范数局部极小即全局极小），学习困难源自秩亏导致的高阶退化鞍点，并以parity函数为案例说明景观几何与计算硬度的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络能表达多项式时间可求值但梯度下降多项式时间不可学的worst-case目标，然而实战中却能学好，关键是何种真实目标的非泛型结构促成了这一点。现有代理模型无法提出该问题：深度线性网络完全没有难学目标，核方法与无限宽极限又无法高效求值这类目标。因此需要一个既包含难学目标、又可高效求值的模型类来研究该问题。

Method: 以树张量网络（TTN）为模型类，它推广了深度线性网络与Tucker分解。作者证明TTN可嵌入任意read-once布尔公式；分析其损失景观的良性条件；并通过parity函数的案例研究考察景观几何与计算硬度的关系。

Result: TTN能嵌入任意read-once布尔公式，因而含有与神经网络相同机制下梯度下降多项式时间不可学的多项式规模目标。尽管如此，作者证明对每个可实目标其损失景观都是条件良性的：每个最小范数局部极小都是全局极小。因此坏局部极小并非区分TTN中典型问题与worst-case问题的原因；TTN中的学习困难反而来自高阶退化鞍点，作者证明这类鞍点由秩亏引起。

Conclusion: 在TTN中，坏局部极小不区分典型与worst-case问题，学习困难由秩亏引起的高阶退化鞍点导致；parity函数案例展示了TTN将景观几何与计算硬度相联系的潜力。

Abstract: Deep neural networks are expressive enough to contain worst-case targets that can be evaluated in polynomial time but cannot be learned in polynomial time by gradient descent. For practical tasks they nonetheless learn well, raising the question of what non-generic structure of real-world targets enables this. Existing surrogate models cannot pose this question because they either lack hard-to-learn targets entirely (deep linear networks) or cannot evaluate such targets efficiently (kernel methods, infinite-width limits). We study tree tensor networks (TTNs), a model class that generalizes deep linear networks and Tucker decompositions. We show they embed arbitrary read-once Boolean formulas, and thus contain polynomial-size targets that cannot be learned by gradient descent in polynomial time under the same mechanism as neural networks. Despite this, we prove that their loss landscapes are conditionally benign for every realizable target: every local minimum that is minimum-norm is global. Thus, surprisingly, bad local minima are not what distinguishes between typical and worst-case problems in TTNs. Instead, learning difficulty in TTNs can arise from high-order degenerate saddle points, which we show are caused by rank-deficiency. This is explored through a case study of the parity function, illustrating the potential for TTNs to relate landscape geometry to computational hardness.

</details>


### [143] [CanvasAnneal: Curriculum Reinforcement Learning for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13060)
*Blake Olson,Yuhang Song,Emmett McQuinn,Yuan Shangguan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CanvasAnneal——一种课程引导的扩散语言模型RL框架，通过注入教师模型的推理先验并逐步退火去除引导，缓解扩散RL的探索瓶颈，加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLM）具备并行生成优势，但在复杂推理与工具使用任务上落后于自回归模型。虽然已有研究将强化学习（RL）应用于DLM，但标准RL方法存在探索瓶颈。

Method: 从更强的教师模型中注入推理先验以引导RL探索。具体做法：在RL初始阶段，将教师生成的推理轨迹注入初始扩散画布（canvas）以实现热启动探索；随着训练推进，逐步移除该引导，要求模型独立生成更多推理轨迹。

Result: 在数学推理与工具使用基准上，CanvasAnneal相比标准diffu-GRPO在MATH500、Countdown和Tau2上取得提升，并在若干任务上显著加快奖励提升速度，但增益具有任务依赖性。

Conclusion: 结构化的训练期引导可以缓解扩散RL中的探索瓶颈，并加快在较难任务上的收敛。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) offer promising parallel generation capabilities but lag behind autoregressive models in complex reasoning and tool-use tasks. While Reinforcement Learning (RL) has recently been applied to enhance DLMs, standard RL approaches suffer from an exploration bottleneck. To address this, we inject reasoning priors from a stronger teacher model to guide RL exploration. In this paper, we introduce CanvasAnneal, a curriculum-guided diffusion RL framework. During the initial RL phase, we warm-start exploration by injecting teacher-generated reasoning traces into the initial diffusion canvas. As training progresses, we gradually remove this guidance and require the model to generate more of the reasoning trajectory independently. Across mathematical reasoning and tool-use benchmarks, CanvasAnneal improves over standard diffu-GRPO on MATH500, Countdown, and Tau2 and substantially accelerates reward improvement on several tasks, while gains are task-dependent. Our results suggest that structured training-time guidance can alleviate exploration bottlenecks in diffusion RL and speed up convergence on harder tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [Signed Sensitivity of Expected Hitting Time to Mutation Rate in the (1+1) EA: Per-State Sign Theorems and Verifiable Certificates for Non-Lumpable Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12510)
*RenKai Wang*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文研究(1+1) EA在标准位变异下期望命中时间对变异率的敏感性：指出改进事件非单调导致Margulis-Russo公式不适用，给出精确三维分离、符号证书与OneMax上的逐初始状态符号定理，并扩展到非可集中正线性族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准(1+1) EA的命中时间$H_p$对变异率的敏感性缺乏严格刻画。既有工具多针对单调事件，而改进事件在变异掩码上非单调，直接套用Margulis-Russo型公式存在形式化陷阱；同时需要判断单步成功率是否足以决定期望命中时间。

Method: 先给出精确三维分离构造：两个适应度函数共享完整单步成功率曲线但期望命中时间为不同有理数。基于runtime导数$H'_p=(I-Q_p)^{-1}Q'_p H_p$构造可计算的双残差符号证书；在OneMax上证明逐初始状态符号定理；将框架推广到非可集中正线性族，给出非可集中性见证、块区间双残差证书、$c\in[1/4,1/2]$上的统一符号界及异质实例证书。所有有限验证使用精确有理算术。

Result: 证明改进事件非单调，故无符号(total-influence)形式Margulis-Russo不适用，正确对象为带符号端点差；构造两个适应度函数共享单步成功率曲线但期望命中时间为两个不同的精确有理数；在OneMax上对每个非最优初始状态证明$0<c<1$时$\partial_c H<0$，$c=1$时仅距离一状态为驻点；对显式非可集中正线性族在$n\ge 8$偶数尺度上得到$c\in[1/4,1/2]$统一界$\partial_c\mathbb{E}T\le -9n/16$，并给出覆盖63态中57态的异质实例证书$H'_x\le -1/6$。

Conclusion: 单步成功率量不能决定期望命中时间；期望命中时间的变异率敏感性需用带符号端点差与双残差证书刻画。所提框架在OneMax及非可集中正线性族上给出可验证的负导数符号结论，且不主张超出该组合的新颖性。

Abstract: For the (1+1) evolutionary algorithm with standard bit mutation, we study the sensitivity of the expected hitting time $H_p=\mathbb{E}_x T$ to the mutation rate. We first point out an easily overlooked formalization pitfall: the improvement event is not monotone in the mutation mask, so the unsigned (total-influence) form of the Margulis-Russo formula does not apply; the correct object is the signed endpoint difference. Second, we give an exact three-dimensional separation: two fitness functions share the entire one-step success-rate curve, yet their expected hitting times are two different exact rational numbers; hence one-step success-rate quantities do not determine the expected hitting time. Building on the runtime derivative $H'_p=(I-Q_p)^{-1}Q'_p H_p$, we construct computable double-residual sign certificates, prove a per-initial-state sign theorem on OneMax (for every non-optimal initial state, $\partial_c H<0$ on $0<c<1$, where $p=c/n$; at $c=1$ only the distance-one state is stationary), and extend the framework to non-lumpable positive linear families: an explicit non-lumpability witness, a block-interval double-residual certificate that covers all states without enumerating them, a uniform sign bound $\partial_c\mathbb{E}T\le -9n/16$ over the whole interval $c\in[1/4,1/2]$ for an explicit family at all even scales $n\ge 8$, and a heterogeneous instance certificate $H'_x\le -1/6$ on 57 of 63 states across $c=1$. All finite verifications use exact rational arithmetic. A bounded systematic literature search did not uncover this exact combination, although the underlying tools are well established; we therefore make no novelty claim beyond the stated combination.

</details>


### [145] [MAAPO:an innovative membrane algorithm based on artificial protozoa optimizer for multilevel threshold image segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12756)
*Xiaopeng Wang,Vaclav Snasel,Seyedali Mirjalili,Jeng-Shyang Pan*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出基于人工原生动物优化器（APO）的膜算法MAAPO，用于全局优化，并应用于多阈值图像分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升元启发式算法的种群多样性与探索-开发平衡，并解决复杂图像分割任务。

Method: 以APO为基础算法，引入膜计算（MC）并行分布式框架，并在自养模型中采用基于轮盘的适应度-距离平衡（RFDB）机制进行自适应参考点选择。

Result: 在CEC2017测试集上与12种先进算法对比表现更优；在基于Otsu与Kapur熵的多阈值分割中取得更高质量，PSNR、SSIM、FSIM指标更佳。

Conclusion: MAAPO为元启发式算法提供了有效增强策略，并为复杂图像分割提供了新的高适用性方法。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel membrane algorithm based on artificial protozoa optimizer (MAAPO) for global optimization problems. The artificial protozoa optimizer (APO) is adopted as the base meta-heuristic algorithm due to its novelty and competitive performance. MAAPO integrates two key innovations:(1) a membrane computing (MC) framework that introduces a parallel distributed paradigm to improve population diversity and search dynamics, and (2) an enhanced autotrophic model within APO that uses a roulette-based fitness-distance balance (RFDB) mechanism for adaptive reference point selection. These strategies collectively enhance the algorithm's exploration-exploitation balance and global search capabilities. To validate its performance, MAAPO is tested against 12 advanced algorithms on the CEC2017 test suite, and further applied to the multilevel thresholding image segmentation problem using Otsu and Kapur entropy as objective functions. The quality of segmented images is assessed using peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR), structural similarity index (SSIM), and feature similarity index (FSIM) metrics. Experimental results demonstrate that MAAPO outperforms its counterparts, delivering superior segmentation quality. This research on MAAPO contributes an effective enhancement strategy to meta-heuristic algorithms and introduces a novel, highly applicable approach for complex image segmentation tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [146] [Occamy-1.0: Open Pareto-frontier 35B Intelligence for Co-work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11977)
*Wenhui Chen,Shiwen Cheng,Hao Dong,Chenda Duan,Ruixiang Feng,Zhong Guan,Boqiang Guo,Xueyuan Han,Haojie Hao,Liangmeng Huang,Zhelong Huang,Xinke Kong,Hongyu Li,Jiazheng Li,Junbo Li,Qingchuan Li,Yukun Lian,Chang Liu,Tianyu Liu,Zicheng Liu,Shuyi Ouyang,Yijun Pan,Kunyu Shi,Xiaojun Tang,Bingquan Wang,Kesu Wang,Yuchen Wang,Sibo Wei,Sicong Xie,Xiaoying Xing,Yi Xu,Zhijun Xu,Hongwei Xue,Qingcheng Zeng,Di Zhang,Guannan Zhang,Haochen Zhang,Tianlong Zhang,Tianyu Zhao,Tianyu Zhao,Yanjun Zheng,Jialong Zhu,Zijian Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Occamy-1.0：面向协作工作流的高性价比共作模型，通过在Qwen3.6-35B-A3B后训练检查点上继续训练，结合执行接地数据与分阶段后训练，在多项共作基准上达到同规模最强并在成本—性能帕累托前沿处于低成本拐点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作智能体需在多次模型调用中完成信息收集、工具使用、编码与文件操作等复合工作流，成本与时延随整段任务累积。其实际价值不仅取决于峰值能力，更取决于能力的交付效率。而日常工作中的多数步骤侧重状态跟踪、协调、恢复与任务收尾，而非前沿规模的推理，因此需要一种成本高效的共作模型。

Method: 以已后训练的Qwen3.6-35B-A3B检查点为起点继续训练得到Occamy-1.0。具体做法包括：构建执行接地的数据与环境；跨多种执行框架采集可重放的长时程轨迹；采用分阶段后训练来逐步建立并巩固互补的执行能力。

Result: 在广泛的共作基准套件上，Occamy-1.0始终位居同规模模型中最强行列，并在若干任务上与显著更大的前沿系统保持竞争力。在设定的评估与定价协议下，其在四个代表性基准上的综合表现在观测到的成本—性能帕累托前沿上处于低成本拐点。工具调用、编码与指令遵循的辅助评估表明该专用化未损害广泛的智能体能力。模型权重与部分训练数据已开源。

Conclusion: 该工作表明通过执行接地数据、可重放长时程轨迹与分阶段后训练，可在中等规模模型上实现成本高效的共作智能体专用化，在保持广泛智能体能力的同时取得成本—性能折中优势，并通过开源权重与训练数据推动实用共作智能体及智能体后训练研究。

Abstract: Co-work agents execute complex workflows that combine information gathering, tool use, coding, and file manipulation across many model invocations. Because cost and latency accumulate over the full episode, their practical value depends not only on peak capability but also on how efficiently that capability is delivered. Yet many steps in everyday work emphasize state tracking, coordination, recovery, and follow-through rather than frontier-scale reasoning. We present Occamy-1.0, a cost-efficient co-work model obtained by further training the post-trained Qwen3.6-35B-A3B checkpoint. We construct execution-grounded data and environments, capture replayable long-horizon trajectories across multiple harnesses, and use staged post-training to develop and consolidate complementary execution capabilities. Across a broad suite of co-work benchmarks, Occamy-1.0 is consistently among the strongest comparably sized models and remains competitive with substantially larger frontier systems on several tasks. Under our stated evaluation and pricing protocol, its aggregate performance across four representative benchmarks places it at the low-cost knee of the observed cost--performance Pareto frontier. Supporting evaluations in tool calling, coding, and instruction following further show that this specialization preserves broad agentic capability. We release the model weights and a subset of the training data to support research on practical co-work agents and agentic post-training.

</details>


### [147] [Harness or Model? Isolating the Harness Effect in Agentic Coding with a Contamination-Controlled Private Suite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11987)
*Mohsen Arjmandi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在256个私密任务的配对同模型对比中，厂商原生coding harness并未稳定优于中立（neutral）harness：Opus 4.8上平均差-1.25pp，GPT-5.5上+1.25pp，置信区间均跨零；但Opus的平均差由方向相反的两层构成（仓库任务-9.0pp、竞赛任务+23.7pp）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业界普遍假设"厂商原生harness搭配自家模型"能解决更多任务，但这一假设缺乏配对、同模型、污染受控的实证检验。作者希望量化harness本身（工具、提示、控制流）对性能与成本的独立贡献。

Method: 在私密、污染受控的256个任务套件上进行同模型配对对比：80个任务在claude-opus-4-8上分别运行于claude-agent-sdk与deepagents，亦在gpt-5.5上运行于openai-codex SDK与deepagents；gemini-3.5-flash与deepseek-v3.2作为侧格。计划800次运行中792次由隔离oracle判分；分析采用task-bootstrap置信区间、标签置换检验，并按冻结价目以逐轮原始用量重新计价。

Result: 两个对比均未显示任一方harness的平均优势：Opus 4.8为-1.25pp（48.8% vs 50.0%，95% CI [-10.0, +7.5]），GPT-5.5为+1.25pp（55.6% vs 54.4%，CI [-4.4, +6.9]）。Opus平均差由方向相反的两层合并：原生harness在61个仓库任务上落后9.0pp，在19个竞赛任务上领先23.7pp（标签置换p=0.003）。正确性与完成度也分离：81次触及墙钟上限被取消的运行中，22次已产出通过补丁。按冻结价目重新计价，中立harness每个已解任务的成本在Opus 4.8上为1.3–1.6倍、在GPT-5.5上为1.2倍（基于观测用量的估计）。Anthropic账户有58次运行无用量记录，将该开销分配给任一侧会使Opus比值在0.7–2.3之间变动，故计费口径下的排序仍未确定。

Conclusion: 厂商原生harness并不构成相对中立harness的普遍优势；结论受任务类型分层、单次运行完成度与用量记录缺失的显著影响。分层依据为事后观察、需设计性重复验证。作者发布orchestrator、判分oracle、重分析代码与派生聚合数据，任务保持私密；本版本更正了2026年8月手稿中因自有遥测用量语义缺陷导致的成本数字（第5.1节）。

Abstract: An agentic coding system couples a language model to a harness: the tools, prompts and control flow that turn a chat model into an autonomous software engineer. Vendors ship harnesses tuned to their own models, and practitioners assume the vendor-native pairing solves more tasks. We measure that assumption with paired same-model contrasts on a private, contamination-controlled suite of 256 repository and post-cutoff contest tasks. The same 80 tasks ran under claude-agent-sdk and under deepagents on claude-opus-4-8, and under the openai-codex SDK and deepagents on gpt-5.5, with gemini-3.5-flash and deepseek-v3.2 as side cells. 792 of 800 planned runs were graded by an isolated oracle. Neither contrast resolves an average advantage for either harness: -1.25 pp for Opus 4.8 (48.8% vs 50.0%, task-bootstrap 95% CI [-10.0, +7.5]) and +1.25 pp for GPT-5.5 (55.6% vs 54.4%, CI [-4.4, +6.9]). The Opus average combines opposite strata: the native harness trails by 9.0 pp on the 61 repository tasks and leads by 23.7 pp on the 19 contest tasks (label-permutation p = 0.003). The partition was chosen after seeing the data and needs a designed replication. Correctness and completion also separate: 22 of 81 runs cancelled at the wall-clock ceiling had produced a passing patch. Re-priced from raw per-turn usage at frozen list prices, the neutral harness cost 1.3 to 1.6 times as much per solved task on Opus 4.8 and 1.2 times on GPT-5.5. These are observed-usage estimates. On the Anthropic account 58 runs left no usage record, and allocating that spend to either cell would move the Opus ratio between 0.7 and 2.3, so the billed ordering is unresolved. This revision corrects an August 2026 manuscript whose cost figures rested on a usage-semantics defect in our own telemetry (Section 5.1). We release the orchestrator, grading oracle, reanalysis code and derived aggregates. The tasks stay private.

</details>


### [148] [T-GADE: Thermodynamical Generative-AI-Driven Evolution of LLM Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12286)
*Kyoko Ogawa,Naoki Mori*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出T-GADE，将热力学遗传算法扩展至LLM结构化产物的进化，以自由能为目标统一世代与稳态更新，并在在线装箱任务上验证其多样性控制与性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 融合进化计算与LLM不仅需要生成能力，还需对种群多样性进行控制；LLM输出的结构化产物（如描述配代码）为多样性度量与选择机制提供了新对象。

Method: T-GADE通过LLM遗传算子与产物级多样性评估扩展热力学遗传算法；采用统一自由能目标支持世代式与稳态更新，并引入Fermi型占据（排除重复基因型）与Bose型占据（允许多重重复）。理论上给出精确的单成员移除规则及恢复EoH零温生存规则的条件。

Result: 在EoH论文的在线装箱任务上，T=0.003的世代式Bose型T-GADE将训练超出量中位数由1.152%降至约0.815%（降低约29%），20次运行/配置，双尾Mann-Whitney p=0.042，Cliff's δ=0.378；在其排名最高的两个最终候选间做验证选择后，转移超出量中位数与EoH相同为0.496%。

Conclusion: 结果表明热力学选择与对保留产物进行验证式使用具有实用价值，可在保持迁移性能的同时改善训练性能。

Abstract: Integrating evolutionary computation and large language models (LLMs) requires control of population diversity as well as generative capability. Among LLM outputs, those with explicit structure, such as a description paired with code, are structured artifacts; we use artifact for short. We propose T-GADE, which evolves these artifacts by extending thermodynamical genetic algorithms through LLM-based genetic operators and artifact-level diversity evaluation. A common free-energy objective supports generational and steady-state updates, with Fermi-type occupancy excluding repeated genotypes and Bose-type occupancy permitting them. We establish exact one-member removal and conditions for recovering the zero-temperature survival rule of Evolution of Heuristics (EoH). On the online bin-packing task studied in the EoH paper, excess measures relative bin-count overhead above a volume lower bound. Training excess uses search instances; transfer excess uses instances with another bin capacity. Generational Bose-type T-GADE at $T=0.003$ reduced median training excess by approximately 29%, from 1.152% to 0.815%, over 20 runs per configuration (two-sided Mann-Whitney $p=0.042$, Cliff's $δ=0.378$). Validation selection among its two highest-ranked final candidates reached the same median transfer excess as EoH, 0.496%. These results demonstrate the utility of thermodynamical selection and validation-based use of retained artifacts.

</details>


### [149] [Reading the Whole Heart: Latent-Attention Masked Autoencoders for Multimodal Cardiac Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12035)
*Andrea Agostini,Simon Böhi,Moritz Vandenhirtz,Samuel Ruiperez-Campillo,Max Krähenmann,Silke Mühlstedt,Irene Cannistraci,Ece Özkan Elsen,Julia E. Vogt,Thomas M. Sutter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Latent-Attention Masked Autoencoders (LAMAE)，一种多模态、结构感知的掩码自编码器，在自监督预训练阶段就联合学习患者级表示，通过在潜在空间中的共享潜在注意力模块实现跨模态信息交换，在多项医院住院任务上超越模态专用预训练及强基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心血管诊断依赖ECG、超声心动图、胸片和临床变量等互补模态的整合，各模态捕捉相关但不同的心脏生理信息。然而现有医学基础模型多为单一模态专用，仅在下游微调或后训练阶段进行模态组合，这既丢弃了临床医生自然整合的跨模态证据，也忽略了每个模态内部的结构信息。

Method: LAMAE是一种多模态、结构感知的掩码自编码器，在自监督预训练过程中联合学习患者级表示。它不做事后融合，而是通过共享的潜在注意力模块在潜在空间中直接交换信息；该模块作用于study-view-entity的层级结构之上，从而支持变量观测的聚合以及对缺失模态的鲁棒处理。模型在超过120万条MIMIC-IV住院记录上进行预训练。

Result: LAMAE在多模态住院任务（院内死亡率、ICD-10与DRG编码、住院时长）上优于模态专用预训练以及强对比学习和视觉-语言基线，同时在单模态任务上保持竞争力。即便测试时仅有单一模态可用，性能增益依然存在。

Conclusion: 同时建模模态内与模态间结构能够产生更鲁棒、更具可迁移性的表示，验证了在预训练阶段进行潜在空间跨模态交互的价值。

Abstract: Cardiovascular diagnosis rests on integrating complementary modalities, like ECG, echocardiography, chest radiographs, and clinical variables, each capturing distinct but correlated aspects of cardiac physiology. Yet most medical foundation models remain modality-specific, combining modalities only for finetuning or post-training. This discards the cross-modal evidence clinicians naturally integrate and ignores the structure within each modality. We introduce Latent-Attention Masked Autoencoders (LAMAE), a multimodal, structure-aware masked autoencoder that jointly learns patient-level representations during self-supervised pretraining. Rather than fusing modalities post hoc, LAMAE exchanges information directly in the latent space through a shared latent-attention module operating over a study-view-entity hierarchy, enabling aggregation of variable observations and graceful handling of missing modalities. Pretrained on over 1.2 million MIMIC-IV hospital stays, LAMAE outperforms modality-specific pretraining and strong contrastive and vision-language baselines across multimodal hospital-stay tasks, such as in-hospital mortality, ICD-10 and DRG coding, and length of stay, while remaining competitive on unimodal tasks. These gains persist even when only a single modality is available at test time, showing that modeling both intra- and inter-modal structure yields more robust, transferable representations.

</details>


### [150] [Competence-Gated Pooling of Language Models and Priors for Event Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12101)
*Aditi Tiwari,Aashrith Bandaru,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究混合预测中语言模型相对外部预测的"边际价值"，提出基于结果的"能力门控"机制，通过域级权重估计、向全局权重收缩与再校准，在2,357个二元问题上将Brier分数从0.0771改善至0.0732，且显著优于全局组合，但在官方市场子集上无明显改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在混合预测场景中，语言模型通常只是可用的若干信号之一，系统往往已拥有市场、群体或统计预测。因此真正需要判断的不是模型的独立准确率，而是模型在已有外部预测之上是否带来额外的边际信息，即"相对能力"。在此设定下，需要明确模型分歧何时能改进外部预测，以及如何决定是否采纳模型输出。

Method: 在Brier损失下刻画模型分歧改进外部预测的条件，并推导使用域特定池化权重（而非全局池化权重）所能获得的增益。据此提出"能力门控"（competence gate）：从已揭晓的结果中估计域级信号源权重，将不确定估计向全局权重收缩，并对池化后的预测进行再校准。

Result: 在2,357个已揭晓的二元问题与五个语言模型上，门控将主要外部基线从0.0771 Brier改善至0.0732，并显著优于全局预测组合。该增益在泄漏控制下、以及在池化结构化集合上与泄漏安全的时间序列先验对比中仍保持显著，并在FRED上获得独立证据。相反，在官方ForecastBench市场子集上门控未带来显著改进，主要让位于市场。对四个Qwen模型，语言化置信度无法可靠识别模型何时优于外部预测，而基于结果估计的能力则支持更好的弃权决策。

Conclusion: 应以测得的边际价值而非独立准确率来指导语言模型的选择性使用。能力门控提供了一种实用方法，在模型确有增量价值时采纳其输出，在无增量价值时（如市场子集）则让位于外部预测；同时表明语言化置信度不足以替代基于结果的能力估计。

Abstract: In hybrid forecasting, a language model is often one of several available signals. A system may already have a market, crowd, or statistical forecast and must decide whether the model adds useful information or should be ignored. The relevant target is therefore not standalone model accuracy, but relative competence, defined as the model's marginal value beyond the available external forecast. Under Brier loss, we characterize when model disagreement can improve an external forecast and derive the gain from using domain-specific rather than global pooling weights. We then introduce a competence gate that estimates domain-level source weights from resolved outcomes, shrinks uncertain estimates toward a global weight, and recalibrates the pooled forecast. Across 2,357 resolved binary questions and five language models, the gate improves the main external baseline from 0.0771 to 0.0732 Brier and significantly outperforms global forecast combinations. The gain remains significant under leakage controls and against a leakage-safe time-series prior on the pooled structured set, with separate evidence on FRED. In contrast, the gate gives no significant improvement on the official ForecastBench market subset, where it largely defers to the market. Across four Qwen models, verbal confidence does not reliably identify when the model outperforms the external forecast, while outcome-estimated competence supports better abstention decisions. These results provide a practical approach for selective model use based on measured marginal value.

</details>


### [151] [Language Is an Insufficient Substrate for Quantitative Reasoning, and Consequential Domains Need Large Quantitative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12105)
*Reuben Vandeventer,David Imrem,David J. Wild*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"大型定量模型"(LQM)这一新模型类别，论证以语言描述为训练基底无法胜任高风险定量决策：描述是定量记录的有损编码且损失不可逆，与模型规模无关。作者指明这类场景还要求可复现性、输出到源记录的血缘追溯、以及校准的不确定性，这三项属性是语言基底结构上无法提供的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应用机器学习领域普遍假设，定价风险、资本配置、患者分诊、网络入侵遏制等关键定量决策的进步将随大语言模型(LLM)的发展而自然实现。作者质疑这一前提，认为进步的瓶颈不在模型能力，而在训练所依赖的表示本身。

Method: 以理论论证为主，不涉及实验。核心手段是将问题形式化为训练表示的性质，而非模型容量的性质：(1) 语言模型训练于由人类描述生成的世界表示，而描述是定量记录的有损编码，且该损失不可逆——任何下游模型在任何规模下都无法从描述中恢复其未编码的信息；(2) 进一步识别出关键场景对模型的三项结构性要求：可复现性、从每个输出回溯到产生它的源记录的血缘关系、校准的不确定性，并论证语言基底无法在构造上满足这些要求。

Result: 论证得到两个结论性判断：定量记录的信息损失是表示层面的、不可逆的，无法通过扩大模型规模弥补；可复现性、血缘追溯与校准不确定性是语言基底在构造上无法提供的属性。据此，作者认为这些属性共同定义了一个独立的模型类别，即大型定量模型(LQM)。

Conclusion: 关键定量决策的进展不应寄望于LLM的自然外溢，而需要一类以定量记录而非语言描述为基底的独立模型类别(LQM)。该类别以可复现性、源记录血缘和校准不确定性为核心约束，其可行性取决于表示层面而非模型规模。

Abstract: The prevailing assumption in applied machine learning is that progress on consequential quantitative decisions such as pricing risk, allocating capital, triaging patients, or containing a network intrusion will follow from progress in large language models (LLMs). A language model is trained on a representation of the world that was produced by human description; description is a lossy encoding of the quantitative record, and the loss is irreversible: no downstream model, at any scale, can recover from a description what the description did not encode. We formalize this as a property of the representation on which a model is trained rather than of the model capacity, and we identify three further properties that consequential settings demand of a model and that a language substrate cannot supply by construction: reproducibility, lineage from every output back to the source records that produced. it, and calibrated uncertainty. We argue that these properties define a distinct model class, which we call the Large Quantitative Model (LQM).

</details>


### [152] [DU-NO: A Parameter-Efficient Double U-Shaped Neural Operator for Phase-Resolving Wave Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12115)
*Enrique Hernandez Noguera,Md Meftahul Ferdaus,Nathan Cooper,Elias Ioup,Mahdi Abdelguerfi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出DU-NO（双U形神经算子），一种多尺度U形谱算子，仅在最浅的两层编码器和解码器层级附加轻量级卷积U-Net分支。模型仅有3.64M参数，比U-FNO少一个数量级，但在FUNWAVE-TVD基准上取得最佳自回归误差，较U-FNO提升14.9%，参数少10.8倍，并在频率各波段（含高波数）均保持优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相分辨波动模型（如FUNWAVE-TVD）是近岸动力学的精度标准，能解析单个波的浅化、折射和破碎，但成本过高，无法满足业务预报所需的集合模拟、不确定性量化和实时预警。神经算子能以低成本达到求解器级精度，但在波主导场中，精确模型规模过大（如U-FNO需数千万参数），因此需要一种既准确又轻量、尤其能保真高波数内容的算子架构。

Method: DU-NO是一种多尺度U形谱神经算子，仅在其最浅的两层编码器和解码器层级附加轻量卷积U-Net分支：高波数内容只存在于细网格上，因此局部全频道路径被安排在含有该内容的层级，而粗粒度的带限层级保持纯谱运算。此外采用深度衰减模模式调度，将参数量控制在3.64M，比U-FNO少一个数量级。

Result: 在公开的FUNWAVE-TVD基准上，DU-NO在六个同等训练架构中取得最佳自回归展开误差，较U-FNO提升14.9%且参数量少10.8倍；频率带分析显示优势覆盖所有波段，包括截断谱算子失效的高波数带。参数匹配对照表明增益源于架构：在相同3.6M预算下，最佳基线仍落后DU-NO 28.6%。该优势还延伸至近岸波之外：在二维Navier-Stokes上匹配最强基线，在PDEBench浅水展开上明显胜出。

Conclusion: DU-NO通过将局部全带卷积路径置于高波数内容所在的浅层、保持深层纯谱，以远小于U-FNO的参数数量实现了更优的波场预测精度，且增益可归因于架构而非参数规模，并具备跨任务的泛化能力。代码、训练模型和评估工件已公开。

Abstract: Phase-resolving wave models such as FUNWAVE-TVD are the accuracy standard for nearshore dynamics, resolving the shoaling, refraction, and breaking of individual waves, but their cost rules them out for the ensembles, uncertainty quantification, and real-time warning that operational forecasting demands. Neural operators promise solver-level accuracy at a fraction of that cost, yet on wave-dominated fields the accurate ones are large: hybrid spectral-convolutional operators such as U-FNO (the strongest baseline in our study after DU-NO) buy their fidelity with tens of millions of parameters. We introduce DU-NO (Double U-shaped Neural Operator), a multiscale U-shaped spectral operator that attaches lightweight convolutional U-Net branches only at its two shallowest encoder and decoder levels. The placement follows a sampling argument: high-wavenumber content exists only on fine grids, so the local, full-band pathways go where that content lives, while the coarse, band-limited levels stay purely spectral. A depth-decaying mode schedule holds the model to 3.64M parameters, an order of magnitude below U-FNO. On our publicly released FUNWAVE-TVD benchmark, DU-NO attains the best autoregressive rollout error of six identically trained architectures, improving on U-FNO by 14.9% with 10.8x fewer parameters, and a frequency-band analysis shows the gain holds across all bands, including the high-wavenumber band where truncated-spectral operators collapse. Parameter-matched controls confirm the gain is architectural: rescaled to the same 3.6M budget, the best baseline still trails DU-NO by 28.6%. The advantage carries beyond nearshore waves: DU-NO matches the strongest baselines on 2D Navier-Stokes and wins clearly on PDEBench shallow-water rollouts. Code, trained models, and evaluation artifacts are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/duno-code-5A7B/.

</details>


### [153] [When Successful Knowledge Graph Edits Displace Correct Answers: Rank-Level Locality beyond Parameter Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12116)
*Yi-Cheng Lai,Jerry Wang,Hsin-Ling Hsu,Li-Chu Chi,Ya-Wen Teng,Hen-Hsen Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种跨三种范围的“排名位移审计”方法，用于衡量知识图谱嵌入编辑中目标提升对正确答案排名的损害，并推导保持分数不变的维度与几何条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有局部性测试仅关注复用被编辑参数的事实，忽略了编辑会把正确答案挤出返回列表的排名效应，因此无法真实评估编辑的副作用。

Method: 定义覆盖三种范围的公共排名位移审计：被编辑参数支持的事实、目标查询的其他正确答案、同一关系中其他查询的正确答案；同时推导使更新提升目标并严格保持选定分数的维度与几何条件。

Result: 在FB15k-237上（DistMult、ComplEx），直接提升总能把目标移入前十，但仅23.0--23.2%的编辑无损害；严格保持成功仅1.3--1.4%且无测得损害；支持正则化实体编辑联合成功率最高36.3--37.7%，排名截断保持达32.8--34.7%，并将平均被挤出答案数从约14降至1.2。

Conclusion: 局部性同时取决于保护范围与编辑机制；KGE编辑应同时报告纠正成功率与排名位移的发生率和严重程度。

Abstract: Editing a knowledge graph embedding (KGE) model to promote a desired answer can displace correct answers from the returned list. Locality tests based only on facts that reuse the edited parameter can miss this ranking effect. We introduce a common rank-displacement audit at three scopes: facts supported by the edited parameter, other correct answers to the target query, and correct answers across queries with the same relation. We also derive dimensional and geometric conditions for an update to improve the target while exactly preserving selected scores. On FB15k-237 with DistMult and ComplEx, direct promotion always moves the target into the top ten, but does so without damage in only 23.0--23.2\% of edits. Strict preservation causes no measured damage, yet succeeds in only 1.3--1.4\%. Support-regularized entity editing gives the highest joint success, 36.3--37.7\%, while rank-truncated preservation reaches 32.8--34.7\% and reduces the mean number of displaced answers from about 14 to 1.2. Experiments across dimensions, scorers, ranking conventions, and a learned editor show that locality depends on both the protected scope and the editing mechanism. KGE editing should therefore report correction success together with the incidence and severity of rank displacement.

</details>


### [154] [Mined from Scientific Literature: Process Schemas for Atomic Layer Deposition and Etching in Materials Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12139)
*Sameer Sadruddin,Eleni Poupaki,Alex Watkins,Bora Karasulu,Adriaan J. M. Mackus,Erwin Kessels,Sören Auer,Jennifer D'Souza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出四个经领域专家评审的JSON Schema，用于标准化描述ALD与ALE的实验和仿真过程，以提升文献可比性与机器可复用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALD与ALE的实验和仿真文献在材料科学领域呈现高度异质性，缺乏统一描述，导致结果难以比较、数据无法被机器自动复用。

Method: 使用schema-miner并以QUDT本体为基础（schema-miner pro）构建四个JSON Schema，覆盖ALD/ALE的实验与仿真过程，结构化材料、工艺条件、配置以及测量或预测结果；随后比较其范围、结构与语义基础，并通过ORKG模板验证schema引导的文献抽取与结构化记录发布。

Result: 获得了四个经领域专家评审的JSON Schema，并展示了其在模式引导的文献信息抽取与结构化记录发布方面的可行性，同时对比了各schema的范围、结构与语义 grounding 差异。

Conclusion: 标准化的ALD/ALE JSON Schema可促进该领域文献数据的互操作、比较和机器可操作复用，并支持基于ORKG的结构化知识发布。

Abstract: Atomic layer deposition (ALD) and atomic layer etching (ALE) are reported heterogeneously across experimental and simulation literature in materials science, hindering comparison and machine-actionable reuse. We present four domain-expert-reviewed JSON Schemas for ALD and ALE experimental and simulation processes. Curated with schema-miner and grounded in QUDT using schema-miner pro, the schemas structure materials, process conditions, configurations , and measured or predicted results. We compare their scope, structure, and semantic grounding, and demonstrate their use for schema-guided literature extraction and publication of structured records through ORKG templates.

</details>


### [155] [Can LLMs in Draft-Verify-Revise Pipelines Resolve Deictic Ambiguity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12162)
*Obinna I. Ekekezie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究草稿-验证-修订（draft-verify-revise）流水线中的"指示性转移"（deictic shift）现象：同一指代表达（如"previous"）在不同阶段LLM间含义漂移，导致裁决错误；通过合成数据集与多模型、多推理强度实验，发现推理强度可极大影响平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 草稿-验证-修订是扩展推理期算力的常见LLM编排模式，但上下文在阶段间级联传递时，不同阶段的LLM可能对"previous"等依赖上下文的表达作出不同解读，产生指示性转移，从而使验证/修订阶段判断失准。作者希望量化这一现象及其对流水线正确性的影响。

Method: 构建合成数据集：10个基础样例，每个渲染为三种条件；在保持共享成分不变的前提下，变化三处因素——草稿阶段LLM（assistant）或验证阶段LLM（grader）是否正确解析该表达，以及修订阶段LLM（meta-evaluator）判定正确读法所需的独立推理量。测试3家提供商的6个模型、21种推理强度配置，采用e-values进行序贯检验；设置主实验与消融实验（移除grader反馈中的错误分类标签）。另用独立LLM分析meta-evaluator每次错误裁决所述理由。

Result: 平衡准确率（敏感度与特异度的非加权平均）从低于随机的0.156到接近完美不等。GPT-5.2从无推理时的0.156升至最高推理强度下的0.942；Gemini 3 Pro在所有推理强度下均高于0.94。Gemini 3 Pro低推理强度即超过GPT-5.2的xhigh强度，而每次试验成本约为后者的5%。meta-evaluator出错时更依赖表面线索而非操作性推理。

Conclusion: 指示性转移是草稿-验证-修订流水线的实质风险，推理强度对判定准确率影响显著且模型间差异大。实施该模式的上下文工程师应警惕指示性转移，并在每个阶段显式指明预期指代对象。

Abstract: Draft-verify-revise is a common LLM orchestration pattern for scaling inference-time compute. One LLM drafts, a second critiques the draft and provides feedback, and a third uses that feedback to revise the draft into the final output. As context cascades between stages, LLMs at different stages can resolve a context-dependent expression such as "previous" differently. When that happens, the expression undergoes a deictic shift, a change in what it refers to. This phenomenon was studied with a synthetic dataset of 10 base examples, each rendered in three conditions. Holding the shared components constant, the conditions varied whether the draft stage LLM (the assistant) or the verify stage LLM (the grader) resolved the expression correctly, and how much independent reasoning the revise stage LLM (the meta-evaluator) needed to determine which reading was correct. Six models from three providers were tested across 21 reasoning effort configurations using e-values for sequential testing, in a primary experiment and an ablation experiment that removed error classification labels from the grader's feedback. A separate LLM analyzed the meta-evaluator's stated rationale for each wrong verdict. Balanced accuracy (the unweighted mean of sensitivity and specificity) ranged from 0.156, below chance, to near-perfect. GPT-5.2 rose from 0.156 without reasoning to 0.942 at its highest reasoning effort level, while Gemini 3 Pro stayed above 0.94 at every level. Gemini 3 Pro at low reasoning effort outscored GPT-5.2 at xhigh reasoning effort for roughly 5% of the cost per trial. When the meta-evaluator erred, it tended to rely on surface cues rather than operational reasoning. Context engineers implementing draft-verify-revise pipelines should be wary of deictic shifts and make the intended referent explicit at each stage.

</details>


### [156] [GLARE: Generative Learning via Adversarial Reward Estimation For Social Dynamics Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12165)
*Tenghao Huang,Zhaoxuan Tan,Muhao Chen,Jonathan May,Mengting Wan,Longqi Yang,Pei Zhou,Sihao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出会议动态预测基准 MDFB 与对抗模仿学习方法 GLARE，用于评估和提升模型在多方会议中生成多轮延续的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 会议延续需要跟踪议程、发言角色、参与者意图及分歧，现有方法难以在长时多方讨论中同时保证任务推进与自然对话流。

Method: 构建 MDFB 基准（2,207 场真实会议、24,794 条面向未来的查询），给定转录前缀与活跃问题，模型单次调用生成多轮延续；提出 GLARE，将对抗模仿学习适配到条件语言生成，判别器对真实延续打分高于当前 actor 样本，其分数作为 KL 正则化策略奖励，并用当前策略负样本迭代重训使奖励景观随 actor 演化。

Result: GLARE 在人类评估中效用胜率平均 0.66、拟人度胜率 0.70，优于 SFT 与 SPIN，但仍低于真实人类延续。MDFB 亦可作为社交推理竞技场，通过参考辅助评判比较包括闭源系统在内的通用模型。

Conclusion: MDFB 同时支持任务特定学习与基于输出的会议行为评估，GLARE 验证了对抗模仿学习在会议生成任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Meeting continuation requires tracking the agenda, speaker roles, participant intentions, and disagreement across long multi-party discussions. We introduce the Meeting Dynamic Forecasting Benchmark (MDFB), constructed from 2,207 real-world meetings and 24,794 future-facing queries. Given a transcript prefix and an active question, a model generates a plausible multi-turn continuation in one call. We evaluate utility---progress toward the question---and human-likeness---plausible conversational flow and role consistency---without requiring exact reproduction of the observed future. We further present GLARE, an adaptation of adversarial imitation learning to conditional language generation. A discriminator ranks the observed continuation above samples from the current actor, and its score supplies a KL-regularized policy reward; retraining on current-policy negatives allows the reward landscape to evolve with the actor. GLARE attains average human-evaluated win rates of 0.66 on utility and 0.70 on human-likeness, outperforming SFT and SPIN while remaining below the observed human continuation. We also demonstrate MDFB as a social reasoning arena for comparing general-purpose models, including closed-source systems, through reference-assisted judgments. Together, these studies illustrate the benchmark's use for both task-specific learning and output-based evaluation of meeting behavior.

</details>


### [157] [WinSyn: An Automated Pipeline for Realistic Enterprise Question-Answering Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12171)
*Amey Varhade,Ananya Sutradhar,Ravishankar Krishnaswamy,Navin Goyal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一套自动化流水线，生成模拟真实职场场景的企业邮件合成数据集，包含长/短问答及有据可依的黄金答案，用以评测企业级深度研究智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业环境对问答智能体极具挑战：信息分散在不断演进且可能相互冲突的邮件、聊天、文档等工件中。现有基准缺乏真实世界复杂度，多为短答案与非自然提问，难以反映企业场景的难点，因此需要更贴近真实、高复杂度的评测数据。

Method: 构建自动化流水线生成合成数据集：模拟持续数月、涉及多达25名不同角色员工交互的长期企业项目，生成强调歧义性、信息分布式存储的邮件数据，并配套自然产生的长式与短式问题及基于数据的有据黄金答案。

Result: 在自建数据集上，使用最新前沿模型对若干标准智能体基线进行评测，各数据集在所有查询上的平均聚合得分均低于80%，表明仍有显著提升空间。

Conclusion: 企业部署仍需大量后续工作；真实、高复杂度的评测数据对开发更强的真实世界企业深度研究系统至关重要。

Abstract: Enterprise settings provide a challenging environment for question-answering agents, which often rely on Retrieval-Augmented Generation, Deep Research (DR), and related techniques. Much of this challenge comes from the complexity of enterprise data: information is often spread across evolving and potentially conflict- ing emails, chat messages, documents, and other artifacts. Existing benchmarks typically have limited real-world complexity, short-form responses, and unnatural queries, so they often fail to capture the challenges of enterprise settings. In this work, we introduce an automated pipeline for generating synthetic datasets of emails reflecting realistic workplace scenarios, along with long- and short-form questions and gold answers grounded in the data. Our method simulates long-running enterprise projects spanning several months and involving up to 25 interacting employees across multiple roles. The data emphasizes ambiguity, distributed information, and naturally occurring queries. To validate the pipeline, we evaluate few standard agentic baselines on our datasets using the latest frontier models. We find that aggregate scores averaged over all queries remain below 80% for each dataset, indicating significant room for improvement. These findings suggest that more work remains to be done for enterprise deployment and underscore the importance of realistic, high-complexity evaluation data for developing stronger real-world enterprise DR systems.

</details>


### [158] [Soft Symbol Grounding for Prototypical Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12247)
*Marcos Galván-López,Nijesh Upreti,Hiram Calvo,Carlos Aguilar-Ibáñez,Vaishak Belle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Soft-PNet，将概念接地重构为在预计算可行符号解缓存上的 Metropolis 游走，并用单一 KL 目标替代任务特定的可微损失，在多个任务上以更少监督和训练时间达到与损失工程化原型网络相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经符号模型通常只在最终标签上接受监督，中间概念不可观测。由于同一标签可对应多种概念赋值，模型可能预测出正确标签却恢复错误概念，即推理捷径（reasoning shortcut）。原型网络通过将概念锚定到少量标注示例来缓解该问题，但现有方法仍以手工设计、任务特定的可微损失耦合感知与推理，每换一个任务就需重新设计损失。

Method: Soft-PNet 去除该可微损失，把概念接地重新表述为对预计算可行符号解缓存的 Metropolis 游走，由每个概念单个标注锚点构建的原型分布引导，并采用一个 KL 目标在原型加权缓存与网络概念预测之间进行训练。该目标跨任务一致，且在解空间无法枚举时仍然适用。

Result: 在 MNIST-EvenOdd、Visual Sudoku 以及稀缺监督下的 Kand-Logic 上，Soft-PNet 在概念层与标签层均达到损失工程化原型网络的水平，并能恢复软接地基线遗漏的概念，同时无需损失工程且训练时间更短。

Conclusion: Soft-PNet 通过用统一的 Metropolis 游走加 KL 目标替代任务特定可微损失，实现了跨任务一致、无需损失工程的概念接地，在保持性能的同时提升了对推理捷径的抑制能力并降低了训练成本。

Abstract: Neuro-symbolic models are usually trained with supervision only on final labels, leaving the intermediate concepts unobserved. Since many concept assignments are consistent with a given label, training can predict labels correctly while recovering the wrong concepts, a failure known as a reasoning shortcut. Prototypical networks reduce shortcuts by anchoring each concept to a few labeled examples, but existing methods still couple perception and reasoning through a hand-crafted, task-specific differentiable loss that must be redesigned for every task. We introduce \textbf{Soft-PNet}, which removes this loss: it reframes concept grounding as a Metropolis walk over a precomputed cache of feasible symbolic solutions, guided by a prototype distribution built from a single labeled anchor per concept, and trains against one KL objective between the prototype-weighted cache and the network's concept predictions. The objective is identical across tasks and remains applicable when the solution space cannot be enumerated. On \texttt{MNIST-EvenOdd}, Visual Sudoku, and \texttt{Kand-Logic} under scarce supervision, Soft-PNet matches loss-engineered prototypical networks at the concept and label levels and recovers concepts that soft-grounding baselines miss, with no loss engineering and lower training time.

</details>


### [159] [GTA: Graph Theory Agent and Benchmark for Algorithmic Graph Reasoning with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12265)
*Zixiang Xu,Yanbo Wang,Chenxi Wang,Lang Gao,Zirui Song,Yue Huang,Zhaorun Chen,Xiangliang Zhang,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出GT Bench基准与Graph Theory Agent (GTA)框架，系统评估LLM在图结构数据上的多步推理能力，并通过表示选择与分解式规划显著提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM图推理评估存在三大局限：任务简单、图规模小；评分侧重代码生成而非图本身的推理；固定单一输入格式。因此无法明确LLM能否可靠地用自然语言执行多步图算法。

Method: 1) 构建GT Bench：覆盖24类经典图问题、44种任务-结构设置、超10万样本，含自然语言、结构化语言、邻接表、邻接矩阵四种表示。2) 对8个LLM进行评测，分析表示、图密度、规模、拓扑与模型间的敏感性。3) 提出GTA：偏好训练的表示选择器 + plan-and-decompose脚手架 + 冻结的执行器LLM。

Result: 准确率与输入表示强相关；最优表示随图密度、规模、拓扑及模型变化；该敏感性在最强推理模型中依然存在但有所减弱。GTA将Phi-4在easy split上从53.5%提升至69.1%，hard split上从33.0%提升至41.5%，优于8个提示与智能体基线，并可无重训练迁移至GraCoRe与NLGraph。

Conclusion: LLM图算法推理对输入表示高度敏感，表示选择是提升性能的关键因素；结合表示选择与规划分解的GTA能有效且可迁移地增强冻结LLM的图推理能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly asked to reason over structured data such as graphs, yet how reliably they can carry out multi-step graph algorithms in language remains unclear. Existing evaluations tend to use simple tasks on small graphs, to score code generation rather than reasoning over the graph itself, or to fix a single input format. We introduce Graph Theory Bench (GT Bench), a benchmark covering 24 classical graph problems in 44 task-structure settings, with over 100,000 examples across four representations: natural language, structured language, adjacency list, and adjacency matrix. Evaluating eight LLMs on GT Bench shows that accuracy is strongly tied to the input representation, that the best representation shifts with graph density, size, and topology as well as with the model, and that this sensitivity persists, attenuated, in the strongest reasoning models. Building on these observations, we propose the Graph Theory Agent (GTA), which pairs a preference-trained representation selector with plan-and-decompose scaffolding around a frozen executor LLM. GTA lifts Phi-4 from 53.5% to 69.1% on the benchmark's easy split and from 33.0% to 41.5% on its hard split, outperforming eight prompting and agent baselines, and transfers without retraining to GraCoRe and NLGraph. Code for benchmark generation and evaluation: https://github.com/xzx34/GTA. The project homepage is available at https://xzx34.github.io/gta/.

</details>


### [160] [Learning Symbolic Constraint Representations from Examples: A Neuro-Symbolic Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12267)
*Nassim Belmecheri,Arnaud Gotlieb,Nadjib Lazaar,Helge Spieker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个神经符号框架用于自动约束获取（CA），通过神经Oracle Transformer模型模拟用户响应并泛化概念知识，减少对人类oracle的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有约束获取方法通常依赖与人类oracle的大量交互，导致学习过程在时间和查询数量上成本高昂。

Method: 引入神经Oracle Transformer模型来模拟用户响应并泛化概念知识；将训练好的oracle与专用CA引擎FastCA交互，系统地将oracle的响应精炼为可靠、一致且可解释的约束网络。

Result: 实验结果表明，这种神经符号交互能有效将数据驱动的模式识别与符号推理结合，在无需先验领域知识的情况下从数据中恢复结构化符号模型。

Conclusion: 该神经符号框架为组合领域中的模型构建自动化提供了一种稳健方法，能显著减少用户参与并自动化CA过程。

Abstract: Learning user-defined concepts as constraint networks has been extensively studied in the constraint acquisition (CA) literature. However, existing approaches typically rely on intensive interactions with a human oracle, making the learning process costly in terms of time and number of queries. In this paper, we propose a neuro-symbolic framework for automatic CA that significantly reduces user involvement by introducing neural Oracle Transformer models which learn to emulate user responses and to generalize conceptual knowledge. Trained on previously available examples, the learned oracle interacts with a dedicated CA engine, FastCA, which systematically refines the oracle's responses into a sound, consistent, and interpretable constraint network. This neuro-symbolic interaction enables the recovery of structured symbolic models from data without prior domain knowledge. Our results demonstrate that this neuro-symbolic interplay effectively aligns data-driven pattern recognition with symbolic reasoning, offering a robust approach to automating model construction in combinatorial domains.

</details>


### [161] [Robust Prototypical Networks for Few-Shot Sensor Fault Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12287)
*Mohammed Ayalew Belay,Amirshayan Haghipour,Pierluigi Salvo Rossi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多回合原型网络（MEPN），通过对多个不相交支持回合的原型取均值来稳定类别原型，在极低样本（one-shot）工业故障诊断中显著优于单回合基线；在同等10样本预算下与ProtoNet(K=10)无统计差异，证明增益源于原型累积而非固定预算下的学习能力提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业故障诊断通常仅有少量带标签的故障样本，few-shot learning因此适用于传感器监测。标准原型网络简单有效，但在极低样本（very-low-shot）场景下，由于每次决策仅依赖很小的支持集，其类别原型可能不稳定，导致诊断性能下降。

Method: 提出多回合原型网络（MEPN）：从多个不相交的支持回合中分别计算原型，并以其均值作为最终类别代表，从而在不改变编码器结构的前提下降低原型方差。在DeFACTO传感器数据集上，将合成的偏置、漂移、尖峰和噪声故障注入真实工业测量数据，开展五分类故障诊断评估（100次独立运行）。

Result: 在逐回合one-shot设置（K=1 shot，聚合N_agg=10个支持回合）下，MEPN达到\SensorOneShotGcpn\%，显著高于单回合基线。在相同的10样本支持预算下，MEPN与K=10的ProtoNet统计上不可区分。

Conclusion: MEPN通过跨多个支持回合的原型累积有效缓解了极低样本下的原型不稳定问题，提升了one-shot工业故障诊断性能；同时其优势机制被确认为原型累积，而非固定样本预算下学习能力的提升。

Abstract: Industrial fault diagnosis often operates with only a handful of labeled fault examples, making few-shot learning attractive for sensor monitoring. Standard prototypical networks are simple and effective; however, their class prototypes may become unstable in the very-low-shot regime because each decision relies on a small support set. We propose \emph{Multi-Episode Prototypical Networks} (MEPN), which aggregate prototypes from multiple disjoint support episodes and use their mean as the final class representative, reducing prototype variance without changing the encoder architecture. We evaluate MEPN on the DeFACTO sensor dataset using five-way fault classification with synthetic bias, drift, spike, and noise faults injected into real industrial measurements. Over 100 independent runs, MEPN reaches \textbf{\SensorOneShotGcpn\%} in the per-episode one-shot setting ($K\!=\!1$ shot, aggregated over $N_{\text{agg}}\!=\!10$ support episodes), substantially above single-episode baselines. Under an equal 10-sample support budget, MEPN and ProtoNet at $K\!=\!10$ are statistically indistinguishable, confirming prototype accumulation as the mechanism rather than superior fixed-budget learning.

</details>


### [162] [Hybrid Physics-AI Framework of Body Center of Mass Dynamics from Wrist-Worn Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12304)
*Shuhao Que,Valentina Breschi,Ying Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出简化的运动学模型(KM)及三种混合AI建模方法(HKM-NN)，利用手腕IMU估计全身质心(COM)加速度，在步态与坐-站活动中验证了可行性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手腕IMU广泛用于日常健康监测，但无法充分表征全身动力学，而全身质心(COM)被视为生理参考标准；因此需要从手腕IMU映射到COM加速度的方法。

Method: 构建基于若干简化假设、可仅凭手腕IMU求解动力学方程的简化运动学模型(KM)；并在此基础上提出三类混合AI建模方法(HKM-NN)：串行学习(ser-)与两种同步学习(sim1-、sim2-)，融合灰箱与黑箱建模。使用10名健康受试者在六种步态活动与坐-站过渡活动中的手腕IMU与真实COM测量数据进行训练与测试。

Result: KM模型误差为步态活动6.7%–12.5%、坐-站5.6%；HKM-NN显著提升性能，步态误差5.3%–9.3%，坐-站最佳误差3.9%。噪声测试中，sim1-/sim2-在高斯扰动下鲁棒性更强，而KM在椒盐噪声下鲁棒性相对较强。

Conclusion: 从手腕IMU估计COM加速度具有可行性；将生物力学结构与数据驱动学习相结合，对在不完美、含噪测量条件下运行的穿戴式传感应用具有重要意义。

Abstract: Wrist-worn IMU has been widely used for daily-life health monitoring. Yet, it does not fully represent whole-body dynamics, for which the body center of mass (COM) is considered the physiological reference standard. Therefore, this work proposes a simplified kinematic model (KM), which is designed to map the wrist IMU to the COM acceleration. It is built upon several reductive assumptions that enable the solvability of the dynamic equations based on wrist IMU measurements alone. This work further proposes three types of hybrid AI modeling methods, namely human kinematic model-based neural network (HKM-NN) models, to leverage the power of both grey-box and black-box modeling. The HKM-NN methods include serial learning (ser-) and two approaches of simultaneous learning (sim1- and sim2-). The proposed models are trained and tested using our dataset, which includes wrist IMU measurements and ground-truth COM measurements from 10 healthy volunteers during six gait activities and sit-to-stand (SS) transitional movement. The results demonstrate the feasibility of estimating COM acceleration from wrist IMU measurements. Our KM model yields satisfactory results, with an error ranging from 6.7% to 12.5% for gait activities and 5.6% for the SS. In comparison with the KM model, our HKM-NN models significantly enhance the performance, achieving 5.3% to 9.3% errors for gait activities, and the best error of 3.9% for the SS. In addition, the HKM-NN models demonstrate distinct robustness characteristics under noisy test conditions, with sim1-/sim2- generally maintaining greater robustness under Gaussian perturbations, while the KM model exhibits comparatively strong robustness under salt-and-pepper noise. These findings highlight the importance of combining biomechanical structure with data-driven learning for wearable sensing applications operating under imperfect and noisy measurement conditions.

</details>


### [163] [Do Influence-Derived Data Perturbations Enable Machine Unlearning? A Controlled Study of Three Plausible Roles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12313)
*Chenkai Wu,Chrispine Kambimbi,Qinyang Zeng,Jun Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对深度扰动学习（DPL）在机器遗忘中的三种角色进行独立验证，发现其公开实现存在两处正确性缺陷；修正后 DPL 在直接删除准则上失败，效用不稳定，且计入方向计算开销后不如简单热启动基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作将 DPL 定位为直接删除信号、保效用正则项与对抗遗忘热启动三种角色，并用较弱角色的证据支撑最强主张；本文旨在按角色分别、在匹配协议下检验这些主张是否成立。

Method: 在匹配协议与精确种子重训练基线下分别测试三种角色；审计公开实现，发现图像扰动方向在增广、归一化张量上计算却施加于原始图像，且标签扰动小于 float32 分辨率导致标签实际未变；修正图像扰动流程后在 CIFAR-10/ResNet-18 三个配对种子上评估，并做单种子 Tiny ImageNet 检验。

Result: 修正后 DPL 在三个配对种子上均未通过直接删除准则；效用效果符号跨种子不一致；计入方向计算时间后劣于简单热启动基线；单种子 Tiny ImageNet 结果同样不支持其作为正则项或热启动，且发布代码的预处理不一致使该处直接比较结论不确定。

Conclusion: DPL 的强删除主张缺乏证据支持，较弱角色的证据不足以支撑强主张；结论仅覆盖随机实例删除场景，不排除其他删除机制下的影响函数方法。作者发布角色匹配评估协议与扰动式删除主张的审计清单。

Abstract: We evaluate Deep Perturbation Learning (DPL), which perturbs training images and labels along influence-derived directions, in three roles in which prior work has positioned it for machine unlearning: a direct deletion signal (the strongest claim), a utility-preserving regularizer, and a warm start for adversarial unlearning. Evidence for the weaker roles has been used to support the stronger one, so we test each role separately under a matched protocol with exact-seed retraining baselines. An audit of the public implementation identifies two correctness issues: image directions are computed on augmented, normalized tensors but applied to raw images, and the label perturbation falls below float32 resolution, leaving labels unchanged. After correcting the image-perturbation pipeline, DPL fails the direct-deletion criterion on CIFAR-10/ResNet-18 in all three paired seeds. Its utility effects are inconsistent in sign across seeds, and once direction-computation time is counted it underperforms simple warm-start baselines. A one-seed Tiny ImageNet check likewise does not favor DPL as a regularizer or warm start; preprocessing inconsistencies in the released code make the direct comparison there inconclusive. These results cover random instance deletion only and do not rule out influence-based methods in other deletion regimes. We release a role-matched evaluation protocol and an audit checklist for perturbation-based deletion claims.

</details>


### [164] [AIM: A Privacy-Aware Interoperable Memory Framework for Multi-Agent Multi-User LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12320)
*Zachary Johnson,Nigel Boachie Kumankumah,Somya Chatterjee,Tejas Sathyamurthi,Min Chen,Xinyi Alice Li,Xiao Wang,Emily Morgan Gelchie,Jessica Lin,Sadid A. Hasan,Sulaiman Vesal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AIM（Agentic Interoperable Memory）多智能体、多用户记忆框架，实现私有/共享记忆的持久管理与索引级访问控制，并发布首个多用户记忆基准MUMBench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM局限于单用户会话，无法跨时间学习用户偏好；现有智能体记忆系统多停留在单用户层面，公共知识无法跨用户共享以提升下游响应。因此需要一种兼顾隐私与共享的多用户记忆框架及相应评测基准。

Method: AIM动态将信息分类为私有（仅属主可访问）或公开（所有用户可访问），并通过索引级访问控制确保私有记忆仅能被其所有者检索，从而在保护敏感数据的同时共享有益知识以提升协调性与一致性；同时构建MUMBench多用户记忆基准，覆盖四个领域的多用户交互数据，包含私有与可共享信息，用于评测检索、创建、更新、删除等多种记忆操作。

Result: 在MUMBench上三次独立运行中，AIM取得96.0%的可见性分类准确率、58.8%的严格操作准确率和70.5%的状态感知操作准确率。

Conclusion: AIM为多智能体、多用户LLM系统提供了隐私感知的统一记忆管理方案，MUMBench作为首个公开的多用户记忆评测基准，可支持多类记忆操作的系统评估，为后续研究提供基础。

Abstract: Traditional large language models (LLMs) are scoped to individual user sessions, limiting their knowledge to a single conversation and preventing them from learning user preferences that evolve over time. Existing agentic memory systems address this limitation but generally operate at the individual-user level, restricting the public knowledge that could be shared across users to improve downstream responses. We introduce AIM (Agentic Interoperable Memory), a unified, privacy-aware memory framework that enables multi-agent, multi-user LLM systems to persistently manage private and shared memory. AIM dynamically classifies information as private, scoped to one user and inaccessible to others, or public, accessible to all users. It enforces index-level access controls so that private memories are retrievable only by their owner, protecting sensitive data while allowing beneficial shared knowledge to improve coordination and consistency. We also introduce MUMBench (Multi-User Memory Benchmark), a dataset of multi-user interactions containing private and shareable information across four domains. To our knowledge, MUMBench is the first public dataset designed to evaluate multiple memory operations, including retrieval, creation, update, and deletion, in a multi-user environment. Across three independent runs on MUMBench, AIM achieves 96.0% visibility classification accuracy, 58.8% strict operation accuracy, and 70.5% state-aware operation accuracy.

</details>


### [165] [Affective Agent: On-Device Personalized Intervention Reasoning for Wearable Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12322)
*Reina Mun,Zishen Wan,Vijay Janapa Reddi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Affective Agent——一种面向可穿戴级硬件的三层参考架构，用于在不确定性下进行个性化的干预推理，无需云端依赖与逐用户重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感计算已推进可穿戴状态推断，但在设备端推理"是否、何时、如何"干预仍是难题，且现有方案多依赖云端或逐用户重训练，难以在可穿戴级硬件上实现闭环个性化干预。

Method: 架构由感知、个性化、推理三层交互组成，将亚十亿参数的紧凑语言模型与生理证据、上下文及用户历史结合；通过宿主管理的结构化记忆演化实现个性化，而非更新逐用户权重。以室内环境质量控制为实例进行验证。

Result: 在留出的模拟器生成的纵向场景（涵盖生理变化、上下文、信号质量与干预历史）上评估，结果显示记忆驱动的个性化与两遍结构化推理改善了干预决策（属合成评估范畴）。

Conclusion: 将决策层迁移至设备端，为从可穿戴状态推断走向可穿戴级硬件上的闭环、个性化干预提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Affective computing has advanced wearable state inference, but on-device reasoning about whether, when, and how to intervene remains challenging. We present Affective Agent, a three-layer reference architecture for personalized intervention reasoning under uncertainty on wearable-class hardware. It combines a compact sub-billion-parameter language model with physiological evidence, context, and user history to decide whether, when, and how to intervene, without cloud dependency or per-user retraining. The architecture is organized into three interacting layers (perception, personalization, and reasoning), adapting to individual users through host-managed structured memory evolution rather than per-user weight updates. We instantiate Affective Agent in indoor environmental quality control and evaluate it on held-out, simulator-generated longitudinal scenarios spanning physiological variation, context, signal quality, and intervention history. Results show that memory-driven personalization and two-pass structured reasoning improve intervention decisions within this synthetic evaluation. By moving the decision layer on-device, this work demonstrates a path from wearable state inference toward closed-loop, personalized intervention on wearable-class hardware.

</details>


### [166] [LoRA-RC: Reservoir Computing with Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12327)
*Wenbin Wan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LoRA-RC通过低秩在线校正自适应循环矩阵，并在谱范数球投影下保证回声状态与增量ISS稳定性，在Lorenz系统突变漂移下显著优于固定RC与仅读出适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统储备池计算（RC）仅训练线性读出层，训练快、在线预测数据效率高；但静态储备池在系统漂移下性能退化，仅适配读出层不足，而无约束的储备池适配会破坏回声状态特性与增量稳定性，损害RC的可靠性。

Method: 提出LoRA-RC：以流式预测误差驱动，对循环矩阵施加低秩校正；基储备池与适配基离线固定，仅在线更新小型核心矩阵，并在每步将其投影到谱范数球上、进行低通滤波。该投影确保每次应用的循环矩阵落在经认证的收缩集合内，并沿每条在线适配路径建立具有路径无关速率与增益的增量输入到状态稳定性（ISS）界。

Result: 在发生参数突变的Lorenz系统上，LoRA-RC将漂移后的预测误差较固定RC降低56%，较仅读出适配降低51%；20个随机种子的消融实验表明，去除投影会使该误差膨胀40倍以上。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通过受限的低秩在线适配，可在保持RC稳定性理论保证的同时有效应对系统漂移，且稳定性约束（谱范数投影）是性能与可靠性的关键。

Abstract: Reservoir computing (RC) trains only a linear readout over a fixed recurrent layer, making it fast and data-efficient for online prediction. However, a static reservoir degrades under system drift, readout-only adaptation is then insufficient, and unconstrained reservoir adaptation can destroy the echo-state and incremental stability properties that make RC reliable. This paper proposes LoRA-RC, which adapts the recurrent matrix through a low-rank correction driven by streaming prediction errors. The base reservoir and adaptation bases are fixed offline; a small core matrix is adapted online, projected onto a spectral-norm ball, and low-pass filtered at each step. The projection guarantees that every applied recurrent matrix remains within a certified contraction set, and an incremental input-to-state stability bound is established for the reservoir along each online adaptation path, with path-independent rate and gain. On a Lorenz system with an abrupt parameter drift, LoRA-RC cuts post-drift prediction error by 56% versus a fixed RC and 51% versus readout-only adaptation; ablations over 20 seeds show that removing the projection inflates this error by more than a factor of 40.

</details>


### [167] [Toward Robust Personalized Alignment for LLMs: Mitigating Persona Drift in Multi-Turn Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12373)
*Youyuan Zhang,Siyuan Li,Fangming Liu,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文针对个性化语言模型中的"人格漂移"问题提出 CORE 方法，通过不确定性感知的信念修正，区分单轮局部证据与持久人格状态更新，并构建 PERSIST 基准进行评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户画像在长期交互中会演化而非永久固定，模型需在偏好真正改变时修正持久人格状态，同时避免由短暂、模糊或未解决的观察引发误更新。人格漂移是当前个性化语言模型的核心挑战。

Method: 提出 CORE，将轮次局部证据（turn-local evidence）与持久人格状态修正（persistent persona-state revision）分离，并通过不确定性感知的信念修正（uncertainty-aware belief revision）选择性地更新有依据的用户偏好。同时构建 PERSIST 基准，作为锚点后的留出式（held-out post-anchor）测试集，用于评估顺序交互压力下的人格状态鲁棒性，涵盖模糊性、冲突与受控社会影响。

Result: 在 ALOE、PersonaChat 与 PERSIST 上，CORE 提升了个性化对齐与鲁棒性，并在归一化闭槽状态保真度（normalized closed-slot state fidelity）上取得互补增益。人类评估与机制性控制实验进一步表明，其显式更新控制优于单纯增强生成能力或仅依赖持久记忆的方案。

Conclusion: 显式的、基于不确定性的状态更新控制是个性化语言模型缓解人格漂移的关键，仅靠更强的生成或持久记忆不足以实现稳健的人格状态维护。

Abstract: Persona drift remains a central challenge for personalized language models, as user profiles evolve over long interactions rather than remain permanently fixed. Models must therefore revise persistent persona states when preferences genuinely change, while avoiding updates driven by transient, ambiguous, or unresolved observations. We propose CORE, which separates turn-local evidence from persistent persona-state revision and selectively updates grounded user preferences through uncertainty-aware belief revision. We also introduce PERSIST, a held-out post-anchor benchmark for persona-state robustness under sequential interaction stress, covering ambiguity, conflict, and controlled social influence. Across ALOE, PersonaChat, and PERSIST, CORE improves personalized alignment and robustness, with complementary gains in normalized closed-slot state fidelity. Human evaluation and mechanistic controls further support explicit update control beyond stronger generation or persistent memory alone.

</details>


### [168] [BlueLM-GUI Technical Report: A Real-Device-Centric Flywheel for Self-Improving Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12394)
*Tong Ye,Kunyang Han,Guozhi Wang,Longqiang Luo,Zhifeng Ding,Yongxiang Zhang,Xiaolei Shen,Yuxuan Zhang,Zhuping Zhang,Tao Xu,Yue Pan,Yucheng Zhao,Yupei Hu,Yuanjiang Ouyang,Danfeng Shen,Runqi Lin,Hongda Cai,Zhaoxiong Wang,Mengjia Yan,Yingjie Zhong,Chen Zhou,Zeyu Zhang,Xuwen Zhu,Penggang Shi,Mingcheng Luo,Ziyang Wu,Min Jin,Mingfu Shen,Zairong Xu,Fan Zhang,Hao Wang,Liang Liu,Zhulin Xie,Lijun Yao,Xiao Liang,Liangmin Wen,Liqiang Feng,Feilong Wu,Min Hu,Min Chen,Guanjing Xiong,Xiaohu Ruan,Xiaoxin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 BlueLM-GUI（35B-A3B 移动 GUI 智能体），以真实设备为中心的"飞轮"范式贯穿三大原则，在 MobileGUI-VBench 达 87.4、AndroidWorld 达 84.9。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动 GUI 智能体正从多模块框架转向端到端原生模型，但工业落地存在三个持续缺口：沙箱训练与生产环境分布不匹配；真实设备上的高成本失败未被充分利用；固定基准饱和后失去指导迭代的能力。

Method: 以真实设备为中心的飞轮，含三原则：Every Sample Matters——双轨流水线，配合异构三系统共识评估与错误纠正及衍生模块，将每条轨迹转化为可用监督；Every Rollout Is Real——三阶段训练（持续预训练、监督微调、在数百台真机上做智能体强化学习），使每次 rollout 扎根生产环境；Every Query Evolves——配额驱动、含三个正交轴的基准方法，支持精确归因并随模型提升系统化升级。

Result: MobileGUI-VBench 得分 87.4，超最佳闭源模型 5.1 分；AndroidWorld 得分 84.9，为开源模型最佳，与闭源模型相当。

Conclusion: 将模型训练与迭代改进同时扎根于真实设备和三大"Every"原则，可获得强健且可迁移的移动 GUI 能力。

Abstract: Mobile GUI agents are shifting from multi-module frameworks to native models trained end-to-end, yet industrial deployment faces three persistent gaps. Sandbox training produces a distribution mismatch with production environments; expensive real-device failures remain underutilized; and fixed benchmarks saturate, losing the power to guide iteration. We present BlueLM-GUI, a 35B-A3B mobile GUI agent built as a real-device-centric flywheel that closes these gaps through three principles. Every Sample Matters: a dual-track pipeline with Heterogeneous Triple-System Consensus evaluation and an Error Correction \& Derivation Module salvages every trajectory into usable supervision. Every Rollout Is Real: a three-stage recipe---continual pre-training, supervised fine-tuning, and agentic reinforcement learning on hundreds of real phones---grounds every rollout in real production environments, so the capability the model learns transfers directly to deployment. Every Query Evolves: a quota-driven benchmark methodology with three orthogonal axes enables precise attribution and allows the benchmark to be systematically upgraded as the model improves. BlueLM-GUI achieves 87.4 on MobileGUI-VBench, surpassing the best closed-source model by 5.1 points, and 84.9 on AndroidWorld, the best result among open-source models and competitive with closed-source models. These results demonstrate that grounding model training and iterative improvement in both real devices and the three Every principles yields strong, robust, and transferable mobile GUI capability.

</details>


### [169] [Is Gaussian Splatting Becoming Neural Again? A Taxonomy and Controlled Study of Learned Parameterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12395)
*YuanHang Wang,Xin Cao,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文系统梳理了3DGS中"神经网络化"趋势，沿五个维度分类19种代表性方法，并通过对照实验表明：选择性神经化（共享外观与不透明度）有益，而神经化解码几何结构无额外收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS结合显式图元与高效光栅化，但近期系统越来越多地用神经网络生成或共享高斯参数。这一趋势缺乏系统刻画，且简单以"是否神经化"的二元标签难以描述其设计差异，因此需要厘清不同神经化选择所针对的具体局限。

Method: 沿五个维度刻画神经参数化趋势：属性解码、空间共享、视角条件解码、拓扑生成、摊销推理。分析19种代表性方法；并在受控的mip-NeRF 360实验中隔离出三种神经参数化形式进行对比。

Result: 共享外观与不透明度提升了重建质量；而用神经网络解码几何结构未带来进一步增益。

Conclusion: 证据支持"选择性神经化"：当共享函数能捕捉可复用相关性、且不牺牲显式splat的局部几何自由度时，神经化才有益；不宜将神经化简化为二元选择。

Abstract: Three-dimensional Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) combines explicit primitives with efficient rasterization, yet recent systems increasingly use neural networks to generate or share Gaussian parameters. We characterize this trend along five axes: attribute decoding, spatial sharing, view-conditioned decoding, topology generation, and amortized inference. An analysis of 19 representative methods shows that these choices address different limitations and cannot be reduced to a binary neural label. We also isolate three forms of neural parameterization in a controlled mip-NeRF 360 study. Sharing appearance and opacity improves reconstruction quality, while decoding geometric structure offers no further gain. The evidence favors selective neuralization: shared functions help when they capture reusable correlations without sacrificing the local geometric freedom of explicit splats.

</details>


### [170] [Niching Agents in The Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12398)
*Gary B. Parker,Jim O'Connor,John Asaro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文扩展了The Core竞争性协同进化算法，通过将智能体生态位化（niching）到原环境的特定子集，验证了生态位智能体在Xpilot中相较全局或跨子环境生态位智能体具有更优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The Core无需传统适应度函数即可进化自主控制，此前已验证其在Xpilot的作战与导航能力；本文旨在探究将智能体生态位化到原环境特定子集时能否提升进化效果。

Method: 采用The Core竞争性协同进化算法，智能体通过局部交互、锦标赛选择、交叉与变异产生后代以进化更优控制器；将智能体生态位化到原测试环境的特定子集，并与其他生态位设置进行对比。

Result: 生态位化到特定子环境的智能体，其成功率优于生态位化到整个系统以及生态位化到不同子环境的智能体。

Conclusion: 把智能体生态位化到原环境的具体子集，有利于提升The Core进化所得控制器的性能，证明分而治之的生态位策略在该协同进化框架中有效。

Abstract: The Core is a unique competitive co-evolution algorithm that allows agents to evolve autonomous control without utilizing a traditional fitness function. The agents evolve via local interactions through tournament selection, crossover, and mutation, producing offspring by evolving better controllers. Previous works have shown The Core's ability to evolve agents capable of combat and navigation in the Xpilot video game. This research expands upon that premise by niching agents to specific subsets of the original environment The Core was tested in. Our results demonstrate the niched agents capacity for success over agents niched to the entire system and agents niched to different sub-environments.

</details>


### [171] [OneLA: Scaling Linear-Attention Decoding to Large Beams in Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12399)
*Xiangrui Yang,Cheng Peng,Yunfeng Zhao,Liang Zeng,Ao Hu,Jiawei Yang,Shengzhe Wang,Jingshan Lv,Xiao Liang,Chen Yang,Jiaqiang Liu,Yiming Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OneLA：面向生成式推荐大束解码的线性注意力解码框架，通过共享提示状态与增量转移记录，避免逐束物化完整循环状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐依赖大束解码生成数百候选，给循环线性注意力带来新扩展挑战。现系统要么为每个束物化完整循环状态，要么重复回放共享历史，导致显著显存与数据搬运开销。

Method: 利用GR工作负载共享提示、仅短后缀发散的特性。用单一共享的提示派生状态加紧凑的仅追加转移记录表示所有束状态；每步只计算所需状态信息，不重建完整循环状态。用轻量祖先索引追踪构成各束历史的转移记录，使束更新无需移动或复制记录。并用融合GPU内核实现跨束共享状态复用。

Result: 端到端解码加速1.54–2.46倍，同时大幅降低循环状态显存占用与数据搬运量。

Conclusion: 该共享状态加增量记录的解码范式有效缓解大束GR下的线性注意力服务瓶颈，兼顾提速与显存节省。

Abstract: Generative recommendation (GR) relies on large-beam decoding to generate hundreds of candidate items, creating a new scaling challenge for recurrent linear attention. Existing linear attention serving systems either materialize a full recurrent state for every beam or repeatedly replay shared history, incurring substantial memory and traffic overhead. To address this, we present OneLA, a linear-attention decoding framework that exploits the shared prompt and short divergent suffixes of GR workloads. Specifically, OneLA represents all beam states using a single shared prompt-derived state and compact, append-only records of their divergent transitions. Using this representation, OneLA computes only the state information required at each decoding step, without reconstructing a full recurrent state for every beam. Furthermore, OneLA uses a lightweight ancestry index to track the transition records that make up each beam's history, allowing beams to be updated without moving or copying existing records. A fused GPU kernel further reuses the shared state across beams. Our analysis shows that OneLA achieves 1.54-2.46x end-to-end decode speedups while substantially reducing recurrent-state memory use and data movement.

</details>


### [172] [Decentralized Evolution of Hexapod Gaits with Independent Leg Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12400)
*Gary B. Parker,John Asaro,Jim O'Connor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种去中心化进化算法，通过独立进化六足机器人每条腿的步态控制器来实现运动，无需集中式协调，从而涌现出高效协同的运动行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 六足机器人步态优化通常依赖集中式协调，复杂度高。作者希望降低优化复杂性，并探索去中心化策略在可扩展、自适应机器人系统中的潜力。

Method: 使用Webots仿真器和Mantis六足机器人，对每条腿的控制器独立进行进化，不使用集中式协调，并允许涌现行为驱动协调；将该去中心化方法与协作协同进化（cooperative coevolution）进行基准对比。

Result: 与协作协同进化相比，去中心化方法在生成稳定且自适应步态方面表现出更高的有效性，并展现出有趣的涌现协调现象。

Conclusion: 独立进化腿部控制器降低了步态优化的复杂度，证明去中心化策略在构建可扩展、自适应机器人系统方面具有潜力。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel approach to hexapod locomotion by evolving each leg's gait independently through a decentralized evolutionary algorithm. Using the Webots simulator and the Mantis hexapod robot, we optimize individual leg controllers without centralized coordination, allowing emergent behaviors to drive the development of efficient, coordinated locomotion. Our decentralized method is benchmarked against cooperative coevolution, demonstrating improved efficacy in generating stable and adaptive gaits while showing interesting emergent coordination. By enabling independent evolution of leg controllers, this method reduces the complexity of gait optimization and highlights the potential of decentralized strategies for scalable and adaptive robotic systems.

</details>


### [173] [Beyond ID Embeddings: Process-Grounded Language Modeling for Cognitive Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12403)
*Minghang Liu,Yuanzhuo Wang,Qiang Qiu,Huawei Shen,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 PLCD 框架，用大语言模型构建概念图式与认知过程图，并以作答记录校准学生后验状态，实现更具语义与冷启动鲁棒性的认知诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统认知诊断模型（CDMs）依赖离散的、基于 ID 的嵌入来表示学生、习题和概念，这与学习者认知的本质不符——知识并非以孤立符号存储和检索。因此，当新习题或新概念出现时，传统 CDMs 面临语义局限性，难以应对冷启动问题。

Method: 提出过程感知的语言认知诊断（PLCD）框架：以语言派生结构作为认知先验，用作答记录校准学生后验状态。具体地，利用大语言模型（LLMs）构建概念图式与认知过程图；采用目标条件语义记忆（target-conditioned semantic memory）检索与每个目标习题相关的历史作答；再通过带有 DA-MoE 专家与过程级对比学习的过程扎根 Language-to-Cognition Mapper，将文本证据映射到统一认知空间。

Result: 实验结果表明，PLCD 不仅在预测学生表现上优于传统基线，还展现出较强的认知迁移能力。

Conclusion: 该工作将 LLMs 的计算能力与心理测量学测量潜在知识状态的目标相连接，表明由作答记录校准的结构化语言先验能够提升冷启动鲁棒性与认知接地（cognitive grounding）。

Abstract: Cognitive Diagnosis Models (CDMs) play a pivotal role in personalized online learning. Traditional CDMs rely on discrete, ID-based embeddings to represent students, exercises, and concepts. This paradigm diverges from the nature of learner cognition, where knowledge is not stored and retrieved as isolated symbols. As a result, CDMs suffer from semantic limitations when new exercises or concepts appear. In this paper, we propose a Process-aware Language Cognitive Diagnosis (PLCD) framework that uses language-derived structures as cognitive priors and response records to calibrate student posterior states. PLCD leverages large language models (LLMs) to construct concept schemas and cognitive process graphs, and uses target-conditioned semantic memory to retrieve historical responses that are relevant to each target exercise. A process-grounded Language-to-Cognition Mapper with DA-MoE experts and process-level contrastive learning then maps the textual evidence into a unified cognitive space. Experimental results show that PLCD not only outperforms traditional baselines in predicting student performance but also exhibits strong cognitive transfer capabilities. These results connect the computational power of LLMs with the psychometric goal of measuring latent knowledge states, suggesting that structured language priors calibrated by response records can improve cold-start robustness and cognitive grounding.

</details>


### [174] [VRL-Bench: Benchmarking agents on computer control tasks under finite trial budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12404)
*Yu Bai,Yukai Miao,Dawei Wang,Li Chen,Yanyu Ren,Yuqian Shi,Dan Li,Ying Xiong,Chengqiu Tan,Run Zhou,Li Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 VRL-Bench 用于在有限试错预算下公平评估语言智能体的试错学习，并发现现有言语记忆方法的增益不稳定；作者进一步提出 VEX$^2$ 调度器以平衡经验利用与探索，在全部六种设置中均取得正向增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有言语强化学习（如 Reflexion）将失败试次转化为文本以指导后续尝试，无需更新模型参数，被视为提升语言智能体在计算机控制等复杂任务上的有效途径。然而，先前评估缺乏统一的有限试错预算设置，导致不同言语记忆方法的增益难以公平比较，且其有效性是否稳定尚不明确。

Method: 作者构建 VRL-Bench，一个在有限试错预算下公平评估试错学习的实验框架。在 MiniWoB 与 WebShop 上对三个模型进行评测，比较 Reflexion 及后续多种言语记忆方法的更新效果，并与无记忆重试基线对照。通过重放实验分析反思带来的影响。进而提出 VEX$^2$，一种言语探索–利用调度器，利用语言模型联合选择策略并分配剩余试错预算。

Result: 各言语记忆方法在部分设置中相较无记忆重试提升了观测成功率，但在另一些设置中反而降低。重放实验表明使用反思可能降低成功率，揭示出利用经验与持续探索之间的权衡。VEX$^2$ 是唯一在全部六种设置中均相较重试取得正向观测成功率增益的更新方法。

Conclusion: 有限预算下的试错学习增益依赖于经验利用与探索的平衡，现有言语记忆方法并不稳健。VEX$^2$ 通过语言模型驱动的策略选择与预算分配调度，能够稳定实现正向增益，为语言智能体的试错学习提供了更可靠的更新机制与评估基准。

Abstract: Learning from trial and error is a promising way to improve language agents on complex tasks such as computer control. Reflexion introduced verbal reinforcement learning, which turns failed trials into text that guides later attempts without updating model parameters. We introduce VRL-Bench, a harness for fair evaluation of trial-and-error learning under finite trial budgets. Across three models on MiniWoB and WebShop, we evaluate updates from several prominent verbal-memory methods spanning Reflexion and later work: each improves observed success over memory-free retry in some settings but reduces it in others. Replay experiments show that using reflection can reduce success rates, revealing a trade-off between exploiting experience and continued exploration. We propose VEX$^2$, a verbal exploration--exploitation scheduler that uses a language model to jointly select policies and allocate the remaining trial budget. VEX$^2$ is the only evaluated update to achieve positive observed success-rate gains over retry in all six settings.

</details>


### [175] [SoK: Rethinking Jailbreaking in the Era of Agentic AI: Attacks, Defenses, and Practical Consideration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12413)
*Md Jueal Mia,Yanzhao Wu,Selcuk Uluagac,M. Hadi Amini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇SoK（知识系统化）论文，围绕完整智能体执行流水线重新梳理越狱安全，构建攻击与防御的统一分类法，提出安全—效用—效率评估框架，并通过受控实证研究揭示现代LLM与智能体时代既有越狱安全结论的三个重要缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正从对话助手演变为具备推理、规划、工具调用、持久记忆、多智能体通信与多步任务执行能力的智能体系统；同时现代模型的原生安全对齐显著强于早期世代，而既有越狱攻防研究多基于旧模型。这引出核心问题：哪些既有越狱安全发现在现代LLM与智能体AI时代仍然成立。

Method: 采用SoK方法，以完整的智能体执行流水线为框架重构越狱安全：一是建立覆盖用户交互、规划与推理、记忆、工具使用、智能体间通信的攻击与防御统一分类法；二是提出安全—效用—效率评估框架，区分原生有害提示安全、对抗性越狱鲁棒性与智能体级安全结果；三是在统一智能体框架内对代表性攻防进行受控实证研究。

Result: 实证研究揭示三个缺口：（1）强原生对齐并不意味着对对抗性越狱的鲁棒性；（2）防御有效性高度依赖模型、攻击方式与组件，且可能带来过度拒答、效用下降与延迟增加等显著代价；（3）最终响应层面的低攻击成功率可能掩盖严重的中间过程失陷——即使最终回复被成功过滤，规划、记忆与工具交互仍可能不安全。

Conclusion: 研究结果促使越狱防御从以响应为中心转向跨层、执行感知的安全范式：在保持实际效用与效率的同时，保护智能体状态、组件间转换以及外部动作。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are rapidly evolving from conversational assistants into agentic AI systems that reason, plan, invoke tools, maintain persistent memory, communicate with other agents, and execute multi-step tasks. At the same time, modern models exhibit substantially stronger native safety alignment than earlier generations on which many jailbreak attacks and defenses were originally studied. This shift raises a fundamental question: \textit{which established jailbreak-security findings remain valid in the era of modern LLMs and agentic AI?} We address this question through a Systematization of Knowledge (SoK) that reframes jailbreak security around the full agentic execution pipeline. We develop unified taxonomies of attacks and defenses spanning user interaction, planning and reasoning, memory, tool use, and inter-agent communication, and introduce a security--utility--efficiency evaluation framework that separates native harmful-prompt safety, adversarial jailbreak robustness, and agent-level security outcomes. We further conduct a controlled empirical study of representative attacks and defenses within a common agentic framework. Our results reveal three important gaps. First, strong native alignment does not imply robustness to adversarial jailbreaks. Second, defense effectiveness is highly model-, attack-, and component-dependent and can come at substantial cost in over-refusal, utility, and latency. Third, low final-response attack success can mask severe intermediate compromise: planning, memory, and tool interactions may remain unsafe even when the final response is successfully filtered. These findings motivate a shift from response-centric jailbreak defense toward cross-layer, execution-aware security that protects agent state, component transitions, and external actions while preserving practical utility and efficiency.

</details>


### [176] [Hierarchical Belief Modeling for Zero-Shot Opponent Adaptation in Partially Observable Multi-Agent Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12422)
*Kowei Shih,Lu Cheng,Zeyu Wang,Yeyun Xu,Kejian Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出分层智能体 HORIZON，用于 Lux AI Season 3 部分可观测、随机化且五局三胜制下的对战，融合对称感知、双记忆信念跟踪、圣物图注意力、信息增益探索与对手条件策略混合，在 JAX 大规模仿真中用 PPO 训练，匹配胜率等指标优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lux AI Season 3 环境具有部分可观测、每局动态随机化以及五局三胜的赛制，要求智能体兼具战术执行与快速适应能力。现有循环或前馈基线难以在跨局元推理与隐藏参数推断上取得优势，因此需要一种显式建模信念、对手风格与探索的分层方案。

Method: HORIZON 采用分层结构：将短时程控制与跨局元推理分离，并结合对称感知的空间编码、双记忆信念跟踪、以圣物为中心的图注意力、信息增益驱动的探索、以及对手条件策略混合。加入辅助的信念预测与世界模型目标以稳定训练，并在大规模 JAX 仿真器中使用 PPO 训练，使智能体显式推断隐藏游戏参数与对手风格。

Result: 实验显示，在匹配胜率、单局胜率、适应增益与联赛评分四项指标上，HORIZON 相对强循环基线与前馈基线均取得稳定提升。

Conclusion: 结果表明，分层设计配合信念跟踪、图注意力与信息增益探索，能有效应对部分可观测与随机化赛制下的快速适应需求，显式隐藏参数与对手风格推断是性能提升的关键，辅助信念与世界模型目标有助于训练稳定。

Abstract: Lux AI Season 3 requires agents to act under partial observability, randomized episode level dynamics, and a best of five match structure that rewards both tactical execution and fast adaptation.
