Embodied-BenchForge constructs six benchmarks covering diverse embodied scenarios in the Offline EQA Track, together with one interactive benchmark containing 220 executable tasks in the Interactive Embodied Track. Evaluations of representative MLLMs and embodied agents show that the benchmarks distinguish model capabilities in both observation-based understanding and closed-loop execution. Quality assessment and ablations validate benchmark quality and the effectiveness of verification and repair, while repair and skill-reuse analyses demonstrate efficient localized recovery and cross-benchmark reusability.

</details>


### [212] [CMA-OT: Hierarchical Expert Supervision for Dance-to-Music Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13118)
*Jinting Wang,Chenxing Li,Dong Yu,Li Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CMA-OT范式，通过课程引导的多尺度表示对齐与尺度感知最优传输，利用外部音乐专家提供分层监督，解决D2M生成的语义鸿沟问题，在两个数据集上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dance-to-music生成需合成与舞蹈视频在节奏和风格上对齐的音乐。核心挑战在于稀疏的舞蹈线索（节奏、风格）与音乐创作所需的密集信息（结构、配器、表现力动态）之间存在语义不匹配。现有方法依赖这些稀疏线索且仅监督最终音频输出，导致音乐表示学习不佳，生成音乐的音乐性与结构连贯性有限。

Method: 提出CMA-OT（Curriculum-guided Multi-scale representation Alignment with scale-aware Optimal Transport）范式：1）引入外部音乐专家为生成器的隐特征提供分层监督，弥合语义鸿沟并增强表示学习；2）设计课程引导的多尺度学习策略，将音乐知识从专家逐步迁移至生成器，实现稳定有效的表示学习；3）提出尺度感知最优传输对齐机制，对分层专家表示与生成器隐特征之间的软对应关系建模，以适应不同专家尺度的语义与结构差异，并在时间错配下实现细粒度对齐。

Result: 在两个数据集上的大量实验表明，CMA-OT在节奏同步、感知质量和整体音乐生成方面均达到最先进（SOTA）性能。

Conclusion: 通过外部音乐专家的分层监督、课程引导的多尺度学习与尺度感知最优传输对齐，CMA-OT有效弥合了稀疏舞蹈线索与密集音乐信息间的语义鸿沟，显著提升了生成音乐的音乐性与结构连贯性。

Abstract: Dance-to-music (D2M) generation aims to synthesize music that is rhythmically and stylistically aligned with dance videos. A key challenge arises from the semantic mismatch between sparse dance cues, such as rhythm and style, and the dense information required for music composition, including structure, instrumentation, and expressive dynamics. Existing methods typically rely on these sparse cues and supervise only the final audio output, resulting in poorly learned music representations and generated music with limited musicality and structural coherence. To address these issues, we propose Curriculum-guided Multi-scale representation Alignment with scale-aware Optimal Transport (CMA-OT), a novel paradigm that leverages an external music expert to provide hierarchical supervision for the generator's latent features, bridging the semantic gap and enhancing representation learning. To effectively incorporate hierarchical supervision, we introduce a curriculum-guided multi-scale learning strategy that progressively transfers musical knowledge from the expert to the music generator, enabling stable and effective representation learning. Moreover, to accommodate the semantic and structural variations across different expert scales and achieve fine-grained alignment under temporal mismatch, we propose a scale-aware optimal transport alignment mechanism, which models soft correspondences between hierarchical expert representations and the generator's latent features. Extensive experiments on two datasets demonstrate that CMA-OT achieves state-of-the-art performance in rhythmic synchronization, perceptual quality, and overall music generation.

</details>


### [213] [A Hybrid LSTM-XGBoost Framework for Multi-Horizon Stock Return Prediction Across Diversified Equity Portfolios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13125)
*Seif ElDein Mostafa,Yahia Ahmed,Farah Datwish,Marwa Solayman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种LSTM与XGBoost结合的混合两阶段架构，用于14只美股的多周期收益预测，30天RMSE为0.0949，并在短周期上以超越朴素基线的方向准确率差距作为更可靠的信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融时间序列具有非平稳、非线性、低信噪比的特点，股票收益预测困难；单一模型（如独立LSTM或仅XGBoost）在精度与稳健性上存在不足，需兼顾时序表征学习与结构化特征的非线性回归能力。

Method: 采用两阶段混合架构：LSTM（两层、64隐藏单元）对5维市场特征的60天滑窗编码为64维时序嵌入；将其与14个手工技术指标拼接成78维混合特征向量，输入经3折交叉验证网格搜索调参的XGBoost回归器。数据为覆盖六个行业板块的14只美股构成的跨股票池化语料，使用严格时序划分与逐股票MinMaxScaling以避免前视偏差，在30/90/252/365个交易日四个预测周期上评估，并构建多周期复合投资评分框架。

Result: 30天周期测试RMSE为0.0949，约为独立LSTM基线的三分之一，且在多数股票上略优于或持平仅XGBoost基线；方向准确率随周期延长而上升，365天达97.6%。作者指出该高准确率主要源于样本中长期收益正基率偏高，故以相对"始终预测为正"朴素预测器的超出基率差距（尤其短周期）作为更有信息量的度量。

Conclusion: 混合LSTM-XGBoost架构在精度上显著优于独立LSTM并可比肩仅XGBoost基线；高周期方向准确率存在基率混淆，需以超出朴素基线的差距衡量真实预测力，短周期方向信号更具参考价值，多周期预测可支撑组合排序与决策支持。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of equity returns remains a major challenge in computational finance due to the non-stationary, nonlinear, and low signal-to-noise ratio nature of financial time series. This paper proposes a hybrid two-stage architecture that combines a long short-term memory (LSTM) network with an XGBoost gradient-boosted regressor for multi-horizon stock return prediction across a diversified panel of 14 U.S. equities spanning six industry sectors. The LSTM component, comprising two stacked layers with 64 hidden units, processes 60-day sliding windows of five sequential market features to produce 64-dimensional temporal embeddings that encode learned sequential market dynamics. These embeddings are concatenated with 14 hand-crafted technical indicators to form a 78-dimensional hybrid feature vector, which is subsequently passed to an XGBoost regressor tuned via 3-fold cross-validation grid search. The framework is trained on a multi-stock pooled corpus using strict chronological splits and per-stock MinMaxScaling to prevent look-ahead bias, and evaluated across four prediction horizons of 30, 90, 252, and 365 trading days. Experimental results demonstrate that the hybrid model achieves a test RMSE of 0.0949 on the 30-day horizon, roughly one-third that of the standalone LSTM baseline, while marginally matching or surpassing the XGBoost-Only baseline across the majority of stocks. Directional accuracy rises with horizon length, reaching 97.6% at 365 days; we show, however, that this largely tracks the high base rate of positive long-horizon returns in the sample, and we therefore benchmark directional accuracy against a naive always-positive predictor and treat the above-base-rate gap at short horizons as the more informative signal. A composite investment scoring framework derived from multi-horizon predictions is further proposed to support portfolio ranking and decision support.

</details>


### [214] [Rethinking Heterogeneous System Disaggregation for Subquadratic Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13134)
*Arya Tschand,Yaosheng Fu,Vikram Sharma Mailthody,Nicolai Oswald,Po-An Tsai,Ritchie Zhao,Oreste Villa,Vijay Janapa Reddi,Karu Sankaralingam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SQD（SubQuadratic Disaggregation），一种按二次/次二次注意力而非算子类型切分解码阶段的异构细粒度分离方案，在 DRAM+SRAM 异构系统上显著提升吞吐与能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型愈发依赖次二次注意力以降低推理显存占用与算力需求，但现有分离式服务系统仍以稠密注意力为中心做调度决策，未针对次二次注意力的算术强度与内存占用特征进行优化，因而在新型异构硬件上难以充分释放性能与能效潜力。

Method: 提出 SQD 细粒度异构分离方案，按二次注意力与次二次注意力切分解码而非按算子类型切分，且可跨多种次二次注意力变体通用。对稀疏注意力模型，将解码拆为需索引完整 KV 的 top-k 选择，以及内存占用静态的 top-k 注意力加 FFN；对线性注意力与滑动窗口注意力模型，将解码拆为稠密注意力层与次二次注意力层加 FFN。

Result: 在调整后的 8xB200 异构系统代理上，相比最强纯 GPU 基线，tokens/J 平均提升：GLM 5.2 为 53%、Nemotron 3 Ultra 为 31%、Gemma 4 31B 为 56%。在固定功耗预算的 Rubin+LPX 系统解析模型中，相比最佳注意力-FFN 分离基线，可实现 1.2x–1.5x 更紧的可达时延与最高 3.6x 的吞吐提升。

Conclusion: 围绕次二次注意力的算术强度与内存足迹进行解码分离，能在新兴 DRAM 与 SRAM-only 异构系统上取得显著吞吐与能效收益；实验还给出面向次二次注意力服务的下一代异构系统在芯片与互连配置方面的架构启示。

Abstract: Frontier language models are more aggressively using subquadratic attention to reduce the memory footprint and compute requirements during inference while still delivering frontier accuracy. While existing systems make dense attention-centric disaggregated serving decisions, we show that disaggregating inference around the unique arithmetic intensity and memory footprint of subquadratic attention LLMs can achieve significant throughput and energy efficiency gains on emerging DRAM-based and SRAM-only heterogeneous systems.
  We introduce SQD (SubQuadratic Disaggregation), a fine-grained heterogeneous disaggregation scheme that splits decode by quadratic and subquadratic attention rather than by operator type, and that applies across subquadratic attention variants. For sparse attention LLMs, we disaggregate decode into top-k selection, which must index through the full KV, and top-k attention plus FFN, which have static memory footprints. For linear and sliding-window attention LLMs, we disaggregate decode into dense attention layers and subquadratic attention layers plus FFN. In an adjusted 8xB200 heterogeneous system proxy, we observe average tokens/J improvements of 53% on GLM 5.2, 31% on Nemotron 3 Ultra, and 56% on Gemma 4 31B over the strongest GPU-only baselines. In an analytical model of a Rubin plus LPX system with fixed power budgets, we observe 1.2x to 1.5x tighter achievable latencies and up to 3.6x higher throughput over the best baseline of attention-FFN disaggregation. Our experiments also reveal architectural insights on chip and interconnect provisioning for next-generation heterogeneous systems serving subquadratic attention.

</details>


### [215] [Evidence-Aligned Local Composition of Discrete Experts for Sequence Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05801)
*Mohammad Panahazari,Usman A. Khan,Shuchin Aeron*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"证据对齐局部组合"方法：在文档损坏且仅有冻结域专家时，利用损坏观测的边际证据推断逐位置软权重，实现无需区域标签或路由器的文档修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实文档常由不同域的文本混合组成（如README在散文、代码、配置间切换）。当此类文档损坏且只有冻结的域专家可用时，修复需要同时判断缺失内容以及在每个位置信任哪个专家，且测试时没有区域标签、也没有训练好的路由器。

Method: 提出证据对齐局部组合（evidence-aligned local composition）：在给定损坏模型下，从损坏观测的边际证据推断对专家的逐位置软权重；证据由专家自身的去噪损失估计，并在位置上进行平滑。由于权重是软的，真实组合为混合时恢复混合，单一专家足够时集中于该专家。

Result: 在分类模拟器、字节级专家以及基于1.3B离散流匹配模型微调的专家上验证：推断权重在自然混合科学文档上达到0.85的字段准确率，在区域词汇不相交的构造混合上达到0.98。当专家真正不同时，修复优于单一全局权重；当专家趋同时退化为全局权重，并与专家分离度指标一致。

Conclusion: 逐位置软权重推断可在无区域标签与无路由器条件下有效实现混合域文档的修复，其收益取决于专家之间的分离程度，为冻结专家组合提供了可行方案。

Abstract: A document modeled as a discrete sequence of tokens can be thought of as being generated from a composition of texts from different domains; a README file, for example, moves between prose, code, and configuration. When such a document is corrupted and only frozen domain experts are available, restoring it requires deciding both what is missing and which expert to trust at each position, at test time and without region labels or a trained router. We introduce evidence-aligned local composition, which infers a soft, position-wise weighting over the experts from the marginal evidence of the corrupted observation under a given corruption model, estimating the evidence from the experts' own denoising losses and smoothing the weights across positions. Because the weighting is soft, it recovers a mixture when the true composition is mixed and concentrates on one expert when that suffices. Across a categorical simulator, byte-level experts, and experts fine-tuned from a $1.3$B discrete flow-matching model, the inferred weights track the true regions at $0.85$ field accuracy on naturally mixed scientific documents, and at $0.98$ on constructed mixtures whose regions are lexically disjoint. Restoration improves over a single global weight when the experts are genuinely distinct and reduces to it when they converge, tracking a measure of expert separation.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [216] [A Deterministic $O^*((3/2)^n)$ Algorithm for the Parity of Directed Hamiltonian Cycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11982)
*Hanqing Li*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文给出有向哈密顿环路数奇偶性的确定性算法，运行时间 O*((3/2)^n)，空间 2^{O(n/log n)}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决有向哈密顿环路计数奇偶性问题，将 Björklund–Husfeldt 的局部度数恒等式转化为可确定性求解的问题，并避免随机化带来的不确定性与去重开销。

Method: 从局部度数恒等式出发，其存活项为 F_2 上的二次系统；将每个坐标编码为三种状态之一，禁止每坐标的一个状态后系统变为仿射线性；用对偶二进制子立方体覆盖三值立方体，得到少量仿射系统。利用 Kuang–Wang 的偶诱导子图构造与条件期望，构造确定性覆盖（每 b 坐标块至多 2(3/2)^b−1 个中心），并用规范所有者规则消除重复枚举；再用第二次条件期望选择人工自环，使生成仿射解总数不超过系统数；每个状态的剩余贡献由一次 F_2 高斯消元求得。

Result: 得到确定性算法，n≥2 时运行时间 O*((3/2)^n)，空间 2^{O(n/log n)}；覆盖规模为每 b 坐标块至多 2(3/2)^b−1 个中心，生成仿射解总数不超过系统数；单顶点退化情形单独处理。

Conclusion: 论文完整证明了编码、所有者规则、两次去随机化及复杂度界，给出有向哈密顿环路奇偶性的确定性判定算法，并单独处理自环即哈密顿环的单点退化情形。

Abstract: We give a deterministic algorithm for the parity of the number of directed Hamiltonian cycles in an $n$-vertex digraph. For $n\geq 2$, the running time is $O^*((3/2)^n)$ and the space usage is $2^{O(n/\log n)}$. The construction starts from the local-degree identity of Björklund and Husfeldt. Their surviving terms are solutions of a quadratic system over $\mathbb{F}_2$. We encode each coordinate of this system by one of three states and observe that forbidding one state per coordinate makes the system affine-linear. A cover of the ternary cube by antipodal binary subcubes then gives a small family of affine systems.
  The cover is made constructive and deterministic. We use the even-induced-subgraph construction of Kuang and Wang, together with conditional expectations, to obtain a cover of a block of $b$ coordinates with at most $2(3/2)^b-1$ centers. A canonical owner for every ternary state removes all duplicate enumeration. A second conditional-expectation argument chooses the artificial self-loops so that the total number of affine solutions generated by all systems is no larger than the number of systems. The remaining contribution of each state is evaluated by one Gaussian elimination over $\mathbb{F}_2$.
  We include complete proofs of the encoding, the owner rule, the two derandomizations, and the complexity bound. The degenerate one-vertex case is handled separately because a self-loop can then itself be a Hamiltonian cycle.

</details>


### [217] [Computing the Shortest Even Directed-Cycle Length in $\widetilde{O}(n^4)$ Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12021)
*Hanqing Li*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文在特征二的域上给出奇数排列单项式和的确定性O(n^3)求值器，并将其应用于随机加权邻接矩阵，从而在Õ(n^4)时间内Monte Carlo地计算有向图最短偶环长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Björklund、Husfeldt与Kaski的算法通过将计算提升到特征四的环上来求最短偶环长度，时间约为Õ(n^{ω+3})。本文希望避免提升特征，直接在特征二内完成奇偶圈覆盖枚举器的求值，从而简化框架并改善复杂度。

Method: 针对特征二域上的非奇异矩阵A，作者利用"两行成比例矩阵"的逆矩阵闭式表达，结合行消元与动态逆维护，构造出直接求奇数排列单项式和的求值器，仅需O(n^3)域运算。对图应用时，取I+zW（W为随机加权邻接矩阵），利用det(I+zW)常数项为1的性质，在2n+1个不同域元素中至少有n+1个使矩阵非奇异；在这些点求值并插值，得到Monte Carlo算法。

Result: 最短偶环长度可在Õ(n^4)时间内计算，错误概率为O(n^{-3})。错误关于存在性是单侧的：无偶环的图总会被正确报告。若结合标准自归约，可进一步在Õ(n^5)时间内构造出具体的偶环。

Conclusion: 该工作给出一套完全停留在特征二内的代数求值方法，绕开了原有框架对特征四提升的依赖，以更简洁的手段获得更快的随机算法，并保持了单侧错误保证。

Abstract: Björklund, Husfeldt, and Kaski gave a randomized $\widetilde{O}(n^{ω+3})$-time algorithm for computing the length of a shortest even cycle in an $n$-vertex directed graph, where $ω$ is the square matrix multiplication exponent. Their algebraic framework evaluates a parity cycle-cover enumerator over a field of characteristic two by lifting the computation to a ring of characteristic four. We give a direct evaluator for nonsingular matrices that stays in characteristic two. If $A$ is nonsingular over a field of characteristic two, the sum of the monomials indexed by odd permutations can be computed deterministically using $O(n^3)$ field operations. The key is an inverse-based closed form for a matrix with two proportional rows, combined with row elimination and dynamic inverse maintenance.
  For a directed graph, we apply the evaluator to $I+zW$, where $W$ is a randomly weighted adjacency matrix. The determinant $\det(I+zW)$ has constant coefficient one, so among any $2n+1$ distinct field elements at least $n+1$ yield nonsingular matrices. Evaluating at these points and interpolating gives a Monte Carlo algorithm that computes the shortest even-cycle length in $\widetilde{O}(n^4)$ time, with error probability $O(n^{-3})$. The error is one-sided with respect to existence: on a graph without an even cycle the algorithm always reports that fact. The result concerns the length; the standard self-reduction produces a cycle in $\widetilde{O}(n^5)$ time.

</details>


### [218] [Accelerating the Local Push Primitive for PageRank Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12076)
*Guanyu Cui,Zhewei Wei,Mingji Yang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 提出一种局部算法，将ACL框架下ε-近似PageRank的计算复杂度从O(1/(αε))提升至Õ(1/(√α·ε))，并解决Fountoulakis-Yang（COLT 2022）提出的ℓ1正则化PageRank开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ACL的原始local push方法计算ε-近似PageRank需O(1/(αε))时间，效率受限；且ℓ1正则化PageRank的快速求解仍是开放问题。作者希望得到更快的原语以加速依赖local push的图算法。

Method: 对Wei和Yang（preprint 2026）主动集方法的精化版本进行势函数分析：在节点的当前活跃集上反复调用SDD求解器并扩展集合，将连续扩展块间的势下降联系起来，从而把扩展次数限制在Õ(1/√α)。

Result: ε-近似PageRank达到Õ(1/(√α·ε))高概率运行时间；ℓ1正则化PageRank达到Õ(1/(√α·ρ))；替换进ACL框架可直接加速PageRank局部图聚类，并通过归约得到更快的有效电阻估计。

Conclusion: 更快的local push原语可广泛改进依赖它的图算法，如局部图聚类与有效电阻估计；核心技术是势函数分析将扩展次数降至Õ(1/√α)。

Abstract: We propose a local algorithm that computes an $\varepsilon$-approximate PageRank vector in the sense of Andersen, Chung, and Lang (ACL; Internet Math. 2007) with teleportation parameter $α$ in $\widetilde{O}\bigl(1 / \bigl(\sqrtα \, \varepsilon\bigr)\bigr)$ time with high probability, improving the $O\bigl(1/(α\varepsilon)\bigr)$ running time of their original local push method. Our method also applies to the $\ell_1$-regularized PageRank problem with a running time of $\widetilde{O}\bigl(1 / \bigl(\sqrtα \, ρ\bigr)\bigr)$ for regularization parameter $ρ$, giving a positive answer to the open problem posed by Fountoulakis and Yang (COLT 2022).
  Our faster primitive has the potential to improve a broad range of graph algorithms that rely on local push. For example, substituting our primitive into the ACL framework directly yields faster PageRank-based local graph clustering, and we also develop reductions that lead to faster algorithms for effective resistance estimation.
  Our main technical contribution is a potential-function analysis of a refinement of the active-set method of Wei and Yang (preprint 2026), which repeatedly invokes an SDD solver on the current active set of nodes and expands the set. We relate the potential decreases over consecutive blocks of expansions to show that the number of expansions is bounded by $\widetilde{O}\bigl(1 / \sqrtα\bigr)$.

</details>


### [219] [A Non-constant Lower Bound for Grammar-Based Compression with Greedy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12106)
*Danny Hucke*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文证明了全局文法压缩算法 Greedy 的近似比下界为 Ω(log n / log log n)，此前的下界仅为常数，非恒定下界的存在性悬而未决二十余年。该下界在长度 n 的无限单词族上成立，且经 Lean 4 形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Greedy 是全局文法压缩的经典算法，但其近似比是否存在非恒定下界长期未解，此前最好下界仅为常数，该问题悬置超过二十年。

Method: 通过对长度 n 的无限单词族构造并展开组合分析，针对采用从左到右出现替换（left-to-right occurrence replacement）且任意破平规则的每一次执行，证明其近似比下界；同时用 Lean 4 对下界进行形式化验证。

Result: 证明了 Greedy 的近似比为 Ω(log n / log log n)，突破了此前常数下界的限制，并在增长字母表上对无限单词族成立。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了悬置二十余年的公开问题，给出了 Greedy 算法首个非恒定近似比下界，且结论经 Lean 4 机器验证，具有高可信度。

Abstract: We prove a lower bound of Ω(log n/ log log n) on the approximation ratio of the global grammar-based compression algorithm Greedy. To our knowledge, the previously best lower bound was a constant, and the existence of a nonconstant lower bound had remained open for more than twenty years. Our bound holds on an infinite family of words of length n, over alphabets of growing size, for every execution using left-to-right occurrence replacement and arbitrary tie-breaking. The lower bound is also formally verified in Lean 4.

</details>


### [220] [A Tight $\widetilde Ω(\sqrt{m})$ Information-Theoretic Lower Bound for Randomized Online Set Cover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12183)
*Ilan Doron-Arad,Joseph,Naor*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文证明了信息论意义下（计算能力无限制）随机化无权在线集合覆盖问题的竞争比下界为 Ω(√m)，排除了 O(log m · log^{1/2-ε} n)-竞争算法；并给出随机顺序模型下的 Ω(m^{1/3}) 下界，以及多项式内存算法的 Ω(m/log m) 下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线集合覆盖存在确定性 O(log m log n)-竞争算法，且该结果对确定性算法及假设 NP⊄BPP 的多项式时间随机化算法本质上是紧的。但对信息论（计算无限制）随机化算法针对 oblivious 对手，已知下界仅为 Ω(log m)，而上界为 O(√m log m)=Õ(√m)，两者之间存在显著间隙，本文旨在缩小这一间隙。

Method: 通过构造性对抗论证，针对信息论意义下计算能力无限制的随机化算法设计新的下界证明技术；并将该技术扩展到随机顺序模型和有限内存（poly(m) 内存）算法的分析中。

Result: 证明了无权在线集合覆盖在信息论随机化模型下的 Ω(√m) 下界，排除了对任意常数 ε>0 的 O(log m · log^{1/2-ε} n)-竞争算法；在随机顺序模型下证明了 Ω(m^{1/3}) 下界；并证明任何具有 poly(m) 内存的算法（即使请求间计算无限制）竞争比为 Ω(m/log m)。

Conclusion: 即使拥有无限计算能力，随机化也无法在无权在线集合覆盖中达到 O(log m)-竞争比，从根本上限制了对数级竞争算法的可能性；同时揭示了内存限制和随机顺序假设下竞争比的进一步下界。

Abstract: Online set cover is a fundamental problem in online algorithms, admitting a deterministic $O(\log m\log n)$-competitive algorithm, where $m$ is the number of sets and $n$ is the number of elements. This is essentially tight for deterministic algorithms as well as for polynomial-time randomized algorithms assuming $\mathrm{NP}\not\subseteq\mathrm{BPP}$. However, the best lower bound known for information-theoretic (computationally unlimited) randomized algorithms against an oblivious adversary is only $Ω(\log m)$, whereas the upper bound in terms of $m$ is $O(\sqrt m\log m)=\widetilde O(\sqrt m)$.
  We prove an $Ω(\sqrt m)$ lower bound for information-theoretic randomized unweighted online set cover, showing that even with unbounded computational power, randomization cannot achieve an $O(\log m)$-competitive ratio. Specifically, our lower bound rules out $O(\log m\cdot\log^{1/2-\varepsilon} n)$-competitive algorithms for every constant $\varepsilon>0$. Our techniques also prove an $Ω(m^{1/3})$ lower bound in the random-order model, and show that every algorithm with $poly(m)$ memory has competitive ratio $Ω(m/\log m)$, even with unlimited computation between requests.

</details>


### [221] [Exponential Lower Bounds for Integer-Weighted Shortest-Paths Preservers of DAGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12211)
*Michael Yi Wang,Nicole Wein*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文否定了Bernstein等人提出的"DAG是否总能用多项式大小的整数权重进行保持最短路的重加权"这一开放问题，证明存在需 $2^{Ω(n)}$ 权重的DAG，并将下界推广至近似版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bernstein、Bodwin和Wein [ITCS'24] 研究图的简化问题：在保持最短路径结构（最短路上的顶点与边序列）的前提下对边重新赋权以缩小宽高比（最大与最小权重之比）。他们证明一般有向图与无向图均需指数级宽高比，而每个DAG可用线性宽高比重加权，但其权重不是整数，由此提出"DAG是否均存在多项式上界整数权重重加权"的开放问题。本文旨在回答该问题。

Method: 针对该开放问题构造反例：设计具有3层顶点、中间层仅含3个顶点的简单结构DAG，通过组合/计数论证证明任何保持最短路的整数重加权均需要 $2^{Ω(n)}$ 量级的权重。随后将构造与下界推广到近似设定，即要求原图中某条 $\alpha$-近似最短路在重加权图中为精确最短路。同时分析中间层顶点数降为2的情形以给出对比上界。

Result: 证明存在DAG，其任何保持最短路的整数重加权都需权重规模达 $2^{Ω(n)}$，且该结论即使在极简结构（3层顶点、中间层仅3个顶点）下仍成立；作为对比，当中间层顶点数减至2时存在线性上界。此外，指数下界可推广至近似版本：即使近似比 $\alpha>1$ 为任意有限值，指数下界依然成立。

Conclusion: 本文否定地回答了Bernstein等人关于DAG整数重加权多项式上界的开放问题，揭示了整数约束与宽高比之间的本质困难，并给出精确的结构阈值（中间层3个顶点导致指数下界，2个顶点则为线性），同时下界在近似最短路保持设定下依然稳健。

Abstract: We study a graph simplification problem introduced by Bernstein, Bodwin, and Wein [ITCS'24]. We start with a graph with arbitrarily large positive edge weights and the goal is to reweight the edges to small aspect ratio (ratio between largest and smallest weight) while preserving the shortest paths structure (the sequence of vertices and edges along shortest paths).
  They studied whether polynomial aspect ratio is always possible. They proved that for general graphs, both directed and undirected, it is not: there exist graphs for which any shortest-paths preserving reweighting requires exponential aspect ratio. In contrast, they showed that every DAG (directed acyclic graph) admits a reweighting with linear aspect ratio. However, the resulting edge weights are not integers. This motivated them to pose the open question of whether all DAGs admit a reweighting with polynomially-bounded integer edge weights.
  Our main result is to answer this question in the negative: we prove that there exist DAGs for which any shortest-paths preserving integer reweighting requires weights of size $2^{Ω(n)}$. In fact, this is even true when the DAG has very simple structure: 3 layers of vertices with only 3 vertices in the middle layer. In contrast, we show that if the number of vertices in the middle layer is decreased to 2, then a linear upper bound is possible.
  We extend our exponential lower bound to the approximate version of the problem where only a single $α$-approximate shortest path in the original graph must be preserved as an exact shortest path in the reweighted graph. Our exponential lower bound holds even for any finite approximation ratio $α>1$.

</details>


### [222] [Spatial Mixing and Deterministic Approximate Counting of Multi-spin Systems beyond Bounded Degree Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12352)
*Zhidan Li,Kuan Yang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出一种超越有界度图的确定性近似计数的通用框架，基于有理多面体递归与线性分式规划获得边缘比率的可认证界，并在正相互作用多自旋系统及稀疏Erdős-Rényi随机图的正常着色计数上取得改进的FPTAS结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多自旋系统的近似计数此前多局限于有界度图，需将被计数结构从有界度推广到连通常数受控的图与稀疏随机图，并改进现有计数保证中的主常数。

Method: 递归构造包含真实边缘向量的有理多面体，用线性分式规划得到边缘比率的可认证上下界；对多项式连通常数D的图上的正相互作用建立强空间混合，条件为Dc<1（c界住相互作用的Birkhoff收缩系数）；进一步用宽松块递归推广到稀疏Erdős-Rényi随机图的正常着色。

Result: 对多项式连通常数D的正相互作用图，在Dc<1时得到强空间混合与FPTAS；对每个固定η∈(0,1)、充分大的固定d及固定整数q≥(2+η)d，以高概率对G~G(n,d/n)的正常q-着色计数给出FPTAS，将Yin与Zhang（APPROX/RANDOM 2016）保证中的主常数3改进为2，并渐近匹配Yin（ICALP 2014）的空间混合区域。

Conclusion: 该框架将确定性近似计数统一推广到无界度与稀疏随机图场景，改进着色计数的主常数并获得与已有空间混合结果渐近一致的FPTAS，为多自旋系统计数提供可认证的通用方法。

Abstract: We develop a framework for deterministic approximate counting of multi-spin systems beyond bounded-degree graphs. The algorithm recursively constructs rational polytopes containing the true marginal vectors and uses linear-fractional programming to obtain certified bounds on marginal ratios. For positive interactions on graphs of polynomial connective constant $D$, we establish strong spatial mixing and a fully polynomial-time approximation scheme (\textbf{FPTAS}) whenever $Dc<1$, where $c$ bounds the Birkhoff contraction coefficients of the interactions.
  We further extend the framework to proper colorings of sparse Erdős-Rényi random graphs using recursion on permissive blocks. For every fixed $η\in(0,1)$, sufficiently large fixed $d$, and fixed integer $q\ge(2+η)d$, we obtain an \textbf{FPTAS} for counting proper $q$-colorings of $G\sim\mathcal G(n,d/n)$ with high probability over $G$. This improves the leading constant $3$ in the earlier counting guarantee of Yin and Zhang (APPROX/RANDOM, 2016) to $2$, and asymptotically matches the spatial mixing regime established by Yin (ICALP, 2014).

</details>


### [223] [Differential Privacy Meets Fixed Parameter Tractability: Algorithms and Lower Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12508)
*Pritish Kamath,Ravi Kumar,Pasin Manurangsi*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文将隐式表示框架中编码器放宽为固定参数可处理(FPT)时间，在ε-差分隐私下为多个组合优化问题取得更优保证，并首次给出与表示无关的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ε-DP下显式输出解的强下界限制了算法表现，需绕开多项式时间算法的近似障碍，同时填补该框架缺乏表示无关下界的空白。

Method: 在Gupta等(SODA 2010)的隐式表示框架(私有编码器生成表示、解码器结合输入输出可行解)基础上，允许编码器运行固定参数可处理(FPT)时间；并基于非均匀Gap-ETH建立下界。

Result: 对多个基础组合优化问题获得改进的近似保证；在充分小ε下，若解码器亚指数时间，则不存在ε-DP编码-解码对达到特定近似保证；并提供对更大ε仍成立的表示相关下界。

Conclusion: 扩展隐式表示为FPT编码器可规避多项式时间近似障碍，同时给出首个表示无关下界，刻画了ε-DP组合优化的内在极限。

Abstract: We study combinatorial optimization problems under the constraint of $ε$-differential privacy ($ε$-DP). Given the strong lower bounds for explicitly outputting solutions, we work within the implicit representation framework of Gupta et al. (SODA 2010), where a private polynomial-time randomized "encoder" generates a representation of a solution, and a "decoder" uses this representation along with the input to extract a valid final solution.
  In this work, we generalize this framework by allowing the encoder to run in fixed-parameter tractable time. This circumvents approximation barriers inherent to polynomial-time algorithms and obtains improved guarantees for many fundamental combinatorial optimization problems.
  Finally, we establish the first representation-independent lower bounds for our framework. Assuming a non-uniform variant of the Gap Exponential Time Hypothesis, for sufficiently small $ε> 0$, we prove that no $ε$-DP encoder-decoder pair can achieve certain approximation guarantees, if the decoder runs in subexponential time. We further provide representation-dependent lower bounds that hold even for larger $ε$.

</details>


### [224] [Rank-1-perturbed trickledown theorems: Mixing time of Glauber dynamics for the Sherrington-Kirkpatrick model up to $β\leq \frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13138)
*Mathews Boban,Anqi Li,Shayan Oveis Gharan*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文引入一类新的"涓滴定理"（局部到全局技术），通过对影响矩阵进行秩-1平移来界定多状态自旋系统Glauber动力学的谱间隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多状态自旋系统中，用传统方法将余维为2的链接的影响矩阵上界为λI时，界往往过大，难以得到紧致的谱间隙估计，因此需要更精细的局部到全局分析工具。

Method: 该技术不再直接把余维为2的链接的影响矩阵上界为λI，而是先施加一个精心选取的秩-1平移后再求上界；秩-1平移虽能显著降低上界，但代价是需要控制秩-1扰动带来的平均损失，作者通过对所有余维为2的链接求平均来界定该损失。

Result: 作为应用，证明了自然Glauber动力学在β ≤ 1/2 + ε（ε为绝对常数）时能以多项式时间混合，从而从Sherrington-Kirkpatrick模型中采样。

Conclusion: 通过秩-1平移与对所有余维2链接的平均损失控制，成功建立了新的涓滴定理框架，并将其应用于Sherrington-Kirkpatrick模型在β ≤ 1/2 + ε下的多项式时间采样。

Abstract: We introduce a new family of trickledown theorems, a.k.a., local to global technique to bound the spectral gap of the Glauber dynamics for multi-state spin systems. In this technique instead of upper-bounding the influence matrix of a link of co-dimension 2 by $λI$ (where $λ$ is the second eigenvalue of the link), we upper-bound the influence matrix after a carefully chosen rank-1 shift. The rank-1 shift allows for a significantly smaller upper-bound but it comes at the cost of bounding the average loss due to rank-1 perturbations.
  As an application we use this method to show that the natural Glauber dynamics mixes in polynomial time to generate samples from the Sherrington-Kirkpatrick model for $β\leq \tfrac{1}{2}+\varepsilon$, for an absolute constant $\varepsilon>0$. At the heart of the proof we manage to bound the loss due to rank-1 perturbations by averaging over all links of co-dimension 2.

</details>
